// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// CATATAN LINK MASUK (opsional, tempel di Sketchware > Add Source Directly pada MainActivity)
//   @Override protected void onNewIntent(android.content.Intent i) { super.onNewIntent(i); setIntent(i); }
// Lalu tambahkan intent-filter di manifest (lihat penjelasan): aether://play, music.aether.app/play,
// youtu.be, open.spotify.com dan SEND text/plain agar menu "Bagikan" dari Spotify memilih AETHER.
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ============================================================================
// AETHER MUSIC v4.3 - FULL CODE
// Tempel seluruh kode ini di dalam onCreate / initializeLogic Activity (Sketchware)
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================
// 1. PERIZINAN RUNTIME OTOMATIS, WAKE LOCK & PENGABAIAN OPTIMASI BATERAI
// ============================================================================
if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23) {
    java.util.List<String> listPermissions = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
        if (checkSelfPermission("android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS") != android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            listPermissions.add("android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS");
        }
        if (checkSelfPermission("android.permission.READ_MEDIA_AUDIO") != android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            listPermissions.add("android.permission.READ_MEDIA_AUDIO");
        }
    } else {
        if (checkSelfPermission(android.Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE) != android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            listPermissions.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE);
        }
        if (checkSelfPermission(android.Manifest.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE) != android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            listPermissions.add(android.Manifest.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE);
        }
    }
    if (!listPermissions.isEmpty()) {
        requestPermissions(listPermissions.toArray(new String[0]), 1001);
    }
    try {
        android.os.PowerManager pm = (android.os.PowerManager) getSystemService(android.content.Context.POWER_SERVICE);
        if (pm != null && !pm.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(getPackageName())) {
            android.content.Intent battIntent = new android.content.Intent(android.provider.Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS);
            battIntent.setData(android.net.Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName()));
            startActivity(battIntent);
        }
    } catch (Exception ignored) {}
}

try {
    android.os.PowerManager pm = (android.os.PowerManager) getSystemService(android.content.Context.POWER_SERVICE);
    if (pm != null) {
        android.os.PowerManager.WakeLock wl = pm.newWakeLock(android.os.PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK, "AetherMusic::LightWakeLock");
        wl.setReferenceCounted(false);
        wl.acquire();
    }
    android.net.wifi.WifiManager wm = (android.net.wifi.WifiManager) getApplicationContext().getSystemService(android.content.Context.WIFI_SERVICE);
    if (wm != null) {
        android.net.wifi.WifiManager.WifiLock wifiLock = wm.createWifiLock(android.net.wifi.WifiManager.WIFI_MODE_FULL_HIGH_PERF, "AetherMusic::LightWifiLock");
        wifiLock.setReferenceCounted(false);
        wifiLock.acquire();
    }
} catch (Exception ignored) {}

setVolumeControlStream(android.media.AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC);

// ============================================================================
// 1B. MODE LANDSCAPE / SMART TV + PENYIMPANAN PERMANEN (SharedPreferences native)
//     Pengaturan landscape & persetujuan aturan TIDAK ikut terhapus saat
//     "Bersihkan Cache Total" ditekan, jadi tidak pernah ter-reset.
// ============================================================================
final android.content.SharedPreferences aetherPrefs = getSharedPreferences("aether_native_prefs", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
boolean detectedTv = false;
try {
    android.app.UiModeManager uiModeMgr = (android.app.UiModeManager) getSystemService(android.content.Context.UI_MODE_SERVICE);
    if (uiModeMgr != null && uiModeMgr.getCurrentModeType() == android.content.res.Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_TELEVISION) detectedTv = true;
    if (getPackageManager().hasSystemFeature("android.software.leanback") || getPackageManager().hasSystemFeature("android.hardware.type.television")) detectedTv = true;
} catch (Exception ignored) {}
final boolean aetherIsTv = detectedTv;

// Di perangkat Android TV, mode landscape otomatis aktif saat pertama kali dibuka
if (aetherIsTv && !aetherPrefs.contains("landscape_mode")) {
    aetherPrefs.edit().putBoolean("landscape_mode", true).apply();
}
try {
    if (aetherPrefs.getBoolean("landscape_mode", false)) {
        setRequestedOrientation(android.content.pm.ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE);
    } else if (!aetherIsTv) {
        setRequestedOrientation(android.content.pm.ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_PORTRAIT);
    }
} catch (Exception ignored) {}

// Privasi: blokir screenshot & rekam layar (FLAG_SECURE) bila diaktifkan dari Pengaturan > Privasi
try {
    if (aetherPrefs.getBoolean("priv_secure", false)) getWindow().addFlags(android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE);
} catch (Exception ignored) {}

// ============================================================================
// 2. PROTEKSI ANTI-SWIPE RECENT APPS & HOT-SWAP WEBVIEW ALWAYS-VISIBLE
// ============================================================================
final Runnable protectTaskFromSwipe = new Runnable() {
    @Override
    public void run() {
        if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 21) {
            try {
                android.app.ActivityManager am = (android.app.ActivityManager) getSystemService(android.content.Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE);
                if (am != null && am.getAppTasks() != null) {
                    for (android.app.ActivityManager.AppTask task : am.getAppTasks()) { task.setExcludeFromRecents(true); }
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
    }
};

try {
    android.view.ViewGroup parentGroup = (android.view.ViewGroup) webview1.getParent();
    if (parentGroup != null) {
        int wvIndex = parentGroup.indexOfChild(webview1);
        android.view.ViewGroup.LayoutParams wvParams = webview1.getLayoutParams();
        int wvId = webview1.getId();

        android.webkit.WebView bgWebView = new android.webkit.WebView(getApplicationContext()) {
            @Override protected void onWindowVisibilityChanged(int visibility) { super.onWindowVisibilityChanged(android.view.View.VISIBLE); }
            @Override public void onWindowFocusChanged(boolean hasWindowFocus) { super.onWindowFocusChanged(true); }
            @Override protected void onVisibilityChanged(android.view.View changedView, int visibility) { super.onVisibilityChanged(changedView, android.view.View.VISIBLE); }
            @Override public boolean isShown() { return true; }
            @Override public int getWindowVisibility() { return android.view.View.VISIBLE; }
            @Override public void onPause() {}
            @Override public void pauseTimers() {}
            @Override public void destroy() {}
        };

        bgWebView.setId(wvId); bgWebView.setLayoutParams(wvParams);
        parentGroup.removeViewAt(wvIndex); parentGroup.addView(bgWebView, wvIndex);
        webview1 = bgWebView;
    }
} catch (Exception ignored) {}

try {
    final android.view.Window.Callback origCallback = getWindow().getCallback();
    getWindow().setCallback(new android.view.Window.Callback() {
        @Override
        public boolean dispatchKeyEvent(android.view.KeyEvent event) {
            // ---- TOMBOL MEDIA REMOTE / HEADSET (Smart TV) ----
            if (event.getAction() == android.view.KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN && webview1 != null) {
                int kc = event.getKeyCode();
                if (kc == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PLAY_PAUSE || kc == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_HEADSETHOOK) {
                    webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof togglePlayFromNotif==='function') togglePlayFromNotif();", null);
                    return true;
                }
                if (kc == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PLAY) {
                    webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof isP!=='undefined' && !isP && typeof togglePlayFromNotif==='function') togglePlayFromNotif();", null);
                    return true;
                }
                if (kc == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PAUSE) {
                    webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof isP!=='undefined' && isP && typeof togglePlayFromNotif==='function') togglePlayFromNotif();", null);
                    return true;
                }
                if (kc == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_NEXT) {
                    webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof nextTrack==='function'){ window.userPaused=false; nextTrack(); }", null);
                    return true;
                }
                if (kc == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PREVIOUS) {
                    webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof prevTrack==='function'){ window.userPaused=false; prevTrack(); }", null);
                    return true;
                }
            }
            if (event.getKeyCode() == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BACK && event.getAction() == android.view.KeyEvent.ACTION_UP) {
                if(webview1 != null) {
                    webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.handleBackButton==='function'){ window.handleBackButton(); } else { 'EXIT'; }", new android.webkit.ValueCallback<String>() {
                        @Override public void onReceiveValue(String value) {
                            if(value != null && value.contains("EXIT")) {
                                protectTaskFromSwipe.run(); moveTaskToBack(true);
                            }
                        }
                    });
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return origCallback.dispatchKeyEvent(event);
        }
        @Override public boolean dispatchKeyShortcutEvent(android.view.KeyEvent e) { return origCallback.dispatchKeyShortcutEvent(e); }
        @Override public boolean dispatchTouchEvent(android.view.MotionEvent e) { return origCallback.dispatchTouchEvent(e); }
        @Override public boolean dispatchTrackballEvent(android.view.MotionEvent e) { return origCallback.dispatchTrackballEvent(e); }
        @Override public boolean dispatchGenericMotionEvent(android.view.MotionEvent e) { return origCallback.dispatchGenericMotionEvent(e); }
        @Override public boolean dispatchPopulateAccessibilityEvent(android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityEvent e) { return origCallback.dispatchPopulateAccessibilityEvent(e); }
        @Override public android.view.View onCreatePanelView(int i) { return origCallback.onCreatePanelView(i); }
        @Override public boolean onCreatePanelMenu(int i, android.view.Menu m) { return origCallback.onCreatePanelMenu(i, m); }
        @Override public boolean onPreparePanel(int i, android.view.View v, android.view.Menu m) { return origCallback.onPreparePanel(i, v, m); }
        @Override public boolean onMenuOpened(int i, android.view.Menu m) { return origCallback.onMenuOpened(i, m); }
        @Override public boolean onMenuItemSelected(int i, android.view.MenuItem item) { return origCallback.onMenuItemSelected(i, item); }
        @Override public void onWindowAttributesChanged(android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams a) { origCallback.onWindowAttributesChanged(a); }
        @Override public void onContentChanged() { origCallback.onContentChanged(); }
        @Override public void onWindowFocusChanged(boolean b) { origCallback.onWindowFocusChanged(true); }
        @Override public void onAttachedToWindow() { origCallback.onAttachedToWindow(); }
        @Override public void onDetachedFromWindow() { origCallback.onDetachedFromWindow(); }
        @Override public void onPanelClosed(int i, android.view.Menu m) { origCallback.onPanelClosed(i, m); }
        @Override public boolean onSearchRequested() { return origCallback.onSearchRequested(); }
        @Override public boolean onSearchRequested(android.view.SearchEvent e) { return origCallback.onSearchRequested(e); }
        @Override public android.view.ActionMode onWindowStartingActionMode(android.view.ActionMode.Callback c) { return origCallback.onWindowStartingActionMode(c); }
        @Override public android.view.ActionMode onWindowStartingActionMode(android.view.ActionMode.Callback c, int i) { return origCallback.onWindowStartingActionMode(c, i); }
        @Override public void onActionModeStarted(android.view.ActionMode m) { origCallback.onActionModeStarted(m); }
        @Override public void onActionModeFinished(android.view.ActionMode m) { origCallback.onActionModeFinished(m); }
    });
} catch (Exception ignored) {}

getApplication().registerActivityLifecycleCallbacks(new android.app.Application.ActivityLifecycleCallbacks() {
    @Override public void onActivityPaused(android.app.Activity activity) { if (activity == MainActivity.this && webview1 != null) { protectTaskFromSwipe.run(); webview1.onResume(); webview1.resumeTimers(); } }
    @Override public void onActivityStopped(android.app.Activity activity) { if (activity == MainActivity.this && webview1 != null) { protectTaskFromSwipe.run(); webview1.onResume(); webview1.resumeTimers(); } }
    @Override public void onActivityCreated(android.app.Activity a, android.os.Bundle b) {}
    @Override public void onActivityStarted(android.app.Activity a) {}
    @Override public void onActivityResumed(android.app.Activity a) { if (a == MainActivity.this && webview1 != null) { webview1.onResume(); webview1.resumeTimers(); } }
    @Override public void onActivitySaveInstanceState(android.app.Activity a, android.os.Bundle b) {}
    @Override public void onActivityDestroyed(android.app.Activity a) {}
});

final android.os.Handler bgWatchdogHandler = new android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper());
bgWatchdogHandler.postDelayed(new Runnable() {
    @Override public void run() {
        try { if (webview1 != null) { webview1.onResume(); webview1.resumeTimers(); webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.keepAliveCheck==='function') window.keepAliveCheck();", null); } } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        bgWatchdogHandler.postDelayed(this, 1000);
    }
}, 1000);

// ============================================================================
// 3. MEDIA SESSION & NOTIFIKASI
// ============================================================================
final android.media.session.MediaSession aetherMediaSession = (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 21) ? new android.media.session.MediaSession(getApplicationContext(), "AetherSession") : null;

if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 21 && aetherMediaSession != null) {
    try {
        aetherMediaSession.setCallback(new android.media.session.MediaSession.Callback() {
            @Override public void onPlay() { webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof togglePlayFromNotif==='function') togglePlayFromNotif();", null); }
            @Override public void onPause() { webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof togglePlayFromNotif==='function') togglePlayFromNotif();", null); }
            @Override public void onSkipToNext() { webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof nextTrack==='function'){ window.userPaused=false; nextTrack(); }", null); }
            @Override public void onSkipToPrevious() { webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof prevTrack==='function'){ window.userPaused=false; prevTrack(); }", null); }
        });
        aetherMediaSession.setActive(true);
    } catch (Exception ignored) {}
}

android.content.BroadcastReceiver mediaControlReceiver = new android.content.BroadcastReceiver() {
    @Override public void onReceive(android.content.Context context, android.content.Intent intent) {
        if (intent == null || intent.getAction() == null) return;
        String action = intent.getAction();
        if (webview1 != null) { webview1.onResume(); webview1.resumeTimers(); }
        if ("com.aether.ACTION_PREV".equals(action)) { webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof prevTrack==='function'){ window.userPaused=false; prevTrack(); }", null); } 
        else if ("com.aether.ACTION_TOGGLE".equals(action)) { webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof togglePlayFromNotif==='function'){ togglePlayFromNotif(); }", null); } 
        else if ("com.aether.ACTION_NEXT".equals(action)) { webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof nextTrack==='function'){ window.userPaused=false; nextTrack(); }", null); }
    }
};
android.content.IntentFilter mediaFilter = new android.content.IntentFilter(); mediaFilter.addAction("com.aether.ACTION_PREV"); mediaFilter.addAction("com.aether.ACTION_TOGGLE"); mediaFilter.addAction("com.aether.ACTION_NEXT");
if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) { registerReceiver(mediaControlReceiver, mediaFilter, android.content.Context.RECEIVER_EXPORTED); } else { registerReceiver(mediaControlReceiver, mediaFilter); }

// ============================================================================
// 3A. PENGUAT VOLUME (LoudnessEnhancer) - bisa diatur 100% s/d 300%
// ============================================================================
final android.media.audiofx.LoudnessEnhancer[] aetherEnh = new android.media.audiofx.LoudnessEnhancer[1];
class AetherBoost {
    String apply(int pct) {
        try {
            if (aetherEnh[0] != null) {
                try { aetherEnh[0].setEnabled(false); aetherEnh[0].release(); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                aetherEnh[0] = null;
            }
            if (pct <= 100) return "OK";
            int mb = (int) Math.round(2000.0 * Math.log10(pct / 100.0));
            android.media.audiofx.LoudnessEnhancer le = new android.media.audiofx.LoudnessEnhancer(0);
            le.setTargetGain(mb);
            le.setEnabled(true);
            aetherEnh[0] = le;
            return "OK";
        } catch (Exception e) {
            aetherEnh[0] = null;
            return "FAIL";
        }
    }
}
final AetherBoost aetherBoost = new AetherBoost();
aetherBoost.apply(aetherPrefs.getInt("vol_boost", 100));

// ============================================================================
// 3A2. LINK MASUK: klik link musik / bagikan dari Spotify langsung membuka AETHER
//      (aether://play?id=..., music.aether.app/play?id=..., youtu.be, open.spotify.com, atau menu Bagikan)
// ============================================================================
final String[] aetherPendingLink = new String[]{ "" };
final Runnable aetherCaptureIntent = new Runnable() {
    @Override public void run() {
        try {
            android.content.Intent it = getIntent();
            if (it == null || it.getBooleanExtra("aether_link_done", false)) return;
            String d = it.getDataString();
            if ((d == null || d.length() == 0) && android.content.Intent.ACTION_SEND.equals(it.getAction())) {
                d = it.getStringExtra(android.content.Intent.EXTRA_TEXT);
            }
            if (d == null || d.trim().length() == 0) return;
            it.putExtra("aether_link_done", true);
            aetherPendingLink[0] = d.trim();
            if (webview1 != null) webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.checkIncomingLink==='function') window.checkIncomingLink();", null);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }
};
aetherCaptureIntent.run();

// ============================================================================
// 3C. ANIMASI FLOATING PERANGKAT BLUETOOTH (tidak permanen, tetap muncul walau aplikasi ditutup)
//     Memakai overlay sistem (izin Tampil di atas aplikasi lain) + AudioDeviceCallback.
// ============================================================================
class AetherBtPop extends android.view.View {
    final android.content.Context ctx;
    final android.view.WindowManager wm;
    final float d;
    android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams lp = null;
    boolean attached = false, looping = false, conn = true;
    String name = "";
    long t0 = 0L;
    int baseY = 0;
    final android.graphics.Paint bg = new android.graphics.Paint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.graphics.Paint st = new android.graphics.Paint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.graphics.Paint fl = new android.graphics.Paint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.text.TextPaint tp = new android.text.TextPaint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.text.TextPaint sp = new android.text.TextPaint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.graphics.RectF r = new android.graphics.RectF();
    final android.graphics.Path path = new android.graphics.Path();
    final android.graphics.Path seg = new android.graphics.Path();

    AetherBtPop(android.content.Context c) {
        super(c);
        ctx = c;
        wm = (android.view.WindowManager) c.getSystemService(android.content.Context.WINDOW_SERVICE);
        d = c.getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;
        bg.setColor(0xF2161618);
        st.setStyle(android.graphics.Paint.Style.STROKE); st.setStrokeCap(android.graphics.Paint.Cap.ROUND); st.setStrokeJoin(android.graphics.Paint.Join.ROUND);
        android.graphics.Typeface med = android.graphics.Typeface.create("sans-serif-medium", android.graphics.Typeface.NORMAL);
        tp.setTypeface(med); tp.setColor(0xFFFFFFFF); tp.setTextSize(d * 16f);
        sp.setTypeface(android.graphics.Typeface.DEFAULT); sp.setTextSize(d * 12.5f);
    }

    float dp(float v) { return v * d; }
    boolean canDraw() { return android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 23 || android.provider.Settings.canDrawOverlays(ctx); }

    void show(String n, boolean connected) {
        boolean shown = false;
        if (!aetherIsTv && canDraw()) {
            name = (n == null || n.length() == 0) ? "Perangkat Bluetooth" : n;
            conn = connected;
            t0 = android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis();
            try {
                if (!attached) {
                    float sw = ctx.getResources().getDisplayMetrics().widthPixels;
                    int w = (int) Math.min(sw - dp(28), dp(350));
                    baseY = (int) dp(96);
                    int type = android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26 ? android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY : android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_PHONE;
                    int flags = android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE | android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL | android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS | android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_HARDWARE_ACCELERATED;
                    lp = new android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams(w, (int) dp(96), type, flags, android.graphics.PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT);
                    lp.gravity = android.view.Gravity.BOTTOM | android.view.Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL;
                    lp.y = baseY - (int) dp(150);
                    wm.addView(this, lp);
                    attached = true;
                }
                startLoop();
                shown = true;
            } catch (Exception e) { attached = false; }
        }
        final String fn = name;
        final boolean fc = connected;
        final boolean fs = shown;
        try {
            if (webview1 != null) webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onBtEvent==='function') window.onBtEvent(" + org.json.JSONObject.quote(n == null ? "" : n) + ", " + fc + ", " + fs + ");", null);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    void remove() {
        try { android.view.Choreographer.getInstance().removeFrameCallback(cb); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        looping = false;
        if (attached) { try { wm.removeViewImmediate(this); } catch (Exception ignored) {} }
        attached = false;
    }

    final android.view.Choreographer.FrameCallback cb = new android.view.Choreographer.FrameCallback() {
        @Override public void doFrame(long ns) {
            looping = false;
            if (!attached) return;
            float t = (android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - t0) / 1000f;
            float total = conn ? 4.8f : 3.2f;
            if (t > total) { remove(); return; }
            float p = slide(t, total);
            if (lp != null) {
                int y = baseY - (int) ((1f - p) * dp(150));
                if (y != lp.y) { lp.y = y; try { wm.updateViewLayout(AetherBtPop.this, lp); } catch (Exception ignored) {} }
            }
            invalidate();
            looping = true;
            android.view.Choreographer.getInstance().postFrameCallback(this);
        }
    };

    void startLoop() {
        if (!looping && attached) { looping = true; android.view.Choreographer.getInstance().postFrameCallback(cb); }
    }

    float slide(float t, float total) {
        if (t < 0.6f) { float x = t / 0.6f - 1f; return 1f + 2.70158f * x * x * x + 1.70158f * x * x; }
        if (t > total - 0.5f) { float x = (t - (total - 0.5f)) / 0.5f; return Math.max(0f, 1f - x * x); }
        return 1f;
    }

    @Override public boolean onTouchEvent(android.view.MotionEvent e) {
        if (e.getAction() == android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN) { t0 = android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - 4300L; }
        return true;
    }

    @Override protected void onDraw(android.graphics.Canvas c) {
        float W = getWidth(), H = getHeight();
        if (W < 2f) return;
        float t = (android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - t0) / 1000f;
        float total = conn ? 4.8f : 3.2f;
        float p = slide(t, total);
        setAlpha(Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, p * 1.4f)));
        int col = conn ? 0xFF1ED760 : 0xFFFF5470;
        float m = dp(4);
        r.set(m, m, W - m, H - m);
        float R = dp(30);
        bg.setStyle(android.graphics.Paint.Style.FILL);
        c.drawRoundRect(r, R, R, bg);
        st.setStrokeWidth(dp(1)); st.setColor(0x33FFFFFF);
        c.drawRoundRect(r, R, R, st);

        float cx = m + dp(46), cy = H / 2f;
        for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
            float ph = (t * 0.9f + k / 3f) % 1f;
            float rad = dp(24) + ph * dp(22);
            st.setColor(col); st.setAlpha((int) ((1f - ph) * 120));
            st.setStrokeWidth(dp(2));
            c.drawCircle(cx, cy, rad, st);
        }
        fl.setStyle(android.graphics.Paint.Style.FILL);
        fl.setColor(0xFF26262B);
        c.drawCircle(cx, cy, dp(24), fl);
        st.setColor(0xFFFFFFFF); st.setAlpha(255); st.setStrokeWidth(dp(3));
        r.set(cx - dp(12), cy - dp(13), cx + dp(12), cy + dp(11));
        c.drawArc(r, 190f, 160f, false, st);
        fl.setColor(0xFFFFFFFF);
        r.set(cx - dp(15), cy - dp(2), cx - dp(8), cy + dp(11));
        c.drawRoundRect(r, dp(3), dp(3), fl);
        r.set(cx + dp(8), cy - dp(2), cx + dp(15), cy + dp(11));
        c.drawRoundRect(r, dp(3), dp(3), fl);

        float tx = cx + dp(44);
        float avail = W - tx - dp(54);
        CharSequence nm = android.text.TextUtils.ellipsize(name, tp, avail, android.text.TextUtils.TruncateAt.END);
        c.drawText(nm, 0, nm.length(), tx, cy - dp(3), tp);
        sp.setColor(col);
        c.drawText(conn ? "Terhubung \u2022 Siap memutar musik" : "Terputus", tx, cy + dp(16), sp);

        float bx = W - dp(40), by = cy;
        fl.setColor(col);
        c.drawCircle(bx, by, dp(16), fl);
        if (conn) {
            float prog = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, (t - 0.45f) / 0.45f));
            path.reset();
            path.moveTo(bx - dp(7), by + dp(0.5f)); path.lineTo(bx - dp(2), by + dp(5.5f)); path.lineTo(bx + dp(8), by - dp(5));
            android.graphics.PathMeasure pm = new android.graphics.PathMeasure(path, false);
            seg.reset();
            pm.getSegment(0f, pm.getLength() * prog, seg, true);
            st.setColor(0xFF000000); st.setAlpha(255); st.setStrokeWidth(dp(2.6f));
            c.drawPath(seg, st);
        } else {
            st.setColor(0xFF000000); st.setAlpha(255); st.setStrokeWidth(dp(2.6f));
            c.drawLine(bx - dp(6), by - dp(6), bx + dp(6), by + dp(6), st);
            c.drawLine(bx + dp(6), by - dp(6), bx - dp(6), by + dp(6), st);
        }
    }
}

final AetherBtPop btPop = new AetherBtPop(getApplicationContext());
class AetherBtHandler {
    final long start = android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis();
    String lastName = "";
    long lastT = 0L;
    void handle(android.media.AudioDeviceInfo[] ds, boolean added) {
        try {
            if (ds == null || !aetherPrefs.getBoolean("bt_popup", true)) return;
            long now = android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis();
            if (now - start < 2500L) return;
            for (android.media.AudioDeviceInfo di : ds) {
                if (!di.isSink()) continue;
                int ty = di.getType();
                if (ty != 7 && ty != 8 && ty != 26 && ty != 27) continue;
                String n = di.getProductName() == null ? "" : di.getProductName().toString();
                String key = n + (added ? "+" : "-");
                if (key.equals(lastName) && now - lastT < 4000L) continue;
                lastName = key; lastT = now;
                btPop.show(n, added);
                break;
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }
}
final AetherBtHandler btHandler = new AetherBtHandler();
try {
    if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23) {
        android.media.AudioManager btAm = (android.media.AudioManager) getSystemService(android.content.Context.AUDIO_SERVICE);
        if (btAm != null) {
            btAm.registerAudioDeviceCallback(new android.media.AudioDeviceCallback() {
                @Override public void onAudioDevicesAdded(android.media.AudioDeviceInfo[] ds) { btHandler.handle(ds, true); }
                @Override public void onAudioDevicesRemoved(android.media.AudioDeviceInfo[] ds) { btHandler.handle(ds, false); }
            }, new android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()));
        }
    }
} catch (Exception ignored) {}

// ============================================================================
// 3B. DYNAMIC ISLAND MUSIK (OVERLAY SISTEM ALA iPHONE)
//     - Tetap muncul di atas semua aplikasi walau AETHER ditutup / di latar belakang
//     - Animasi pegas (spring) morph compact <-> expanded, equalizer, marquee, lirik
//     - Ukuran otomatis menyesuaikan lebar layar HP & posisi kamera (punch-hole/notch)
//     - Butuh izin "Tampil di atas aplikasi lain" (SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW), diaktifkan dari Profil
// ============================================================================
class AetherIsland extends android.view.View {
    class Spring {
        float x, v, t, k, c;
        Spring(float x0, float k0, float c0) { x = x0; t = x0; k = k0; c = c0; }
        void step(float dt) {
            int n = 3; float h = dt / n;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { float a = -k * (x - t) - c * v; v += a * h; x += v * h; }
        }
        boolean idle() { return Math.abs(x - t) < 0.0015f && Math.abs(v) < 0.02f; }
    }

    final android.content.Context appCtx;
    final android.view.WindowManager wm;
    final float dens;
    float compactDp = 36f, expandDp = 160f;
    float cfgCw = 0f, cfgEw = 0f, offX = 0f, offY = 0f;
    int tpl = 0;
    boolean previewing = false, previewExpanded = false;
    android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams lp = null;

    boolean attached = false, islandOn = false, fg = true, playing = false, hasTrack = false, dismissed = false;
    boolean touching = false, moved = false, longPressed = false, looping = false;
    String title = "", artist = "", lyric = "";
    android.graphics.Bitmap art = null, artShaderBmp = null;
    android.graphics.BitmapShader artShader = null;
    int accent = 0xFF1ED760, hitBtn = -1;
    float posSec = 0f, durSec = 0f, animT = 0f, mqClock = 0f, anchorTop = 0f, downX = 0f, downY = 0f;
    long posStamp = 0L, transientUntil = 0L, lastNs = 0L;

    final Spring morph = new Spring(0f, 300f, 23f);
    final Spring show = new Spring(0f, 250f, 21f);
    final Spring press = new Spring(1f, 520f, 30f);
    final Spring pop = new Spring(1f, 460f, 18f);

    final android.graphics.Paint bgP = new android.graphics.Paint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.graphics.Paint strokeP = new android.graphics.Paint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.graphics.Paint fillP = new android.graphics.Paint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.graphics.Paint artP = new android.graphics.Paint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.graphics.Paint iconP = new android.graphics.Paint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.text.TextPaint titleP = new android.text.TextPaint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.text.TextPaint subP = new android.text.TextPaint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.text.TextPaint smallP = new android.text.TextPaint(android.graphics.Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG);
    final android.graphics.RectF rect = new android.graphics.RectF();
    final android.graphics.RectF rect2 = new android.graphics.RectF();
    final android.graphics.RectF rPrev = new android.graphics.RectF();
    final android.graphics.RectF rPlay = new android.graphics.RectF();
    final android.graphics.RectF rNext = new android.graphics.RectF();
    final android.graphics.Path path = new android.graphics.Path();
    final android.graphics.Path path2 = new android.graphics.Path();
    final android.graphics.Matrix artMatrix = new android.graphics.Matrix();

    AetherIsland(android.content.Context c) {
        super(c);
        appCtx = c;
        wm = (android.view.WindowManager) c.getSystemService(android.content.Context.WINDOW_SERVICE);
        dens = c.getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;
        bgP.setColor(0xFF000000);
        strokeP.setStyle(android.graphics.Paint.Style.STROKE);
        strokeP.setStrokeWidth(dens * 0.8f);
        strokeP.setColor(0x24FFFFFF);
        android.graphics.Typeface med = android.graphics.Typeface.create("sans-serif-medium", android.graphics.Typeface.NORMAL);
        titleP.setTypeface(med); titleP.setColor(0xFFFFFFFF); titleP.setTextSize(dens * 16f);
        subP.setTypeface(android.graphics.Typeface.DEFAULT); subP.setTextSize(dens * 13.5f);
        smallP.setTypeface(med); smallP.setTextSize(dens * 11f); smallP.setColor(0x99FFFFFF);
        iconP.setColor(0xFFFFFFFF);
        iconP.setStyle(android.graphics.Paint.Style.FILL_AND_STROKE);
        iconP.setStrokeJoin(android.graphics.Paint.Join.ROUND);
        iconP.setStrokeWidth(dens * 1.6f);
        setContentDescription("Dynamic Island AETHER");
        loadCfg();
    }

    float dp(float v) { return v * dens; }
    String vTitle() { return previewing ? "Pratinjau Dynamic Island" : title; }
    String vArtist() { return previewing ? "AETHER Music" : artist; }
    boolean vPlaying() { return previewing ? true : playing; }
    float vDur() { return previewing ? 200f : durSec; }
    String vLyric() { return previewing ? "Lirik berjalan tampil di sini" : lyric; }
    int fgCol() { return tpl == 4 ? 0xFF111111 : 0xFFFFFFFF; }
    void loadCfg() {
        try {
            compactDp = aetherPrefs.getInt("island_ch", 36);
            expandDp = aetherPrefs.getInt("island_eh", 160);
            cfgCw = aetherPrefs.getInt("island_cw", 0);
            cfgEw = aetherPrefs.getInt("island_ew", 0);
            offX = aetherPrefs.getInt("island_x", 0);
            offY = aetherPrefs.getInt("island_y", 0);
            tpl = aetherPrefs.getInt("island_tpl", 0);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }
    void setCfg(int cw, int ch, int ew, int eh, int x, int y, int t) {
        cfgCw = Math.max(0, cw); compactDp = Math.max(26, Math.min(64, ch));
        cfgEw = Math.max(0, ew); expandDp = Math.max(120, Math.min(240, eh));
        offX = x; offY = y; tpl = Math.max(0, Math.min(4, t));
        if (attached) { computeAnchor(); startLoop(); invalidate(); }
    }
    void setPreview(boolean on, boolean expand) {
        previewing = on; previewExpanded = expand;
        if (on) {
            if (canDraw()) { addOverlay(); if (attached) { show.t = 1f; morph.t = expand ? 1f : 0f; removeCallbacks(collapseRun); startLoop(); } }
        } else { applyVisibility(); }
    }
    boolean canDraw() { return android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 23 || android.provider.Settings.canDrawOverlays(appCtx); }
    boolean usable() { return !aetherIsTv && canDraw() && ((islandOn && hasTrack) || previewing); }
    boolean expanded() { return morph.t > 0.5f || morph.x > 0.35f; }
    float predictedPos() {
        float p = previewing ? 64f : posSec;
        if (!previewing && playing) p += (android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - posStamp) / 1000f;
        float dd = vDur();
        if (dd > 0f && p > dd) p = dd;
        return p < 0f ? 0f : p;
    }
    String fmt(float s) { int t = (int) s; return (t / 60) + ":" + ((t % 60) < 10 ? "0" : "") + (t % 60); }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ jendela overlay
    void computeAnchor() {
        float top = dp(6);
        try {
            android.content.res.Resources res = appCtx.getResources();
            int rid = res.getIdentifier("status_bar_height", "dimen", "android");
            int sb = rid > 0 ? res.getDimensionPixelSize(rid) : 0;
            if (sb > 0) top = Math.max(dp(4), sb / 2f - dp(compactDp) / 2f);
            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28) {
                android.view.WindowInsets wi = getWindow().getDecorView().getRootWindowInsets();
                if (wi != null && wi.getDisplayCutout() != null) {
                    float sw = res.getDisplayMetrics().widthPixels;
                    for (android.graphics.Rect r : wi.getDisplayCutout().getBoundingRects()) {
                        if (r.top <= dp(4) && Math.abs(r.centerX() - sw / 2f) < sw * 0.18f) {
                            top = Math.max(dp(2), r.centerY() - dp(compactDp) / 2f);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        anchorTop = top;
    }
    int posY() { return Math.max(0, Math.round(anchorTop + dp(offY))); }

    void addOverlay() {
        if (attached) return;
        try {
            computeAnchor();
            int type = android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26 ? android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY : android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_PHONE;
            int flags = android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE | android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL | android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN | android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS | android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_HARDWARE_ACCELERATED;
            lp = new android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams((int) dp(60), (int) dp(24), type, flags, android.graphics.PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT);
            lp.gravity = android.view.Gravity.TOP | android.view.Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL;
            lp.y = posY();
            lp.x = Math.round(dp(offX));
            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28) {
                lp.layoutInDisplayCutoutMode = android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_SHORT_EDGES;
            }
            lp.setTitle("AetherIsland");
            show.x = 0f; show.v = 0f; morph.x = 0f; morph.v = 0f; morph.t = 0f;
            wm.addView(this, lp);
            attached = true;
        } catch (Exception e) { attached = false; }
    }

    void removeOverlay() {
        try { android.view.Choreographer.getInstance().removeFrameCallback(frameCb); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        looping = false; lastNs = 0L;
        removeCallbacks(collapseRun); removeCallbacks(visRun); removeCallbacks(longPressRun);
        if (attached) { try { wm.removeViewImmediate(this); } catch (Exception ignored) {} }
        attached = false;
        show.x = 0f; show.v = 0f; show.t = 0f; morph.x = 0f; morph.v = 0f; morph.t = 0f;
    }

    void layoutWindow() {
        if (!attached || lp == null) return;
        android.util.DisplayMetrics dm = appCtx.getResources().getDisplayMetrics();
        float sw = dm.widthPixels;
        float cw = cfgCw > 0f ? Math.min(sw - dp(4), dp(cfgCw)) : Math.max(dp(150), Math.min(dp(210), sw * 0.46f));
        float ch = dp(compactDp);
        float ew = cfgEw > 0f ? Math.min(sw - dp(4), dp(cfgEw)) : Math.min(sw - dp(16), dp(400));
        float eh = dp(expandDp);
        float p = morph.x;
        float W = cw + (ew - cw) * p;
        float H = ch + (eh - ch) * p;
        float sv = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1.08f, show.x));
        float k = 0.35f + 0.65f * Math.min(1f, sv);
        W = Math.min(sw - dp(4), Math.max(dp(36), W * k));
        H = Math.max(dp(18), H * k);
        int iw = Math.round(W), ih = Math.round(H);
        int iy = Math.round(posY() - (1f - Math.min(1f, sv)) * dp(14));
        int ix = Math.round(dp(offX));
        if (iw != lp.width || ih != lp.height || iy != lp.y || ix != lp.x) {
            lp.width = iw; lp.height = ih; lp.y = iy; lp.x = ix;
            try { wm.updateViewLayout(this, lp); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ loop animasi
    final android.view.Choreographer.FrameCallback frameCb = new android.view.Choreographer.FrameCallback() {
        @Override public void doFrame(long ns) {
            looping = false;
            if (!attached) return;
            float dt = lastNs == 0L ? 0.016f : Math.min(0.034f, (ns - lastNs) / 1e9f);
            lastNs = ns;
            animT += dt;
            if (expanded()) mqClock += dt;
            morph.step(dt); show.step(dt); press.step(dt); pop.step(dt);
            if (show.t == 0f && show.x < 0.04f) { removeOverlay(); return; }
            layoutWindow();
            invalidate();
            boolean busy = !(morph.idle() && show.idle() && press.idle() && pop.idle());
            if (busy) {
                looping = true;
                android.view.Choreographer.getInstance().postFrameCallback(this);
            } else if ((hasTrack || previewing) && (playing || previewing || expanded())) {
                looping = true;
                android.view.Choreographer.getInstance().postFrameCallbackDelayed(this, 30);
            } else {
                lastNs = 0L;
            }
        }
    };

    void startLoop() {
        if (!attached) return;
        if (!looping) { looping = true; android.view.Choreographer.getInstance().postFrameCallback(frameCb); }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ status tampil / sembunyi
    final Runnable visRun = new Runnable() { @Override public void run() { applyVisibility(); } };
    final Runnable collapseRun = new Runnable() {
        @Override public void run() { if (!touching) { toggleExpand(false); applyVisibility(); } }
    };

    void applyVisibility() {
        boolean want = previewing ? canDraw() : (usable() && !dismissed && (!fg || android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < transientUntil || morph.t > 0.5f));
        if (want) {
            addOverlay();
            if (attached) {
                show.t = 1f; startLoop();
                removeCallbacks(visRun);
                if (fg) {
                    long left = transientUntil - android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis();
                    postDelayed(visRun, Math.max(0L, left) + 80L);
                }
            }
        } else if (attached) {
            show.t = 0f; morph.t = 0f; startLoop();
        }
    }

    void toggleExpand(boolean on) {
        morph.t = on ? 1f : 0f;
        removeCallbacks(collapseRun);
        if (on && !previewing) postDelayed(collapseRun, 5200);
        startLoop();
    }

    void scheduleCollapse(long ms) { removeCallbacks(collapseRun); postDelayed(collapseRun, ms); }

    void dismiss() { dismissed = true; applyVisibility(); }

    void applyEnabled(boolean on) {
        islandOn = on && !aetherIsTv;
        if (!islandOn && attached) { show.t = 0f; morph.t = 0f; startLoop(); }
        else applyVisibility();
    }

    void onForeground(boolean f) {
        fg = f;
        if (f) {
            transientUntil = 0L;
            try {
                if (webview1 != null) webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.refreshIslandToggle==='function') window.refreshIslandToggle();", null);
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        try {
            if (webview1 != null) webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onAppState==='function') window.onAppState(" + (f ? "true" : "false") + ");", null);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        applyVisibility();
    }

    void destroy() { removeOverlay(); }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ data dari aplikasi
    void update(String t, String a, android.graphics.Bitmap b, boolean isPlay) {
        if (t == null) t = ""; if (a == null) a = "";
        boolean changed = !t.equals(title) || !a.equals(artist);
        boolean playChanged = isPlay != playing;
        if (playChanged) { posSec = predictedPos(); posStamp = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime(); }
        title = t; artist = a; hasTrack = true; playing = isPlay;
        if (b != null && b != art) { art = b; accent = computeAccent(b); }
        if (changed) {
            dismissed = false; lyric = ""; posSec = 0f; durSec = 0f; mqClock = 0f;
            posStamp = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();
        }
        if (fg && (changed || playChanged)) transientUntil = android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() + 3600L;
        applyVisibility();
        if (!attached) return;
        if (changed) { toggleExpand(true); morph.v += 3f; pop.v -= 3f; }
        else if (playChanged) { morph.v += 4.5f; pop.x = 0.86f; }
        startLoop(); invalidate();
    }

    void setProgress(float pos, float dur, boolean pl) {
        if (previewing) return;
        posSec = pos; durSec = dur; posStamp = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();
        if (pl != playing) { playing = pl; startLoop(); }
        if (attached && expanded()) invalidate();
    }

    void setLyric(String s) {
        lyric = s == null ? "" : s;
        if (attached) invalidate();
    }

    int computeAccent(android.graphics.Bitmap b) {
        try {
            android.graphics.Bitmap sm = android.graphics.Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(b, 12, 12, true);
            float best = -1f; int bestColor = 0xFF1ED760; float[] hsv = new float[3];
            for (int x = 0; x < 12; x++) {
                for (int y = 0; y < 12; y++) {
                    int px = sm.getPixel(x, y);
                    android.graphics.Color.colorToHSV(px, hsv);
                    float score = hsv[1] * hsv[2] * (hsv[2] > 0.25f ? 1f : 0.2f);
                    if (score > best) { best = score; bestColor = px; }
                }
            }
            android.graphics.Color.colorToHSV(bestColor, hsv);
            hsv[1] = Math.max(0.55f, hsv[1]); hsv[2] = Math.max(0.85f, hsv[2]);
            return android.graphics.Color.HSVToColor(hsv);
        } catch (Exception e) { return 0xFF1ED760; }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ gambar
    int scaleColor(int col, float f) {
        return android.graphics.Color.rgb(Math.min(255, (int) (android.graphics.Color.red(col) * f)), Math.min(255, (int) (android.graphics.Color.green(col) * f)), Math.min(255, (int) (android.graphics.Color.blue(col) * f)));
    }

    void drawBackground(android.graphics.Canvas c, float W, float H, float R) {
        bgP.setShader(null);
        if (tpl == 1) {
            bgP.setShader(new android.graphics.LinearGradient(0f, 0f, W, H, scaleColor(accent, 0.5f), 0xFF000000, android.graphics.Shader.TileMode.CLAMP));
        } else if (tpl == 2) {
            bgP.setColor(0xE61C1C1E);
        } else if (tpl == 4) {
            bgP.setColor(0xFFF2F2F7);
        } else {
            bgP.setColor(0xFF000000);
        }
        c.drawRoundRect(rect, R, R, bgP);
        bgP.setShader(null);
        if (tpl == 3) {
            float[] ws = new float[]{ 12f, 8f, 4f, 2f };
            int[] al = new int[]{ 26, 48, 110, 255 };
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                strokeP.setStrokeWidth(dens * ws[i]);
                strokeP.setColor(accent); strokeP.setAlpha(al[i]);
                c.drawRoundRect(rect, R, R, strokeP);
            }
        } else {
            strokeP.setStrokeWidth(dens * 0.8f);
            strokeP.setColor(tpl == 4 ? 0x24000000 : (tpl == 2 ? 0x55FFFFFF : 0x24FFFFFF));
            c.drawRoundRect(rect, R, R, strokeP);
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDraw(android.graphics.Canvas c) {
        float W = getWidth(), H = getHeight();
        if (W < 2f || H < 2f) return;
        float p = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, morph.x));
        float ps = press.x;
        c.save();
        c.scale(ps, ps, W / 2f, H / 2f);
        float R = Math.min(H / 2f, dp(18) + (dp(40) - dp(18)) * p);
        rect.set(0f, 0f, W, H);
        drawBackground(c, W, H, R);
        path.reset();
        path.addRoundRect(rect, R, R, android.graphics.Path.Direction.CW);
        c.clipPath(path);
        float ac = 1f - Math.min(1f, p * 2.4f);
        float ae = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, (p - 0.4f) * 2.4f));
        if (ac > 0.02f) drawCompact(c, W, H, ac);
        if (ae > 0.02f) drawExpanded(c, W, H, ae);
        c.restore();
    }

    void drawCompact(android.graphics.Canvas c, float W, float H, float a) {
        int ia = (int) (255 * a);
        float pad = Math.max(dp(4), H * 0.16f);
        float s = H - 2f * pad;
        drawArt(c, pad + dp(1), (H - s) / 2f, s, Math.max(dp(5), s * 0.28f), ia);
        float eqW = Math.min(dp(22), W * 0.12f);
        drawEq(c, W - pad - dp(9) - eqW, H / 2f, eqW, H * 0.46f, ia, 4);
    }

    void drawExpanded(android.graphics.Canvas c, float W, float H, float a) {
        int ia = (int) (255 * a);
        int fg = fgCol();
        float sc = Math.max(0.8f, Math.min(1.5f, expandDp / 160f));
        float fs = Math.max(0.85f, Math.min(1.25f, sc));
        float pad = dp(18);
        float artS = dp(58) * sc;
        float top = dp(16) * sc;
        titleP.setTextSize(dens * 16f * fs); subP.setTextSize(dens * 13.5f * fs); smallP.setTextSize(dens * 11f * fs);
        drawArt(c, pad, top, artS, dp(13) * sc, ia);
        float tx = pad + artS + dp(12);
        float eqW = dp(22);
        float avail = W - tx - pad - eqW - dp(10);
        if (avail < dp(30)) avail = dp(30);
        titleP.setColor(fg); titleP.setAlpha(ia);
        drawMarquee(c, vTitle(), tx, top + dp(24) * sc, avail, titleP);
        boolean useLy = vPlaying() && vLyric().length() > 0;
        if (useLy) { subP.setColor(accent); subP.setAlpha(ia); } else { subP.setColor(fg); subP.setAlpha((int) (ia * 0.62f)); }
        CharSequence ell = android.text.TextUtils.ellipsize(useLy ? vLyric() : vArtist(), subP, avail, android.text.TextUtils.TruncateAt.END);
        c.drawText(ell, 0, ell.length(), tx, top + dp(46) * sc, subP);
        drawEq(c, W - pad - eqW, top + dp(29) * sc, eqW, dp(24) * sc, ia, 4);

        float py = dp(92) * sc;
        float dur = vDur();
        float pos = predictedPos();
        float frac = dur > 0f ? Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, pos / dur)) : 0f;
        fillP.setColor(fg); fillP.setAlpha((int) (ia * 0.25f));
        rect2.set(pad, py - dp(2), W - pad, py + dp(2));
        c.drawRoundRect(rect2, dp(2), dp(2), fillP);
        fillP.setAlpha((int) (ia * 0.92f));
        rect2.set(pad, py - dp(2), pad + (W - 2f * pad) * frac, py + dp(2));
        c.drawRoundRect(rect2, dp(2), dp(2), fillP);
        smallP.setColor(fg); smallP.setAlpha((int) (ia * 0.6f));
        smallP.setTextAlign(android.graphics.Paint.Align.LEFT);
        c.drawText(fmt(pos), pad, py + dp(16) * fs, smallP);
        smallP.setTextAlign(android.graphics.Paint.Align.RIGHT);
        c.drawText("-" + fmt(Math.max(0f, dur - pos)), W - pad, py + dp(16) * fs, smallP);
        smallP.setTextAlign(android.graphics.Paint.Align.LEFT);

        float cy = dp(133) * sc, cx = W / 2f;
        float gap = Math.min(dp(66), W * 0.2f);
        float hh = dp(26) * sc;
        iconP.setColor(fg); iconP.setAlpha(ia);
        rPrev.set(cx - gap - dp(28), cy - hh, cx - gap + dp(28), cy + hh);
        rPlay.set(cx - dp(30), cy - hh, cx + dp(30), cy + hh);
        rNext.set(cx + gap - dp(28), cy - hh, cx + gap + dp(28), cy + hh);
        iconSkip(c, cx - gap, cy, dp(22) * sc * btnScale(0), true);
        if (vPlaying()) iconPause(c, cx, cy, dp(30) * sc * btnScale(1)); else iconPlay(c, cx, cy, dp(30) * sc * btnScale(1));
        iconSkip(c, cx + gap, cy, dp(22) * sc * btnScale(2), false);
    }

    float btnScale(int idx) { return hitBtn == idx ? Math.max(0.7f, Math.min(1.15f, pop.x)) : 1f; }

    void drawArt(android.graphics.Canvas c, float x, float y, float s, float r, int alpha) {
        rect2.set(x, y, x + s, y + s);
        if (art != null && !art.isRecycled()) {
            float bw = art.getWidth(), bh = art.getHeight();
            float side = Math.min(bw, bh) * 0.76f;
            float sl = (bw - side) / 2f, st = (bh - side) / 2f;
            artMatrix.setTranslate(-sl, -st);
            artMatrix.postScale(s / side, s / side);
            artMatrix.postTranslate(x, y);
            if (artShader == null || artShaderBmp != art) {
                artShader = new android.graphics.BitmapShader(art, android.graphics.Shader.TileMode.CLAMP, android.graphics.Shader.TileMode.CLAMP);
                artShaderBmp = art;
            }
            artShader.setLocalMatrix(artMatrix);
            artP.setShader(artShader);
            artP.setAlpha(alpha);
            c.drawRoundRect(rect2, r, r, artP);
        } else {
            fillP.setColor(accent); fillP.setAlpha((int) (alpha * 0.85f));
            c.drawRoundRect(rect2, r, r, fillP);
            smallP.setColor(0xFFFFFFFF); smallP.setAlpha(alpha); smallP.setTextAlign(android.graphics.Paint.Align.CENTER);
            c.drawText("\u266A", x + s / 2f, y + s / 2f + dp(4), smallP);
            smallP.setTextAlign(android.graphics.Paint.Align.LEFT);
        }
    }

    void drawEq(android.graphics.Canvas c, float x, float cy, float w, float maxH, int alpha, int n) {
        float bw = w / (n * 1.7f);
        float gap = (w - bw * n) / (n - 1);
        fillP.setColor(accent); fillP.setAlpha(alpha);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            float lvl = vPlaying() ? (0.28f + 0.72f * (0.5f + 0.5f * (float) Math.sin(animT * (4.2f + i * 1.7f) + i * 1.9f))) : 0.2f;
            float h = Math.max(bw, maxH * lvl);
            float bx = x + i * (bw + gap);
            rect2.set(bx, cy - h / 2f, bx + bw, cy + h / 2f);
            c.drawRoundRect(rect2, bw / 2f, bw / 2f, fillP);
        }
    }

    void drawMarquee(android.graphics.Canvas c, String text, float x, float baseline, float avail, android.text.TextPaint p) {
        float tw = p.measureText(text);
        c.save();
        c.clipRect(x, baseline - p.getTextSize() * 1.1f, x + avail, baseline + p.getTextSize() * 0.4f);
        if (tw <= avail) {
            c.drawText(text, x, baseline, p);
        } else {
            float gap = dp(28), speed = dp(34), pause = 1.4f;
            float cycle = (tw + gap) / speed;
            float t = mqClock % (cycle + pause);
            float off = t < pause ? 0f : (t - pause) * speed;
            c.drawText(text, x - off, baseline, p);
            c.drawText(text, x - off + tw + gap, baseline, p);
        }
        c.restore();
    }

    void iconPlay(android.graphics.Canvas c, float cx, float cy, float s) {
        path2.reset();
        path2.moveTo(cx - s * 0.34f, cy - s * 0.5f);
        path2.lineTo(cx + s * 0.52f, cy);
        path2.lineTo(cx - s * 0.34f, cy + s * 0.5f);
        path2.close();
        c.drawPath(path2, iconP);
    }

    void iconPause(android.graphics.Canvas c, float cx, float cy, float s) {
        float bw = s * 0.28f, gp = s * 0.16f;
        rect2.set(cx - gp / 2f - bw, cy - s * 0.5f, cx - gp / 2f, cy + s * 0.5f);
        c.drawRoundRect(rect2, bw * 0.3f, bw * 0.3f, iconP);
        rect2.set(cx + gp / 2f, cy - s * 0.5f, cx + gp / 2f + bw, cy + s * 0.5f);
        c.drawRoundRect(rect2, bw * 0.3f, bw * 0.3f, iconP);
    }

    void iconSkip(android.graphics.Canvas c, float cx, float cy, float s, boolean back) {
        float d = back ? -1f : 1f;
        path2.reset();
        path2.moveTo(cx - d * s * 0.5f, cy - s * 0.42f);
        path2.lineTo(cx + d * 0.02f * s, cy);
        path2.lineTo(cx - d * s * 0.5f, cy + s * 0.42f);
        path2.close();
        path2.moveTo(cx + d * 0.02f * s, cy - s * 0.42f);
        path2.lineTo(cx + d * s * 0.54f, cy);
        path2.lineTo(cx + d * 0.02f * s, cy + s * 0.42f);
        path2.close();
        c.drawPath(path2, iconP);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ sentuhan
    final Runnable longPressRun = new Runnable() {
        @Override public void run() {
            if (touching && !moved) {
                longPressed = true;
                performHapticFeedback(android.view.HapticFeedbackConstants.LONG_PRESS);
                toggleExpand(morph.t < 0.5f);
            }
        }
    };

    int hitTest(float x, float y) {
        if (rPrev.contains(x, y)) return 0;
        if (rPlay.contains(x, y)) return 1;
        if (rNext.contains(x, y)) return 2;
        return -1;
    }

    void sendJs(String js) {
        try {
            if (webview1 != null) { webview1.onResume(); webview1.resumeTimers(); webview1.evaluateJavascript(js, null); }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    void openApp() {
        try {
            android.content.Intent i = new android.content.Intent(appCtx, MainActivity.class);
            i.setAction(android.content.Intent.ACTION_MAIN);
            i.addCategory(android.content.Intent.CATEGORY_LAUNCHER);
            i.addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK | android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP | android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_REORDER_TO_FRONT);
            appCtx.startActivity(i);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    void handleTap(float x, float y) {
        if (previewing) return;
        if (morph.x > 0.6f) {
            int h = hitTest(x, y);
            if (h == 0) { sendJs("if(typeof prevTrack==='function'){ window.userPaused=false; prevTrack(); }"); return; }
            if (h == 1) {
                posSec = predictedPos(); posStamp = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();
                playing = !playing;
                sendJs("if(typeof togglePlayFromNotif==='function') togglePlayFromNotif();");
                startLoop(); return;
            }
            if (h == 2) { sendJs("if(typeof nextTrack==='function'){ window.userPaused=false; nextTrack(); }"); return; }
        }
        openApp();
    }

    @Override public boolean onTouchEvent(android.view.MotionEvent e) {
        int act = e.getActionMasked();
        float x = e.getX(), y = e.getY();
        if (act == android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN) {
            touching = true; moved = false; longPressed = false; downX = x; downY = y;
            hitBtn = (morph.x > 0.6f) ? hitTest(x, y) : -1;
            press.t = hitBtn >= 0 ? 1f : 0.965f;
            pop.t = hitBtn >= 0 ? 0.8f : 1f;
            removeCallbacks(collapseRun);
            postDelayed(longPressRun, 420);
            startLoop();
            return true;
        } else if (act == android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE) {
            if (Math.abs(x - downX) > dp(10) || Math.abs(y - downY) > dp(10)) { moved = true; removeCallbacks(longPressRun); }
            return true;
        } else if (act == android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_UP || act == android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL) {
            touching = false;
            removeCallbacks(longPressRun);
            press.t = 1f; pop.t = 1f;
            if (act == android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_UP && !longPressed) {
                float dy = y - downY, dx = x - downX;
                if (moved && dy < -dp(18) && Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx)) {
                    if (morph.t > 0.5f) toggleExpand(false); else dismiss();
                } else if (moved && dy > dp(18) && Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx)) {
                    toggleExpand(true);
                } else if (!moved) {
                    handleTap(x, y);
                }
            }
            hitBtn = -1;
            if (morph.t > 0.5f) scheduleCollapse(4500);
            startLoop();
            return true;
        }
        return true;
    }
}

final AetherIsland island = new AetherIsland(getApplicationContext());
island.applyEnabled(aetherPrefs.getBoolean("island_enabled", false));

getApplication().registerActivityLifecycleCallbacks(new android.app.Application.ActivityLifecycleCallbacks() {
    @Override public void onActivityResumed(android.app.Activity a) {
        if (a == MainActivity.this) {
            try { if (!aetherPrefs.getBoolean("priv_secure", false)) getWindow().clearFlags(android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            aetherCaptureIntent.run();
            { int vb = aetherPrefs.getInt("vol_boost", 100); if (vb > 100 && aetherEnh[0] == null) aetherBoost.apply(vb); }
            island.onForeground(true);
        }
    }
    @Override public void onActivityPaused(android.app.Activity a) {
        if (a == MainActivity.this) {
            try { if (aetherPrefs.getBoolean("priv_recents", false)) getWindow().addFlags(android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            island.onForeground(false);
        }
    }
    @Override public void onActivityDestroyed(android.app.Activity a) { if (a == MainActivity.this) island.destroy(); }
    @Override public void onActivityCreated(android.app.Activity a, android.os.Bundle b) {}
    @Override public void onActivityStarted(android.app.Activity a) {}
    @Override public void onActivityStopped(android.app.Activity a) {}
    @Override public void onActivitySaveInstanceState(android.app.Activity a, android.os.Bundle b) {}
});

    @Override public android.graphics.Bitmap getDefaultVideoPoster() { return android.graphics.Bitmap.createBitmap(1, 1, android.graphics.Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888); }
});

webview1.setWebViewClient(new android.webkit.WebViewClient() {
    @Override public boolean shouldOverrideUrlLoading(android.webkit.WebView view, String url) {
        return !(url != null && (url.contains("youtube.com") || url.contains("youtube-nocookie.com") || url.contains("googlevideo.com") || url.contains("ytimg.com") || url.contains("music.aether.app")));
    }
    @Override
    public android.webkit.WebResourceResponse shouldInterceptRequest(android.webkit.WebView view, final android.webkit.WebResourceRequest request) {
        if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 21 && request != null && request.getUrl() != null) {
            String u = request.getUrl().toString();
            if (u.contains("music.aether.app/offline_audio/")) {
                try {
                    String vid = u.substring(u.lastIndexOf("/") + 1).replace(".m4a", "").trim();
                    java.io.File f = new java.io.File(offlineDir, vid + ".m4a");
                    if (f.exists() && f.length() > 0) {
                        java.util.Map<String, String> headers = new java.util.HashMap<>();
                        headers.put("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");
                        headers.put("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
                        headers.put("Content-Length", String.valueOf(f.length()));
                        return new android.webkit.WebResourceResponse("audio/mp4", "UTF-8", 200, "OK", headers, new java.io.FileInputStream(f));
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        }
        return super.shouldInterceptRequest(view, request);
    }
});

webview1.addJavascriptInterface(new Object() {
    private android.graphics.Bitmap cachedArt = null; private String cachedLogoUrl = "";

    // ================= v4.3: BUKA LINK DENGAN PILIHAN APLIKASI (WhatsApp, Spotify, dll) =================
    private void openLinkInternal(final String url) {
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try {
                    android.net.Uri u = android.net.Uri.parse(url);
                    String scheme = u.getScheme() == null ? "" : u.getScheme().toLowerCase();
                    if (!("http".equals(scheme) || "https".equals(scheme) || "whatsapp".equals(scheme))) return;
                    String host = u.getHost() == null ? "" : u.getHost().toLowerCase();
                    String[] pkgs = new String[0];
                    if (host.endsWith("whatsapp.com") || host.equals("wa.me")) pkgs = new String[]{ "com.whatsapp", "com.whatsapp.w4b" };
                    else if (host.endsWith("spotify.com") || host.startsWith("spotify.")) pkgs = new String[]{ "com.spotify.music" };
                    else if (host.endsWith("youtube.com") || host.equals("youtu.be")) pkgs = new String[]{ "com.google.android.youtube", "com.google.android.apps.youtube.music" };
                    else if (host.endsWith("instagram.com")) pkgs = new String[]{ "com.instagram.android" };
                    else if (host.endsWith("tiktok.com")) pkgs = new String[]{ "com.zhiliaoapp.musically", "com.ss.android.ugc.trill" };
                    else if (host.equals("t.me") || host.endsWith("telegram.org")) pkgs = new String[]{ "org.telegram.messenger" };
                    else if (host.endsWith("facebook.com")) pkgs = new String[]{ "com.facebook.katana" };
                    else if (host.equals("x.com") || host.endsWith("twitter.com")) pkgs = new String[]{ "com.twitter.android" };
                    for (String p : pkgs) {
                        try {
                            android.content.Intent i = new android.content.Intent(android.content.Intent.ACTION_VIEW, u);
                            i.setPackage(p);
                            i.addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                            startActivity(i);
                            return;
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                    }
                    android.content.Intent base = new android.content.Intent(android.content.Intent.ACTION_VIEW, u);
                    android.content.Intent ch = android.content.Intent.createChooser(base, "Buka dengan");
                    ch.addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                    startActivity(ch);
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        });
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void openLink(String url) { openLinkInternal(url); }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void openLinkSettings() {
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try {
                    android.content.Intent i;
                    if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 31) i = new android.content.Intent(android.provider.Settings.ACTION_APP_OPEN_BY_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, android.net.Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName()));
                    else i = new android.content.Intent(android.provider.Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS, android.net.Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName()));
                    startActivity(i);
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        });
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public boolean isOnline() { return isOnlineNative(); }

    // ================= v4.3: SIMPAN / BAGIKAN GAMBAR (JPG streak & statistik) =================
    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public String saveImage(String b64, String fileName, final boolean share) {
        try {
            byte[] bytes = android.util.Base64.decode(b64, android.util.Base64.DEFAULT);
            String name = fileName.replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9._-]", "_");
            if (!name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".jpg")) name = name + ".jpg";
            android.net.Uri uri = null;
            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 29) {
                android.content.ContentValues cv = new android.content.ContentValues();
                cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, name);
                cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.MIME_TYPE, "image/jpeg");
                cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.RELATIVE_PATH, "Pictures/AETHER");
                cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 1);
                uri = getContentResolver().insert(android.provider.MediaStore.Images.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, cv);
                if (uri == null) return "FAIL";
                java.io.OutputStream os = getContentResolver().openOutputStream(uri);
                os.write(bytes); os.flush(); os.close();
                cv.clear(); cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 0);
                getContentResolver().update(uri, cv, null, null);
            } else {
                android.graphics.Bitmap bmp = android.graphics.BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(bytes, 0, bytes.length);
                String p = android.provider.MediaStore.Images.Media.insertImage(getContentResolver(), bmp, name, "AETHER");
                if (p == null) return "FAIL";
                uri = android.net.Uri.parse(p);
            }
            if (share && uri != null) {
                final android.net.Uri fu = uri;
                runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
                    @Override public void run() {
                        try {
                            android.content.Intent s = new android.content.Intent(android.content.Intent.ACTION_SEND);
                            s.setType("image/jpeg");
                            s.putExtra(android.content.Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, fu);
                            s.putExtra(android.content.Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, "Dari AETHER Music \uD83C\uDFB5");
                            s.addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION);
                            android.content.Intent ch = android.content.Intent.createChooser(s, "Bagikan");
                            ch.addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                            startActivity(ch);
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                    }
                });
            }
            return "OK";
        } catch (Exception e) { return "FAIL"; }
    }

    // ================= v4.3: ANIMASI BLUETOOTH =================
    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public boolean getBtPopup() { return aetherPrefs.getBoolean("bt_popup", true); }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface

        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() { btPop.show("AETHER Buds Pro", true); }
        });
    }

    // ================= v4.2: PENGUAT VOLUME =================
    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public int getVolumeBoost() { return aetherPrefs.getInt("vol_boost", 100); }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public String setVolumeBoost(int pct) {
        final int p = Math.max(100, Math.min(300, pct));
        aetherPrefs.edit().putInt("vol_boost", p).apply();
        final String[] res = new String[]{ "OK" };
        final java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch latch = new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try { res[0] = aetherBoost.apply(p); } catch (Exception e) { res[0] = "FAIL"; }
                latch.countDown();
            }
        });
        try { latch.await(1500, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        return res[0];
    }

    // ================= v4.2: LINK MASUK =================

        aetherPendingLink[0] = "";
        return s == null ? "" : s;
    }

    // ================= v4.2: HTTP TEKS (halaman embed Spotify) =================
    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void nativeHttpText(final String url, final String reqId) {
        new Thread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                String body = "";
                boolean ok = false;
                try {
                    java.net.URL u = new java.net.URL(url);
                    String host = u.getHost() == null ? "" : u.getHost().toLowerCase();
                    if ("https".equals(u.getProtocol()) && httpHostOk(host)) {
                        java.net.HttpURLConnection conn = (java.net.HttpURLConnection) u.openConnection();
                        conn.setConnectTimeout(8000); conn.setReadTimeout(12000);
                        conn.setInstanceFollowRedirects(true);
                        conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 13; Pixel 7) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0.0.0 Mobile Safari/537.36");
                        conn.setRequestProperty("Accept", "text/html,application/json;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8");
                        conn.setRequestProperty("Accept-Language", "en-US,en;q=0.8");
                        if (conn.getResponseCode() == 200) {
                            java.io.InputStream is = conn.getInputStream();
                            java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
                            byte[] buf = new byte[8192]; int n; int total = 0;
                            while ((n = is.read(buf)) != -1 && total < 3145728) { baos.write(buf, 0, n); total += n; }
                            is.close();
                            body = new String(baos.toByteArray(), "UTF-8");
                            ok = true;
                        }
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                final boolean fOk = ok;
                final String fBody = body;
                runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
                    @Override public void run() {
                        if (webview1 != null) {
                            webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeHttpText==='function') window.onNativeHttpText('" + reqId + "', " + fOk + ", " + org.json.JSONObject.quote(fBody) + ");", null);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        }).start();
    }

    // ---------------- DYNAMIC ISLAND (kontrol dari Profil + data real-time dari JS) ----------------
    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public String getIslandState() {
        if (aetherIsTv || !aetherPrefs.getBoolean("island_enabled", false)) return "off";
        return island.canDraw() ? "on" : "noperm";
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface

        if (aetherIsTv) return "UNSUPPORTED";
        aetherPrefs.edit().putBoolean("island_enabled", on).apply();
        final boolean needPerm = on && !island.canDraw();
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {

                    island.applyEnabled(on);
                    if (needPerm) {
                        android.content.Intent pi = new android.content.Intent(android.provider.Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION, android.net.Uri.parse("package:" + getPackageName()));
                        startActivity(pi);
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        });
        return needPerm ? "NEED_PERMISSION" : "OK";
    }
