    // ---------------- RUANG TERDEKAT: LOKASI KASAR (dibulatkan ~1 km, tidak disimpan) ----------------
    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void getCoarseLocation(final String reqId) {
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() { locFlow(reqId, 0); }
        });
    }

    private void locFlow(final String reqId, final int attempt) {
        try {
            boolean has = android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 23
                || checkSelfPermission("android.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION") == android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
                || checkSelfPermission("android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION") == android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED;
            if (!has) {
                if (attempt == 0) requestPermissions(new String[]{ "android.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION" }, 1002);
                if (attempt >= 30) { locResult(reqId, "DENIED", 0d, 0d); return; }
                new android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(new Runnable() {
                    @Override public void run() { locFlow(reqId, attempt + 1); }
                }, 1000);
                return;
            }
            fetchLocation(reqId);
        } catch (Exception e) { locResult(reqId, "UNAVAILABLE", 0d, 0d); }
    }

    private void fetchLocation(final String reqId) {
        try {
            final android.location.LocationManager lm = (android.location.LocationManager) getSystemService(android.content.Context.LOCATION_SERVICE);
            if (lm == null) { locResult(reqId, "UNAVAILABLE", 0d, 0d); return; }
            android.location.Location best = null;
            for (String prov : lm.getProviders(true)) {
                try {
                    android.location.Location l = lm.getLastKnownLocation(prov);
                    if (l != null && (best == null || l.getTime() > best.getTime())) best = l;
                } catch (SecurityException ignored) {}
            }
            final android.location.Location fallback = best;
            if (best != null && System.currentTimeMillis() - best.getTime() < 6L * 60L * 60L * 1000L) {
                locResult(reqId, "OK", best.getLatitude(), best.getLongitude());
                return;
            }
            String prov = null;
            if (lm.isProviderEnabled(android.location.LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER)) prov = android.location.LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER;
            else if (lm.isProviderEnabled(android.location.LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER)) prov = android.location.LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER;
            if (prov == null) {
                if (fallback != null) locResult(reqId, "OK", fallback.getLatitude(), fallback.getLongitude());
                else locResult(reqId, "UNAVAILABLE", 0d, 0d);
                return;
            }
            final boolean[] done = new boolean[]{ false };
            final android.location.LocationListener ll = new android.location.LocationListener() {
                @Override public void onLocationChanged(android.location.Location l) {
                    if (done[0]) return;
                    done[0] = true;
                    try { lm.removeUpdates(this); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                    locResult(reqId, "OK", l.getLatitude(), l.getLongitude());
                }
                @Override public void onStatusChanged(String p, int s, android.os.Bundle b) {}
                @Override public void onProviderEnabled(String p) {}
                @Override public void onProviderDisabled(String p) {}
            };
            lm.requestSingleUpdate(prov, ll, android.os.Looper.getMainLooper());
            new android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(new Runnable() {
                @Override public void run() {
                    if (done[0]) return;
                    done[0] = true;
                    try { lm.removeUpdates(ll); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                    if (fallback != null) locResult(reqId, "OK", fallback.getLatitude(), fallback.getLongitude());
                    else locResult(reqId, "UNAVAILABLE", 0d, 0d);
                }
            }, 10000);
        } catch (Exception e) { locResult(reqId, "UNAVAILABLE", 0d, 0d); }
    }

    private void locResult(final String reqId, final String status, double lat, double lng) {
        // dibulatkan 2 desimal (~1 km) demi privasi
        final double rl = Math.round(lat * 100d) / 100d;
        final double rg = Math.round(lng * 100d) / 100d;
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                if (webview1 != null) {
                    webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeLocationResult==='function') window.onNativeLocationResult('" + reqId + "', '" + status + "', " + rl + ", " + rg + ");", null);
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // ---------------- HTTP GET AMAN (whitelist: lirik + terjemahan) ----------------
    private boolean httpHostOk(String h) {
        return h.equals("lrclib.net") || h.endsWith(".lrclib.net") || h.equals("translate.googleapis.com") || h.equals("api.mymemory.translated.net") || h.equals("open.spotify.com") || h.equals("spotify.link") || h.equals("spotify.app.link") || h.equals("www.youtube.com") || h.equals("youtube.com");
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void nativeHttpGet(final String url, final String reqId) {
        new Thread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                String body = "";
                boolean ok = false;
                try {
                    java.net.URL u = new java.net.URL(url);
                    String host = u.getHost() == null ? "" : u.getHost().toLowerCase();
                    if ("https".equals(u.getProtocol()) && httpHostOk(host)) {
                        java.net.HttpURLConnection conn = (java.net.HttpURLConnection) u.openConnection();
                        conn.setConnectTimeout(6000); conn.setReadTimeout(8000);
                        conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "AETHER Music/4.3 (Android)");
                        conn.setRequestProperty("Accept", "application/json");
                        if (conn.getResponseCode() == 200) {
                            java.io.InputStream is = conn.getInputStream();
                            java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
                            byte[] buf = new byte[4096]; int n; int total = 0;
                            while ((n = is.read(buf)) != -1 && total < 524288) { baos.write(buf, 0, n); total += n; }
                            is.close();
                            body = new String(baos.toByteArray(), "UTF-8");
                            ok = true;
                        }
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                final boolean fOk = ok;
                final String fBody = body;
                runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
                    @Override public void run() {
                        if (webview1 != null) {
                            webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeHttpResult==='function') window.onNativeHttpResult('" + reqId + "', " + fOk + ", " + org.json.JSONObject.quote(fBody) + ");", null);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        }).start();
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void shareRoom(String code, String name) {
        try {
            android.content.Intent shareIntent = new android.content.Intent(android.content.Intent.ACTION_SEND);

            startActivity(android.content.Intent.createChooser(shareIntent, "Bagikan Ruangan"));
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    }

    // ================= v4.1.1: IZIN GENERIK (mikrofon, lokasi, notifikasi) =================
    private boolean permAllowed(String p) {
        return "android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO".equals(p) || "android.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION".equals(p) || "android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS".equals(p);
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public boolean hasPermission(String perm) {
        if (!permAllowed(perm)) return false;
        if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 23) return true;
        if ("android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS".equals(perm) && android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 33) return true;
        return checkSelfPermission(perm) == android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED;
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void requestPermission(final String perm, final String reqId) {
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() { permFlow(perm, reqId, 0); }
        });
    }

    private void permFlow(final String perm, final String reqId, final int attempt) {
        try {
            if (!permAllowed(perm)) { permResult(reqId, false); return; }
            if (hasPermission(perm)) { permResult(reqId, true); return; }
            if (attempt == 0) requestPermissions(new String[]{ perm }, 1010);
            if (attempt >= 30) { permResult(reqId, false); return; }
            new android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(new Runnable() {
                @Override public void run() { permFlow(perm, reqId, attempt + 1); }
            }, 1000);
        } catch (Exception e) { permResult(reqId, false); }
    }

    private void permResult(final String reqId, final boolean ok) {
        if (webview1 != null) {
            webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativePerm==='function') window.onNativePerm('" + reqId + "', " + ok + ");", null);
        }
    }

    // ================= v4.1.1: PENCARIAN SUARA (SpeechRecognizer bawaan Android) =================
    private android.speech.SpeechRecognizer voiceSr = null;
    private long voiceLastLevel = 0L;

    private void voiceEvt(final String reqId, final String type, final String text) {
        if (webview1 != null) {
            webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeVoice==='function') window.onNativeVoice('" + reqId + "', '" + type + "', " + org.json.JSONObject.quote(text == null ? "" : text) + ");", null);
        }
    }

    private void voiceKill() {
        try { if (voiceSr != null) { voiceSr.cancel(); voiceSr.destroy(); } } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        voiceSr = null;
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void startVoiceSearch(final String reqId, final String lang) {
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() { voiceStart(reqId, lang, 0); }
        });
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void stopVoiceSearch() {
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() { try { if (voiceSr != null) voiceSr.stopListening(); } catch (Exception ignored) {} }
        });
    }

    private void voiceStart(final String reqId, final String lang, final int attempt) {
        try {
            if (!hasPermission("android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO")) {
                if (attempt == 0) requestPermissions(new String[]{ "android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO" }, 1011);
                if (attempt >= 30) { voiceEvt(reqId, "error", "permission"); return; }
                new android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(new Runnable() {
                    @Override public void run() { voiceStart(reqId, lang, attempt + 1); }
                }, 1000);
                return;
            }
            if (!android.speech.SpeechRecognizer.isRecognitionAvailable(getApplicationContext())) { voiceEvt(reqId, "error", "unavailable"); return; }
            voiceKill();
            voiceSr = android.speech.SpeechRecognizer.createSpeechRecognizer(getApplicationContext());
            voiceSr.setRecognitionListener(new android.speech.RecognitionListener() {
                @Override public void onReadyForSpeech(android.os.Bundle b) { voiceEvt(reqId, "ready", ""); }
                @Override public void onBeginningOfSpeech() { voiceEvt(reqId, "begin", ""); }
                @Override public void onRmsChanged(float rms) {
                    long n = System.currentTimeMillis();
                    if (n - voiceLastLevel > 70) { voiceLastLevel = n; voiceEvt(reqId, "level", String.valueOf(rms)); }
                }
                @Override public void onBufferReceived(byte[] buf) {}
                @Override public void onEndOfSpeech() { voiceEvt(reqId, "end", ""); }
                @Override public void onError(int code) { voiceEvt(reqId, "error", String.valueOf(code)); voiceKill(); }
                @Override public void onResults(android.os.Bundle b) {
                    java.util.ArrayList<String> r = b.getStringArrayList(android.speech.SpeechRecognizer.RESULTS_RECOGNITION);
                    voiceEvt(reqId, "final", (r != null && r.size() > 0) ? r.get(0) : "");
                    voiceKill();
                }
                @Override public void onPartialResults(android.os.Bundle b) {
                    java.util.ArrayList<String> r = b.getStringArrayList(android.speech.SpeechRecognizer.RESULTS_RECOGNITION);
                    if (r != null && r.size() > 0) voiceEvt(reqId, "partial", r.get(0));
                }
                @Override public void onEvent(int t, android.os.Bundle b) {}
            });
            android.content.Intent it = new android.content.Intent(android.speech.RecognizerIntent.ACTION_RECOGNIZE_SPEECH);
            it.putExtra(android.speech.RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE_MODEL, android.speech.RecognizerIntent.LANGUAGE_MODEL_FREE_FORM);
            it.putExtra(android.speech.RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_PARTIAL_RESULTS, true);
            it.putExtra(android.speech.RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_MAX_RESULTS, 1);
            if (lang != null && lang.length() > 1) it.putExtra(android.speech.RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE, lang);
            voiceSr.startListening(it);
        } catch (Exception e) { voiceEvt(reqId, "error", "exception"); voiceKill(); }
    }

    // ================= v4.1.1: KUNCI APLIKASI (PIN tersimpan + biometrik sidik jari / wajah) =================
    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public String getLockConfig() { return aetherPrefs.getString("lock_cfg", ""); }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void setLockConfig(String json) { aetherPrefs.edit().putString("lock_cfg", json == null ? "" : json).apply(); }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public String biometricStatus() {
        try {
            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 28) return "UNSUPPORTED";
            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 29) {
                android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricManager bm = (android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricManager) getSystemService(android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricManager.class);
                if (bm == null) return "UNSUPPORTED";
                int r = bm.canAuthenticate();
                if (r == android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricManager.BIOMETRIC_SUCCESS) return "OK";
                if (r == android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricManager.BIOMETRIC_ERROR_NONE_ENROLLED) return "NONE_ENROLLED";
                return "NO_HARDWARE";
            }
            return "OK";
        } catch (Exception e) { return "UNSUPPORTED"; }
    }

    private void bioResult(final String reqId, final String st) {
        if (webview1 != null) {
            webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeBiometric==='function') window.onNativeBiometric('" + reqId + "', '" + st + "');", null);
        }
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void startBiometric(final String reqId, final String title, final String sub) {
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try {
                    if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 28) { bioResult(reqId, "UNSUPPORTED"); return; }
                    android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricPrompt.Builder b = new android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricPrompt.Builder(MainActivity.this);
                    b.setTitle(title);
                    b.setSubtitle(sub);
                    b.setNegativeButton("Pakai PIN", getMainExecutor(), new android.content.DialogInterface.OnClickListener() {
                        @Override public void onClick(android.content.DialogInterface d, int w) { bioResult(reqId, "CANCEL"); }
                    });
                    android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricPrompt bp = b.build();
                    bp.authenticate(new android.os.CancellationSignal(), getMainExecutor(), new android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricPrompt.AuthenticationCallback() {
                        @Override public void onAuthenticationSucceeded(android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricPrompt.AuthenticationResult r) { bioResult(reqId, "OK"); }
                        @Override public void onAuthenticationError(int code, CharSequence msg) { bioResult(reqId, code == 10 || code == 13 ? "CANCEL" : "ERROR"); }
                        @Override public void onAuthenticationFailed() {}
                    });
                } catch (Exception e) { bioResult(reqId, "ERROR"); }
            }
        });
    }

    // ================= v4.1.1: PRIVASI =================
    private boolean privKeyOk(String k) {
        return "priv_secure".equals(k) || "priv_recents".equals(k) || "priv_notif_private".equals(k) || "priv_incognito".equals(k)
            || "priv_no_loc".equals(k) || "priv_anon_name".equals(k) || "priv_nohist".equals(k) || "priv_clear_exit".equals(k);
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public boolean getPrivacyFlag(String key) { return privKeyOk(key) && aetherPrefs.getBoolean(key, false); }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void setPrivacyFlag(final String key, final boolean v) {
        if (!privKeyOk(key)) return;
        aetherPrefs.edit().putBoolean(key, v).apply();
        if ("priv_secure".equals(key)) {
            runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
                @Override public void run() {
                    try {
                        if (v) getWindow().addFlags(android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE);
                        else getWindow().clearFlags(android.view.WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE);
                    } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                }
            });
        }
    }

    // ================= v4.1.1: PENYIMPANAN UNDUHAN =================
    private long dirSizeBytes(java.io.File d) {
        long t = 0L;
        try {
            java.io.File[] fs = d.listFiles();
            if (fs != null) for (java.io.File f : fs) { if (f.isFile()) t += f.length(); }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        return t;
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public String getStorageInfo() {
        try {
            org.json.JSONObject o = new org.json.JSONObject();
            org.json.JSONArray arr = new org.json.JSONArray();
            long used = 0L; int count = 0;
            java.io.File[] fs = offlineDir.listFiles();
            if (fs != null) {
                for (java.io.File f : fs) {
                    if (f.isFile() && f.getName().endsWith(".m4a")) {
                        used += f.length(); count++;
                        org.json.JSONObject it = new org.json.JSONObject();
                        it.put("id", f.getName().replace(".m4a", ""));
                        it.put("size", f.length());
                        it.put("mod", f.lastModified());
                        arr.put(it);
                    }
                }
            }
            o.put("used", used); o.put("count", count); o.put("files", arr);
            o.put("limitMb", aetherPrefs.getInt("store_limit_mb", 1024));
            o.put("publicCopy", aetherPrefs.getBoolean("store_public", true));
            try {
                android.os.StatFs sf = new android.os.StatFs(getFilesDir().getPath());
                o.put("free", sf.getAvailableBytes()); o.put("total", sf.getTotalBytes());
            } catch (Exception e2) { o.put("free", 0); o.put("total", 0); }
            return o.toString();
        } catch (Exception e) { return "{}"; }
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void setStorageLimit(int mb) { aetherPrefs.edit().putInt("store_limit_mb", Math.max(0, mb)).apply(); }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void setPublicCopy(boolean on) { aetherPrefs.edit().putBoolean("store_public", on).apply(); }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public boolean deleteOfflineFile(String id) {
        try {
            if (id == null || !id.matches("[A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,20}")) return false;
            java.io.File f = new java.io.File(offlineDir, id + ".m4a");
            return f.exists() && f.delete();
        } catch (Exception e) { return false; }
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public int deleteAllOffline() {
        int n = 0;
        try {
            java.io.File[] fs = offlineDir.listFiles();
            if (fs != null) for (java.io.File f : fs) { if (f.isFile() && f.delete()) n++; }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        return n;
    }

    private void copyToPublicMusic(java.io.File src, String baseName) {
        try {
            String clean = baseName.replaceAll("[\\\\/:*?\"<>|]", "_") + ".m4a";
            byte[] buf = new byte[32768]; int n;
            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 29) {
                android.content.ContentValues cv = new android.content.ContentValues();
                cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, clean);
                cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.MIME_TYPE, "audio/mp4");
                cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.RELATIVE_PATH, "Music/AETHER");
                cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 1);
                android.net.Uri uri = getContentResolver().insert(android.provider.MediaStore.Audio.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, cv);
                if (uri != null) {
                    java.io.OutputStream os = getContentResolver().openOutputStream(uri);
                    java.io.FileInputStream fis = new java.io.FileInputStream(src);
                    while ((n = fis.read(buf)) != -1) os.write(buf, 0, n);
                    os.flush(); os.close(); fis.close();
                    cv.clear(); cv.put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 0);
                    getContentResolver().update(uri, cv, null, null);
                }
            } else {
                java.io.File pubDir = new java.io.File(android.os.Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(android.os.Environment.DIRECTORY_MUSIC), "AETHER");
                if (!pubDir.exists()) pubDir.mkdirs();
                java.io.FileInputStream fis = new java.io.FileInputStream(src);
                java.io.FileOutputStream pfos = new java.io.FileOutputStream(new java.io.File(pubDir, clean));
                while ((n = fis.read(buf)) != -1) pfos.write(buf, 0, n);
                pfos.close(); fis.close();
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void clearNativeCache() {
        try {
            if (offlineDir.exists() && offlineDir.isDirectory()) {
                java.io.File[] files = offlineDir.listFiles();
                if (files != null) {
                    for (java.io.File f : files) { f.delete(); }
                }
            }
            java.io.File cacheDir = getApplicationContext().getCacheDir();
            if (cacheDir != null && cacheDir.isDirectory()) {
                java.io.File[] cFiles = cacheDir.listFiles();
                if (cFiles != null) {
                    for (java.io.File cf : cFiles) { cf.delete(); }
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try { if (webview1 != null) webview1.clearCache(true); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        });
    }

        } catch(Exception ignored){}
    }

    private String[] resolveBestAudioStream(String videoId) {
        String[][] clients = new String[][] {
            {
                "{\"context\":{\"client\":{\"clientName\":\"ANDROID_VR\",\"clientVersion\":\"1.60.19\",\"androidSdkVersion\":34,\"osName\":\"Android\",\"osVersion\":\"14\",\"hl\":\"id\",\"gl\":\"ID\"}},\"videoId\":\"" + videoId + "\",\"contentCheckOk\":true,\"racyCheckOk\":true}",
                "com.google.android.apps.youtube.vr.oculus/1.60.19 (Linux; U; Android 14; en_US; Quest 3; Build/UP1A.231005.007.A1; Cronet/132.0.6808.3)"
            },
            {
                "{\"context\":{\"client\":{\"clientName\":\"IOS\",\"clientVersion\":\"19.45.4\",\"deviceMake\":\"Apple\",\"deviceModel\":\"iPhone16,2\",\"osName\":\"iPhone\",\"osVersion\":\"18.1.0.22B83\",\"hl\":\"id\",\"gl\":\"ID\"}},\"videoId\":\"" + videoId + "\",\"contentCheckOk\":true,\"racyCheckOk\":true}",
                "com.google.ios.youtube/19.45.4 (iPhone16,2; U; CPU iOS 18_1_0 like Mac OS X;)"
            },
            {
                "{\"context\":{\"client\":{\"clientName\":\"ANDROID_TESTSUITE\",\"clientVersion\":\"1.9\",\"androidSdkVersion\":30,\"osName\":\"Android\",\"osVersion\":\"11\",\"hl\":\"id\",\"gl\":\"ID\"}},\"videoId\":\"" + videoId + "\",\"contentCheckOk\":true,\"racyCheckOk\":true}",
                "com.google.android.youtube/1.9 (Linux; U; Android 11; en_US)"
            },
            {
                "{\"context\":{\"client\":{\"clientName\":\"ANDROID\",\"clientVersion\":\"19.44.38\",\"androidSdkVersion\":34,\"osName\":\"Android\",\"osVersion\":\"14\",\"hl\":\"id\",\"gl\":\"ID\"}},\"videoId\":\"" + videoId + "\",\"contentCheckOk\":true,\"racyCheckOk\":true}",
                "com.google.android.youtube/19.44.38 (Linux; U; Android 14; en_US) gzip"
            }
        };

        for (int c = 0; c < clients.length; c++) {
            try {
                java.net.HttpURLConnection conn = (java.net.HttpURLConnection) new java.net.URL("https://www.youtube.com/youtubei/v1/player?prettyPrint=false").openConnection();
                conn.setRequestMethod("POST");
                conn.setConnectTimeout(5000); conn.setReadTimeout(5000);
                conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json");
                conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", clients[c][1]);
                conn.setDoOutput(true);
                java.io.OutputStream os = conn.getOutputStream();
                os.write(clients[c][0].getBytes("UTF-8")); os.close();
                if (conn.getResponseCode() == 200) {
                    java.io.InputStream is = conn.getInputStream();
                    java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
                    byte[] buf = new byte[4096]; int n;
                    while ((n = is.read(buf)) != -1) baos.write(buf, 0, n);
                    is.close();
                    org.json.JSONObject root = new org.json.JSONObject(new String(baos.toByteArray(), "UTF-8"));
                    org.json.JSONObject sd = root.optJSONObject("streamingData");
                    if (sd != null) {
                        String bestUrl = null;
                        org.json.JSONArray af = sd.optJSONArray("adaptiveFormats");
                        if (af != null) {
                            for (int i = 0; i < af.length(); i++) {
                                org.json.JSONObject fmt = af.getJSONObject(i);
                                String mime = fmt.optString("mimeType", "");
                                String u = fmt.optString("url", "");
                                if (!u.isEmpty() && mime.startsWith("audio/mp4")) { bestUrl = u; break; }
                                if (!u.isEmpty() && mime.startsWith("audio/") && bestUrl == null) { bestUrl = u; }
                            }
                        }
                        if (bestUrl == null) {
                            org.json.JSONArray fmts = sd.optJSONArray("formats");
                            if (fmts != null && fmts.length() > 0) { bestUrl = fmts.getJSONObject(0).optString("url", null); }
                        }
                        if (bestUrl != null && !bestUrl.isEmpty()) { return new String[]{ bestUrl, clients[c][1] }; }
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }

        String[] pipedApis = new String[] { "https://pipedapi.kavin.rocks/streams/", "https://api.piped.projectsegfau.lt/streams/", "https://pipedapi.smnz.de/streams/", "https://pipedapi.adminforge.de/streams/", "https://piped-api.garudalinux.org/streams/" };
        for (String api : pipedApis) {
            try {
                java.net.HttpURLConnection conn = (java.net.HttpURLConnection) new java.net.URL(api + videoId).openConnection();
                conn.setConnectTimeout(4500); conn.setReadTimeout(4500);
                conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0");
                if (conn.getResponseCode() == 200) {
                    java.io.InputStream is = conn.getInputStream(); java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
                    byte[] buf = new byte[4096]; int n;
                    while ((n = is.read(buf)) != -1) baos.write(buf, 0, n); is.close();
                    org.json.JSONObject d = new org.json.JSONObject(new String(baos.toByteArray(), "UTF-8"));
                    org.json.JSONArray arr = d.optJSONArray("audioStreams");
                    if (arr != null && arr.length() > 0) {
                        String u = null;
                        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length(); i++) {
                            org.json.JSONObject o = arr.getJSONObject(i);
                            if (o.optString("mimeType", "").contains("mp4")) { u = o.optString("url", null); break; }
                        }
                        if (u == null) u = arr.getJSONObject(0).optString("url", null);
                        if (u != null && !u.isEmpty()) return new String[]{ u, "Mozilla/5.0" };
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        return null;
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void extractDirectAudio(final String videoId) {
        new Thread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {

                final String finalStream = (res != null && res[0] != null) ? res[0] : "";
                runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
                    @Override public void run() {
                        if (webview1 != null) {
                            String safeUrl = finalStream.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("'", "\\'");
                            webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeStreamResult==='function') window.onNativeStreamResult('" + videoId + "', '" + safeUrl + "');", null);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        }).start();
    }

    // ================= v4.3: DOWNLOADER BARU =================
    // Banyak sumber audio (7 klien YouTube + Piped + Invidious), unduh per potongan (Range) agar tidak ditolak,
    // dan alasan gagal yang jelas (bukan lagi selalu "periksa koneksi internet").
    private boolean isOnlineNative() {
        try {
            android.net.ConnectivityManager cm = (android.net.ConnectivityManager) getSystemService(android.content.Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE);
            if (cm == null) return true;
            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23) {
                android.net.Network n = cm.getActiveNetwork();
                if (n == null) return false;
                android.net.NetworkCapabilities nc = cm.getNetworkCapabilities(n);
                return nc != null && nc.hasCapability(android.net.NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET);
            }
            android.net.NetworkInfo ni = cm.getActiveNetworkInfo();
            return ni != null && ni.isConnected();
        } catch (Exception e) { return true; }
    }

    private String httpBody(String u, String ua, String postJson, int timeoutMs) {
        try {
            java.net.HttpURLConnection conn = (java.net.HttpURLConnection) new java.net.URL(u).openConnection();
            conn.setConnectTimeout(timeoutMs); conn.setReadTimeout(timeoutMs);
            conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", ua);
            if (postJson != null) {
                conn.setRequestMethod("POST");
                conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json");
                conn.setDoOutput(true);
                java.io.OutputStream os = conn.getOutputStream();
                os.write(postJson.getBytes("UTF-8")); os.close();
            }
            if (conn.getResponseCode() != 200) return null;
            java.io.InputStream is = conn.getInputStream();
            java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
            byte[] buf = new byte[8192]; int n; int total = 0;
            while ((n = is.read(buf)) != -1 && total < 4194304) { baos.write(buf, 0, n); total += n; }
            is.close();
            return new String(baos.toByteArray(), "UTF-8");
        } catch (Exception e) { return null; }
    }

    private String ytBody(String name, String ver, String extra, String third, String vid) {
        return "{\"context\":{\"client\":{\"clientName\":\"" + name + "\",\"clientVersion\":\"" + ver + "\"" + extra + ",\"hl\":\"id\",\"gl\":\"ID\"}" + third + "},\"videoId\":\"" + vid + "\",\"contentCheckOk\":true,\"racyCheckOk\":true}";
    }

    private String[] candidateAt(String vid, int idx) {
        String[][] C = new String[][] {
            { "ANDROID_VR", "1.60.19", ",\"androidSdkVersion\":34,\"osName\":\"Android\",\"osVersion\":\"14\"", "", "com.google.android.apps.youtube.vr.oculus/1.60.19 (Linux; U; Android 14; en_US; Quest 3; Build/UP1A.231005.007.A1; Cronet/132.0.6808.3)" },
            { "IOS", "19.45.4", ",\"deviceMake\":\"Apple\",\"deviceModel\":\"iPhone16,2\",\"osName\":\"iPhone\",\"osVersion\":\"18.1.0.22B83\"", "", "com.google.ios.youtube/19.45.4 (iPhone16,2; U; CPU iOS 18_1_0 like Mac OS X;)" },
            { "ANDROID_TESTSUITE", "1.9", ",\"androidSdkVersion\":30,\"osName\":\"Android\",\"osVersion\":\"11\"", "", "com.google.android.youtube/1.9 (Linux; U; Android 11; en_US)" },
            { "ANDROID", "19.44.38", ",\"androidSdkVersion\":34,\"osName\":\"Android\",\"osVersion\":\"14\"", "", "com.google.android.youtube/19.44.38 (Linux; U; Android 14; en_US) gzip" },
            { "TVHTML5_SIMPLY_EMBEDDED_PLAYER", "2.0", "", ",\"thirdParty\":{\"embedUrl\":\"https://www.youtube.com/\"}", "Mozilla/5.0 (PlayStation; PlayStation 4/12.00) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 (KHTML, like Gecko) Version/15.4 Safari/605.1.15" },
            { "ANDROID_MUSIC", "7.27.52", ",\"androidSdkVersion\":34,\"osName\":\"Android\",\"osVersion\":\"14\"", "", "com.google.android.apps.youtube.music/7.27.52 (Linux; U; Android 14; en_US) gzip" },
            { "MWEB", "2.20241202.07.00", ",\"osName\":\"Android\"", "", "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 14; Pixel 8) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0.0.0 Mobile Safari/537.36" }
        };
        try {
            if (idx < C.length) {
                String[] c = C[idx];
                String body = httpBody("https://www.youtube.com/youtubei/v1/player?prettyPrint=false", c[4], ytBody(c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3], vid), 6000);
                if (body == null) return null;
                org.json.JSONObject sd = new org.json.JSONObject(body).optJSONObject("streamingData");
                if (sd == null) return null;
                String best = null;
                org.json.JSONArray af = sd.optJSONArray("adaptiveFormats");
                if (af != null) {
                    for (int i = 0; i < af.length(); i++) {
                        org.json.JSONObject f = af.getJSONObject(i);
                        String mime = f.optString("mimeType", ""), u = f.optString("url", "");
                        if (!u.isEmpty() && mime.startsWith("audio/mp4")) { best = u; break; }
                    }
                }
                if (best == null) {
                    org.json.JSONArray fm = sd.optJSONArray("formats");
                    if (fm != null && fm.length() > 0) { String u = fm.getJSONObject(0).optString("url", ""); if (!u.isEmpty()) best = u; }
                }
                return best == null ? null : new String[]{ best, c[4] };
            }
            String[] piped = new String[] { "https://pipedapi.kavin.rocks/streams/", "https://api.piped.private.coffee/streams/", "https://pipedapi.leptons.xyz/streams/", "https://pipedapi.nosebs.ru/streams/", "https://pipedapi.adminforge.de/streams/" };
            int pi = idx - C.length;
            if (pi < piped.length) {
                String body = httpBody(piped[pi] + vid, "Mozilla/5.0", null, 6000);
                if (body == null) return null;
                org.json.JSONArray arr = new org.json.JSONObject(body).optJSONArray("audioStreams");
                if (arr == null) return null;
                String u = null;
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.length(); i++) {
                    org.json.JSONObject o = arr.getJSONObject(i);
                    if (o.optString("mimeType", "").contains("mp4") && !o.optString("url", "").isEmpty()) { u = o.optString("url"); break; }
                }
                if (u == null && arr.length() > 0) u = arr.getJSONObject(0).optString("url", "");
                return (u == null || u.isEmpty()) ? null : new String[]{ u, "Mozilla/5.0" };
            }
            String[] inv = new String[] { "https://inv.nadeko.net", "https://yewtu.be", "https://invidious.nerdvpn.de", "https://invidious.privacyredirect.com" };
            int ii = pi - piped.length;
            if (ii < inv.length) {
                String body = httpBody(inv[ii] + "/api/v1/videos/" + vid + "?fields=adaptiveFormats", "Mozilla/5.0", null, 7000);
                if (body == null) return null;
                org.json.JSONArray arr = new org.json.JSONObject(body).optJSONArray("adaptiveFormats");
                if (arr == null) return null;
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.length(); i++) {
                    org.json.JSONObject o = arr.getJSONObject(i);
                    if (o.optString("type", "").startsWith("audio/mp4") && !o.optString("url", "").isEmpty()) return new String[]{ o.optString("url"), "Mozilla/5.0" };
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        return null;
    }

    private void dlProgress(final String vid, final int pct) {
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                if (webview1 != null) webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeDownloadProgress==='function') window.onNativeDownloadProgress('" + vid + "', " + pct + ");", null);
            }
        });
    }

    private String downloadChunked(String url, String ua, java.io.File outFile, String vid, boolean report) {
        java.io.FileOutputStream fos = null;
        try {
            long pos = 0L, total = -1L;
            final long CH = 1048576L;
            int lastPct = -1;
            fos = new java.io.FileOutputStream(outFile);
            byte[] buf = new byte[32768];
            for (int guard = 0; guard < 400; guard++) {
                long end = pos + CH - 1L;
                if (total > 0 && end > total - 1L) end = total - 1L;
                java.net.HttpURLConnection conn = (java.net.HttpURLConnection) new java.net.URL(url).openConnection();
                conn.setConnectTimeout(15000); conn.setReadTimeout(25000);
                conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", ua);
                conn.setRequestProperty("Accept", "*/*");
                conn.setRequestProperty("Range", "bytes=" + pos + "-" + end);
                conn.setRequestProperty("Referer", "https://www.youtube.com/");
                int code = conn.getResponseCode();
                if (code == 416 && pos > 0L) break;
                if (code != 200 && code != 206) return "HTTP_" + code;
                String cr = conn.getHeaderField("Content-Range");
                if (cr != null && cr.indexOf('/') > 0) {
                    try { total = Long.parseLong(cr.substring(cr.indexOf('/') + 1).trim()); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                } else if (code == 200) {
                    total = conn.getContentLength();
                }
                java.io.InputStream is = conn.getInputStream();
                int n; long got = 0L;
                while ((n = is.read(buf)) != -1) { fos.write(buf, 0, n); got += n; }
                is.close();
                pos += got;
                if (report && total > 0) {
                    int pct = (int) Math.min(99L, (pos * 100L) / total);
                    if (pct != lastPct && pct % 5 == 0) { lastPct = pct; dlProgress(vid, pct); }
                }
                if (code == 200) break;
                if (got == 0L) break;
                if (total > 0 && pos >= total) break;
            }
            fos.flush(); fos.close(); fos = null;
            return outFile.length() > 25000L ? "OK" : "TOO_SMALL";
        } catch (java.net.UnknownHostException e) { return "NO_NET";
        } catch (java.net.SocketTimeoutException e) { return "TIMEOUT";
        } catch (javax.net.ssl.SSLException e) { return "SSL";
        } catch (java.io.IOException e) { return "IO";
        } catch (Exception e) { return "IO";
        } finally { try { if (fos != null) fos.close(); } catch (Exception ignored) {} }
    }

    private void performDownload(final String videoId, final String title, final String artist, final String thumb, final boolean showToast) {
        try {
            if (aetherPrefs.getBoolean("priv_incognito", false) && !showToast) return;
            int limMb = aetherPrefs.getInt("store_limit_mb", 1024);
            java.io.File chk = new java.io.File(offlineDir, videoId + ".m4a");
            if (limMb > 0 && !chk.exists() && dirSizeBytes(offlineDir) >= (long) limMb * 1048576L) {
                if (showToast) {
                    runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
                        @Override public void run() {
                            if (webview1 != null) webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeStorageFull==='function') window.onNativeStorageFull();", null);
                        }
                    });
                }
                return;
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        boolean ok = false;
        String reason = "NO_STREAM";
        try {
            java.io.File outFile = new java.io.File(offlineDir, videoId + ".m4a");
            if (outFile.exists() && outFile.length() > 25000L) {
                ok = true;
            } else {
                if (outFile.exists()) outFile.delete();
                if (!isOnlineNative()) {
                    reason = "NO_NET";
                } else {
                    long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + (showToast ? 75000L : 30000L);
                    for (int idx = 0; idx < 16 && !ok && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline; idx++) {
                        String[] cand = candidateAt(videoId, idx);
                        if (cand == null) continue;
                        String r = downloadChunked(cand[0], cand[1], outFile, videoId, showToast);
                        if ("OK".equals(r)) { ok = true; }
                        else { reason = r; try { if (outFile.exists()) outFile.delete(); } catch (Exception ignored) {} }
                    }
                }
                if (ok && showToast && aetherPrefs.getBoolean("store_public", true)) {
                    copyToPublicMusic(outFile, artist + " - " + title);
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) { reason = "IO"; }

        final boolean success = ok;
        final String fReason = reason;
        runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                if (webview1 != null) {
                    String st = title.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("'", "\\'");
                    String sa = artist.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("'", "\\'");
                    String sl = thumb.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("'", "\\'");
                    if (showToast) {
                        if (!success) webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeDownloadError==='function') window.onNativeDownloadError('" + videoId + "', '" + fReason + "');", null);
                        webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeDownloadComplete==='function') window.onNativeDownloadComplete('" + videoId + "', '" + st + "', '" + sa + "', '" + sl + "', " + success + ");", null);
                    } else if (success) {
                        webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeAutoCacheComplete==='function') window.onNativeAutoCacheComplete('" + videoId + "', '" + st + "', '" + sa + "', '" + sl + "');", null);
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void nativeSearch(final String query, final boolean isHome, final String reqId) {
        new Thread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                org.json.JSONArray results = new org.json.JSONArray();
                try {
                    String qFixed = isHome ? (query + " Official Audio") : query;
                    String payload = "{\"context\":{\"client\":{\"clientName\":\"WEB\",\"clientVersion\":\"2.20241024.01.00\",\"hl\":\"id\",\"gl\":\"ID\"}},\"query\":\"" + qFixed.replace("\"", "\\\"") + "\"}";
                    java.net.HttpURLConnection conn = (java.net.HttpURLConnection) new java.net.URL("https://www.youtube.com/youtubei/v1/search?prettyPrint=false").openConnection();
                    conn.setRequestMethod("POST"); conn.setConnectTimeout(5000); conn.setReadTimeout(5000);
                    conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json");
                    conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0.0.0 Safari/537.36");
                    conn.setDoOutput(true); java.io.OutputStream os = conn.getOutputStream();
                    os.write(payload.getBytes("UTF-8")); os.close();

                    if (conn.getResponseCode() == 200) {
                        java.io.InputStream is = conn.getInputStream(); java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
                        byte[] buf = new byte[4096]; int n;
                        while ((n = is.read(buf)) != -1) baos.write(buf, 0, n); is.close();
                        org.json.JSONObject root = new org.json.JSONObject(new String(baos.toByteArray(), "UTF-8"));
                        extractVideoRenderers(root, results, new java.util.HashSet<String>(), isHome);
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}

                final String jsonOut = results.toString();
                runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
                    @Override public void run() {
                        if (webview1 != null) {
                            String safeJson = jsonOut.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("'", "\\'");
                            webview1.evaluateJavascript("if(typeof window.onNativeSearchCallback==='function') window.onNativeSearchCallback('" + reqId + "', '" + safeJson + "');", null);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        }).start();
    }
    
    private void extractVideoRenderers(Object node, org.json.JSONArray out, java.util.Set<String> seen, boolean isHome) {
        if (node == null || out.length() >= 22) return;
        try {
            if (node instanceof org.json.JSONObject) {
                org.json.JSONObject obj = (org.json.JSONObject) node;
                if (obj.has("videoRenderer")) {
                    org.json.JSONObject vr = obj.optJSONObject("videoRenderer");
                    if (vr != null) {
                        String vid = vr.optString("videoId", "");
                        if (vid.length() == 11 && !seen.contains(vid)) {
                            String tit = "";
                            org.json.JSONObject tObj = vr.optJSONObject("title");
                            if (tObj != null) {
                                org.json.JSONArray runs = tObj.optJSONArray("runs");
                                if (runs != null && runs.length() > 0) tit = runs.getJSONObject(0).optString("text", "");
                                if (tit.isEmpty()) tit = tObj.optString("simpleText", "");
                            }
                            String art = "AETHER Music";
                            org.json.JSONObject bObj = vr.optJSONObject("longBylineText");
                            if (bObj == null) bObj = vr.optJSONObject("ownerText");
                            if (bObj == null) bObj = vr.optJSONObject("shortBylineText");
                            if (bObj != null) {
                                org.json.JSONArray runs = bObj.optJSONArray("runs");
                                if (runs != null && runs.length() > 0) art = runs.getJSONObject(0).optString("text", "AETHER");
                            }
                            art = art.replace(" - Topic", "").replace("VEVO", "").replace("Official", "").trim();

                            String lenTxt = ""; org.json.JSONObject lObj = vr.optJSONObject("lengthText");
                            if (lObj != null) lenTxt = lObj.optString("simpleText", "");
                            int sec = 210;
                            if (!lenTxt.isEmpty()) {
                                String[] p = lenTxt.split(":");
                                try {
                                    if (p.length == 2) sec = Integer.parseInt(p[0].trim()) * 60 + Integer.parseInt(p[1].trim());
                                    else if (p.length == 3) sec = Integer.parseInt(p[0].trim()) * 3600 + Integer.parseInt(p[1].trim()) * 60 + Integer.parseInt(p[2].trim());
                                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                            }

                            if (!lenTxt.isEmpty() && sec >= 45 && sec <= 600 && !tit.isEmpty()) {
                                boolean skip = false;
                                if (isHome) {
                                    String low = (tit + " " + art).toLowerCase();
                                    if (low.contains("cover") || low.contains("karaoke") || low.contains("koplo") || low.contains("slowed") || low.contains("reverb") || low.contains("full album")) {
                                        skip = true;
                                    }
                                }
                                if (!skip) {
                                    seen.add(vid);
                                    String cleanRaw = tit.replaceAll("(?i)\\(Official.*?\\)|\\[Official.*?\\]|Official Music Video|Official Video|Official Audio|Official Lyric Video|Lyric Video|Lyrics|Visualizer", "").trim();
                                    if (cleanRaw.contains(" - ")) {
                                        String[] sp = cleanRaw.split(" - ", 2);
                                        art = sp[0].trim(); cleanRaw = sp[1].trim();
                                    }
                                    if (cleanRaw.isEmpty()) cleanRaw = tit;
                                    org.json.JSONObject item = new org.json.JSONObject();
                                    item.put("id", vid);
                                    item.put("t", cleanRaw.length() > 42 ? cleanRaw.substring(0, 42) + "..." : cleanRaw);
                                    item.put("a", art.length() > 35 ? art.substring(0, 35) + "..." : art);
                                    item.put("l", "https://i.ytimg.com/vi/" + vid + "/hqdefault.jpg");
                                    out.put(item);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                java.util.Iterator<String> keys = obj.keys();
                while (keys.hasNext() && out.length() < 22) { String k = keys.next(); extractVideoRenderers(obj.opt(k), out, seen, isHome); }
            } else if (node instanceof org.json.JSONArray) {
                org.json.JSONArray arr = (org.json.JSONArray) node;
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.length() && out.length() < 22; i++) { extractVideoRenderers(arr.opt(i), out, seen, isHome); }
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
    public void showNotification(final String title, final String artist, final String logo, final String state) {
        new Thread(new Runnable() {
            @Override public void run() {
                try {
                    if (logo != null && !logo.isEmpty() && !logo.equals(cachedLogoUrl)) {
                        java.net.HttpURLConnection conn = (java.net.HttpURLConnection) new java.net.URL(logo).openConnection();
                        conn.setConnectTimeout(3500); conn.connect();
                        cachedArt = android.graphics.BitmapFactory.decodeStream(conn.getInputStream());
                        cachedLogoUrl = logo;
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}

                runOnUiThread(new Runnable() {
                    @Override public void run() {
                        try {
                            boolean isPlaying = "PLAYING".equals(state);
                            island.update(title, artist, cachedArt, isPlaying);
                            if (isPlaying) protectTaskFromSwipe.run();

                            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 21 && aetherMediaSession != null) {
                                aetherMediaSession.setPlaybackState(new android.media.session.PlaybackState.Builder().setActions(android.media.session.PlaybackState.ACTION_PLAY | android.media.session.PlaybackState.ACTION_PAUSE | android.media.session.PlaybackState.ACTION_SKIP_TO_NEXT | android.media.session.PlaybackState.ACTION_SKIP_TO_PREVIOUS).setState(isPlaying ? android.media.session.PlaybackState.STATE_PLAYING : android.media.session.PlaybackState.STATE_PAUSED, 0, 1.0f).build());
                                android.media.MediaMetadata.Builder metaBuilder = new android.media.MediaMetadata.Builder().putString(android.media.MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_TITLE, title).putString(android.media.MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_ARTIST, artist);
                                if (cachedArt != null) metaBuilder.putBitmap(android.media.MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_ALBUM_ART, cachedArt);
                                aetherMediaSession.setMetadata(metaBuilder.build());
                            }

                            android.app.NotificationManager nm = (android.app.NotificationManager) getSystemService(android.content.Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
                            String channelId = "aether_liquid_ctrl_v9";

                            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
                                android.app.NotificationChannel channel = new android.app.NotificationChannel(channelId, "AETHER Music", android.app.NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW);
                                channel.setShowBadge(false); nm.createNotificationChannel(channel);
                            }

                            int pFlags = android.app.PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 23 ? android.app.PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE : 0);
                            android.app.PendingIntent piPrev = android.app.PendingIntent.getBroadcast(getApplicationContext(), 201, new android.content.Intent("com.aether.ACTION_PREV").setPackage(getPackageName()), pFlags);
                            android.app.PendingIntent piToggle = android.app.PendingIntent.getBroadcast(getApplicationContext(), 202, new android.content.Intent("com.aether.ACTION_TOGGLE").setPackage(getPackageName()), pFlags);
                            android.app.PendingIntent piNext = android.app.PendingIntent.getBroadcast(getApplicationContext(), 203, new android.content.Intent("com.aether.ACTION_NEXT").setPackage(getPackageName()), pFlags);
                            
                            android.content.Intent openIntent = new android.content.Intent(getApplicationContext(), MainActivity.class);
                            openIntent.setAction(android.content.Intent.ACTION_MAIN); openIntent.addCategory(android.content.Intent.CATEGORY_LAUNCHER); openIntent.addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK | android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP);
                            android.app.PendingIntent piOpen = android.app.PendingIntent.getActivity(getApplicationContext(), 200, openIntent, pFlags);

                            android.app.Notification.Builder builder = (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) ? new android.app.Notification.Builder(getApplicationContext(), channelId) : new android.app.Notification.Builder(getApplicationContext());
                            builder.setContentTitle(title).setContentText(artist).setSubText(isPlaying ? "AETHER • Playing" : "AETHER • Paused").setSmallIcon(isPlaying ? android.R.drawable.ic_media_play : android.R.drawable.ic_media_pause).setContentIntent(piOpen).setOngoing(true).setAutoCancel(false);
                            if (cachedArt != null) builder.setLargeIcon(cachedArt);

                            if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 21) {
                                builder.setVisibility(aetherPrefs.getBoolean("priv_notif_private", false) ? android.app.Notification.VISIBILITY_PRIVATE : android.app.Notification.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC).setColor(0xFF0B1320).setCategory(android.app.Notification.CATEGORY_TRANSPORT);
                                builder.addAction(new android.app.Notification.Action.Builder(android.R.drawable.ic_media_previous, "Prev", piPrev).build());
                                builder.addAction(new android.app.Notification.Action.Builder(isPlaying ? android.R.drawable.ic_media_pause : android.R.drawable.ic_media_play, isPlaying ? "Pause" : "Play", piToggle).build());
                                builder.addAction(new android.app.Notification.Action.Builder(android.R.drawable.ic_media_next, "Next", piNext).build());
                                android.app.Notification.MediaStyle mediaStyle = new android.app.Notification.MediaStyle();
                                if (aetherMediaSession != null) mediaStyle.setMediaSession(aetherMediaSession.getSessionToken());
                                mediaStyle.setShowActionsInCompactView(0, 1, 2); builder.setStyle(mediaStyle);
                            }
                            android.app.Notification notif = builder.build();
                            notif.flags |= android.app.Notification.FLAG_NO_CLEAR | android.app.Notification.FLAG_ONGOING_EVENT;
                            nm.notify(502, notif);
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                    }
                });
            }
        }).start();
    }
}, "AndroidInterface");

+ "    function trimText(str, max) { return str.length > max ? str.substring(0, max) + '...' : str; }\n"
+ "    function parseDur(iso) {\n"
+ "      if(!iso) return 240;\n"
+ "      let m = iso.match(/PT(?:(\\d+)H)?(?:(\\d+)M)?(?:(\\d+)S)?/);\n"
+ "      if(!m) return 240;\n"
+ "      return (parseInt(m[1]||0)*3600) + (parseInt(m[2]||0)*60) + parseInt(m[3]||0);\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    window._searchCallbacks = {};\n"
+ "    window.onNativeSearchCallback = function(reqId, jsonStr) {\n"
+ "      if(window._searchCallbacks[reqId]) {\n"
+ "        try { window._searchCallbacks[reqId](JSON.parse(jsonStr)); } catch(e) { window._searchCallbacks[reqId]([]); }\n"
+ "        delete window._searchCallbacks[reqId];\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "    function callNativeSearchFallback(qry, isHome) {\n"
+ "      return new Promise(resolve => {\n"
+ "        if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.nativeSearch === 'function') {\n"
+ "          let reqId = 'req_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7);\n"
+ "          window._searchCallbacks[reqId] = resolve;\n"
+ "          window.AndroidInterface.nativeSearch(qry, !!isHome, reqId);\n"
+ "          setTimeout(() => { if(window._searchCallbacks[reqId]) { delete window._searchCallbacks[reqId]; resolve([]); } }, 6500);\n"
+ "        } else { resolve([]); }\n"
+ "      });\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    async function search(qry, isHome=false, maxRes=20) {\n"
+ "      try {\n"
+ "        let res = await fetch(`https://www.googleapis.com/youtube/v3/search?part=snippet&type=video&videoCategoryId=10&maxResults=${maxRes}&q=${encodeURIComponent(qry)}&key=${K}`);\n"
+ "        let d = await res.json();\n"
+ "        if(d && d.items && d.items.length > 0) {\n"
+ "          let ids = d.items.map(i=>i.id.videoId).filter(Boolean).join(',');\n"
+ "          let dr = await fetch(`https://www.googleapis.com/youtube/v3/videos?part=snippet,contentDetails,status&id=${ids}&key=${K}`);\n"
+ "          let dd = await dr.json();\n"
+ "          let bannedHomeRegex = /\\b(cover|karaoke|remix|slowed|reverb|koplo|nightcore|parodi|parody|reaction|full album|nonstop|kompilasi|compilation|medley|pengamen|dj )\\b/i;\n"
+ "          let list = (dd.items || []).filter(i => {\n"
+ "            let sec = parseDur(i.contentDetails ? i.contentDetails.duration : '');\n"
+ "            if(sec < 45 || sec > 600) return false;\n"
+ "            if(isHome) { if(bannedHomeRegex.test(i.snippet.title + ' ' + i.snippet.channelTitle)) return false; }\n"
+ "            return true;\n"
+ "          }).map(i => {\n"
+ "            let raw = i.snippet.title.replace(/\\(Official.*?\\)|\\[Official.*?\\]|Official Music Video|Official Video|Official Audio|Official Lyric Video|Lyric Video|Lyrics|MV|M\\/V|Visualizer/gi, \"\").trim();\n"
+ "            let art = i.snippet.channelTitle.replace(/ - Topic|VEVO|Official/gi, \"\").trim();\n"
+ "            if(raw.includes(\" - \")) { let p = raw.split(\" - \"); art = p[0].trim(); raw = p.slice(1).join(\" - \").trim(); }\n"
+ "            raw = raw.replace(/^[\"'\\-|]+|[\"'\\-|]+$/g, '').trim();\n"
+ "            let thumb = (i.snippet.thumbnails.high || i.snippet.thumbnails.medium || i.snippet.thumbnails.default).url;\n"
+ "            return { id: i.id, t: trimText(raw || i.snippet.title, 42), a: trimText(art, 35), l: thumb };\n"
+ "          });\n"
+ "          if(list.length > 0) return list;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "      } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      return await callNativeSearchFallback(qry, isHome);\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"

+ "    async function fallbackTopicAudio() {\n"
+ "      if(!trk || altTried) { isP = false; updUI(); return; }\n"
+ "      altTried = true;\n"
+ "      try {\n"
+ "        let qStr = `${trk.a} ${trk.t} Official Audio`; let alts = await search(qStr, false, 6);\n"

+ "        if(alt) { trk.id = alt.id; useN = false; if(ytReady && ytP && ytP.loadVideoById) { ytP.loadVideoById(trk.id); ytP.unMute(); ytP.playVideo(); return; } }\n"
+ "      } catch(e) {}\n"

+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    async function onErr(e) { if(!trk || useN) return; if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.extractDirectAudio === 'function') { window.AndroidInterface.extractDirectAudio(trk.id); } else { fallbackTopicAudio(); } }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function genSimData(str) {\n"
+ "       let hash = 0;\n"
+ "       for(let i=0; i<str.length; i++) hash = str.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);\n"
+ "       let listeners = Math.abs(hash % 9000000) + 1200000;\n"
+ "       let rank = Math.abs(hash % 150) + 1;\n"
+ "       return { l: (listeners/1000000).toFixed(1) + 'M', r: rank };\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    async function play(i) {\n"
+ "      if(!q.length || i<0 || i>=q.length) return;\n"
+ "      idx = i; trk = q[i]; isP = false; useN = false; altTried = false;\n"
+ "      nAud.pause(); nAud.removeAttribute('src');\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      if(!playCounts[trk.id]) {\n"
+ "        playCounts[trk.id] = { id: trk.id, t: trk.t, a: trk.a, l: trk.l, count: 1 };\n"
+ "      } else {\n"
+ "        playCounts[trk.id].count = (playCounts[trk.id].count || 0) + 1;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      localStorage.setItem('aether_play_counts', JSON.stringify(playCounts));\n"
+ "      if(activeTab === 4) renderMostPlayed();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('pArt').src = trk.l;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('pTit').innerText = trk.t;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('pArtis').innerText = trk.a;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('mImg').src = trk.l;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('mTit').innerText = trk.t;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('mArtis').innerText = trk.a;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('dynamicBg').style.backgroundImage = `url('${trk.l}')`;\n"
+ "      \n"
+ "      document.getElementById('lyrTitle').innerText = trk.t;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('artBioImg').src = trk.l;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('artBioName').innerText = trk.a;\n"
+ "      let sim = genSimData(trk.a);\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('artListeners').innerText = sim.l;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('artRank').innerText = sim.r;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('btnFollowArt').classList.remove('following');\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('btnFollowArt').innerText = 'Ikuti';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('fullPlayer').scrollTop = 0;\n"
+ "      \n"
+ "      if(ytVidMute && typeof ytVidMute.loadVideoById === 'function') {\n"
+ "         ytVidMute.loadVideoById(trk.id); ytVidMute.mute(); ytVidMute.playVideo();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('mini').classList.remove('hidden');\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('seek').value = 0;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('miniProg').style.width = '0%';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('cTime').innerText = '0:00';\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      recents = [trk, ...recents.filter(r=>r.id!==trk.id)].slice(0, 12);\n"
+ "      localStorage.setItem('aether_rec', JSON.stringify(recents));\n"
+ "      renderRecentCarousel(recents);\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      let off = offs.find(o=>o.id===trk.id);\n"
+ "      if(off) {\n"
+ "        if(ytReady && ytP && ytP.stopVideo) ytP.stopVideo();\n"
+ "        useN = true;\n"
+ "        if(off.buf) { let b = new Blob([off.buf], {type:'audio/mp4'}); nAud.src = URL.createObjectURL(b); }\n"
+ "        else { nAud.src = `https://music.aether.app/offline_audio/${trk.id}.m4a`; }\n"
+ "        nAud.play().then(()=>{ isP = true; updUI(); }).catch(()=>{ useN = false; if(ytReady && ytP && ytP.loadVideoById) { ytP.loadVideoById(trk.id); ytP.unMute(); ytP.playVideo(); } });\n"
+ "        isP = true; updUI();\n"
+ "      } else {\n"
+ "        if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.autoCacheTrack === 'function') {\n"
+ "            window.AndroidInterface.autoCacheTrack(trk.id, trk.t, trk.a, trk.l);\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        if(ytReady && ytP && typeof ytP.loadVideoById === 'function') { ytP.loadVideoById(trk.id); ytP.unMute(); ytP.playVideo(); } else { pendingTrkId = trk.id; }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      if(window.AndroidInterface) window.AndroidInterface.showNotification(trk.t, trk.a, trk.l, 'PLAYING');\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function tog() {\n"
+ "      if(!trk) return;\n"
+ "      if(isP) {\n"
+ "         if(useN) nAud.pause(); else if(ytReady && ytP) ytP.pauseVideo(); \n"
+ "         if(ytVidMute && typeof ytVidMute.pauseVideo === 'function') ytVidMute.pauseVideo();\n"
+ "         isP = false; \n"
+ "      } else {\n"
+ "         if(useN) nAud.play(); else if(ytReady && ytP) ytP.playVideo(); \n"
+ "         if(ytVidMute && typeof ytVidMute.playVideo === 'function') ytVidMute.playVideo();\n"
+ "         isP = true; \n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      updUI();\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "    window.togglePlayFromNotif = tog; window.nextTrack = next; window.prevTrack = prev;\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function next() { if(isSkp || !q.length) return; isSkp=true; play((idx+1)%q.length); setTimeout(()=>isSkp=false, 700); }\n"
+ "    function prev() { if(isSkp || !q.length) return; isSkp=true; play((idx-1+q.length)%q.length); setTimeout(()=>isSkp=false, 700); }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function updUI() {\n"
+ "      let ic = isP ? '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-pause\"></i>' : '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-play\"></i>';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('pBtn').innerHTML = ic; document.getElementById('mBtn').innerHTML = ic;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('pArt').classList.toggle('playing-art', isP);\n"
+ "      let liked = trk && favs.some(f=>f.id===trk.id);\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('mLikeIc').className = liked ? 'fa-solid fa-circle-check' : 'fa-solid fa-circle-plus';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('mLikeIc').style.color = liked ? '#1ed760' : '#fff';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('fpLikeIc').className = liked ? 'fa-solid fa-heart' : 'fa-regular fa-heart';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('fpLikeIc').style.color = liked ? '#1ed760' : '#fff';\n"
+ "      if(window.AndroidInterface && trk) window.AndroidInterface.showNotification(trk.t, trk.a, trk.l, isP?'PLAYING':'PAUSED');\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function fmtTime(s) { return Math.floor(s/60)+':'+String(Math.floor(s%60)).padStart(2,'0'); }\n"
+ "    nAud.onended = () => { if(useN) next(); };\n"
+ "    setInterval(() => {\n"
+ "      if(!isP) return;\n"
+ "      let c=0, d=0;\n"
+ "      if(useN){ c=nAud.currentTime; d=nAud.duration; }\n"

+ "\n"
+ "    document.getElementById('seek').oninput = e => {\n"

+ "         let t = (e.target.value/100)*d; \n"
+ "         if(useN) nAud.currentTime = t; else ytP.seekTo(t, true); \n"
+ "         if(ytVidMute && typeof ytVidMute.seekTo === 'function') ytVidMute.seekTo(t, true);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "    };\n"

+ "    }\n"

+ "    function toggleLandscapeMode() {\n"
+ "      isLandscapeMode = !isLandscapeMode;\n"
+ "      try { localStorage.setItem('aether_landscape', isLandscapeMode ? '1' : '0'); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      applyLandscapeClass();\n"
+ "      if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.setLandscapeMode === 'function') {\n"
+ "        window.AndroidInterface.setLandscapeMode(isLandscapeMode);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      toast(isLandscapeMode ? 'Mode Landscape (Smart TV) Diaktifkan' : 'Mode Portrait Diaktifkan');\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"

+ "    function isRulesAccepted() {\n"
+ "      try {\n"
+ "        if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.getRulesAccepted === 'function' && window.AndroidInterface.getRulesAccepted()) return true;\n"
+ "      } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      try { return localStorage.getItem('aether_rules_ok') === '1'; } catch(e) { return false; }\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function openRules() {\n"
+ "      let acc = isRulesAccepted();\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('rulesBtnAgree').style.display = acc ? 'none' : 'flex';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('rulesBtnDecline').style.display = acc ? 'none' : 'flex';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('rulesBtnClose').style.display = acc ? 'flex' : 'none';\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('rulesBox').scrollTop = 0;\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('rulesMod').classList.add('open');\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function closeRules() {\n"
+ "      if(!isRulesAccepted()) { toast('Setujui aturan untuk melanjutkan'); return; }\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('rulesMod').classList.remove('open');\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function acceptRules() {\n"
+ "      try { localStorage.setItem('aether_rules_ok', '1'); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      try {\n"
+ "        if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.setRulesAccepted === 'function') window.AndroidInterface.setRulesAccepted(true);\n"
+ "      } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('rulesMod').classList.remove('open');\n"
+ "      toast('Terima kasih! Aturan penggunaan disetujui');\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function declineRules() {\n"
+ "      if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.closeApp === 'function') {\n"
+ "        window.AndroidInterface.closeApp();\n"
+ "      } else {\n"
+ "        toast('Aplikasi tidak dapat dipakai tanpa persetujuan aturan');\n"
+ "      }\n"
