+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // NAVIGASI REMOTE SMART TV (D-PAD): panah = pindah fokus, OK = pilih\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const DPAD_SEL = '[onclick], button, input, .rules-box, .pill, .n-item, .q-card, .mix-card, .recent-card, .track, .lib-item, .browse-card, .vibe-card, .set-row, .avatar-btn, .track-more, .create-item, .sec-more, .btn-follow';\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function dpadIsOpen(id) {\n"
+ "      let el = document.getElementById(id);\n"
+ "      return !!(el && el.classList.contains('open'));\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function dpadScopes() {\n"
+ "      if(dpadIsOpen('rulesMod')) return [document.getElementById('rulesMod')];\n"
+ "      if(dpadIsOpen('mod')) return [document.getElementById('mod')];\n"
+ "      if(dpadIsOpen('createMod')) return [document.getElementById('createMod')];\n"
+ "      if(dpadIsOpen('fullPlayer')) return [document.getElementById('fullPlayer')];\n"
+ "      let sc = [];\n"
+ "      let views = document.querySelectorAll('.view');\n"
+ "      if(views[activeTab]) sc.push(views[activeTab]);\n"
+ "      let nav = document.querySelector('.nav');\n"
+ "      if(nav) sc.push(nav);\n"
+ "      let mini = document.getElementById('mini');\n"
+ "      if(mini && !mini.classList.contains('hidden')) sc.push(mini);\n"
+ "      return sc;\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function dpadCandidates() {\n"
+ "      let out = [];\n"
+ "      let seenEl = new Set();\n"
+ "      function addEl(el) {\n"
+ "        if(seenEl.has(el) || el.disabled) return;\n"
+ "        let r = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n"
+ "        if(r.width < 4 || r.height < 4) return;\n"
+ "        let cs = window.getComputedStyle(el);\n"
+ "        if(cs.visibility === 'hidden' || cs.display === 'none') return;\n"
+ "        if(!el.hasAttribute('tabindex')) el.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');\n"
+ "        seenEl.add(el); out.push(el);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      dpadScopes().forEach(sc => {\n"
+ "        if(sc.id === 'mini') addEl(sc);\n"
+ "        sc.querySelectorAll(DPAD_SEL).forEach(addEl);\n"
+ "      });\n"
+ "      return out;\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function dpadDefault(c) {\n"
+ "      if(dpadIsOpen('rulesMod')) {\n"
+ "        return c.find(el => el.classList.contains('rules-box')) || c[0];\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      if(!dpadIsOpen('mod') && !dpadIsOpen('createMod') && dpadIsOpen('fullPlayer')) {\n"
+ "        let p = document.getElementById('pBtn');\n"
+ "        if(c.includes(p)) return p;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      return c[0];\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function dpadFocus(el) {\n"
+ "      if(!el) return;\n"
+ "      try { el.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch(e) { el.focus(); }\n"
+ "      try { el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', inline: 'nearest', behavior: 'smooth' }); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function dpadMove(dir) {\n"
+ "      let cands = dpadCandidates();\n"
+ "      if(!cands.length) return;\n"
+ "      let cur = document.activeElement;\n"
+ "      if(!cur || !cands.includes(cur)) { dpadFocus(dpadDefault(cands)); return; }\n"
+ "      let a = cur.getBoundingClientRect();\n"
+ "      let ax = a.left + a.width / 2, ay = a.top + a.height / 2;\n"
+ "      let best = null, bestScore = Infinity;\n"
+ "      cands.forEach(el => {\n"
+ "        if(el === cur) return;\n"
+ "        let b = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n"
+ "        let bx = b.left + b.width / 2, by = b.top + b.height / 2;\n"
+ "        let main, cross;\n"
+ "        if(dir === 'right') { if(bx <= ax + 2) return; main = bx - ax; cross = Math.abs(by - ay); }\n"
+ "        else if(dir === 'left') { if(bx >= ax - 2) return; main = ax - bx; cross = Math.abs(by - ay); }\n"
+ "        else if(dir === 'down') { if(by <= ay + 2) return; main = by - ay; cross = Math.abs(bx - ax); }\n"
+ "        else { if(by >= ay - 2) return; main = ay - by; cross = Math.abs(bx - ax); }\n"
+ "        let score = main + cross * 2;\n"
+ "        if(score < bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = el; }\n"
+ "      });\n"
+ "      if(best) dpadFocus(best);\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {\n"
+ "      let k = e.keyCode;\n"
+ "      let ae = document.activeElement;\n"
+ "      let isText = !!(ae && ae.tagName === 'INPUT' && ae.type !== 'range');\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      if(k === 37 || k === 38 || k === 39 || k === 40) {\n"
+ "        let dir = k === 37 ? 'left' : k === 38 ? 'up' : k === 39 ? 'right' : 'down';\n"
+ "        if(isText && (dir === 'left' || dir === 'right')) return;\n"
+ "        document.body.classList.add('dpad-active');\n"
+ "\n"
+ "        // Slider lagu: kiri/kanan = geser durasi\n"
+ "        if(ae && ae.type === 'range' && (dir === 'left' || dir === 'right')) {\n"
+ "          let v = parseFloat(ae.value) + (dir === 'right' ? 3 : -3);\n"
+ "          ae.value = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, v));\n"
+ "          ae.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));\n"
+ "          e.preventDefault(); return;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        // Kotak aturan: atas/bawah = scroll teks dulu, baru pindah fokus\n"
+ "        if(ae && ae.classList && ae.classList.contains('rules-box') && (dir === 'up' || dir === 'down')) {\n"
+ "          let atEnd = dir === 'down' ? (ae.scrollTop + ae.clientHeight >= ae.scrollHeight - 2) : (ae.scrollTop <= 0);\n"
+ "          if(!atEnd) { ae.scrollTop += (dir === 'down' ? 90 : -90); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        e.preventDefault();\n"
+ "        dpadMove(dir);\n"
+ "        return;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      if(k === 13 || k === 23) {\n"
+ "        if(isText) return;\n"
+ "        if(ae && ae !== document.body && typeof ae.click === 'function') {\n"
+ "          document.body.classList.add('dpad-active');\n"
+ "          e.preventDefault();\n"
+ "          ae.click();\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        return;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      if(k === 27) {\n"
+ "        e.preventDefault();\n"
+ "        if(typeof window.handleBackButton === 'function') window.handleBackButton();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "    }, true);\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    ['touchstart', 'mousedown'].forEach(evt => {\n"
+ "      document.addEventListener(evt, () => { document.body.classList.remove('dpad-active'); }, true);\n"
+ "    });\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // v4.1.1 - INTI: konfigurasi, privasi, izin, halaman, dropdown, skeleton\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const Cfg = (function() {\n"
+ "      const KEY = 'aether_cfg';\n"
+ "      let o = {};\n"
+ "      try { o = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || '{}') || {}; } catch(e) { o = {}; }\n"
+ "      const def = { theme: 'liquid', trans: false, transLang: 'id', sweep: true, lySize: 'm', resume: true, voiceLang: 'id-ID', lockMethod: 'off', lockDelay: 30, islandTpl: 0 };\n"
+ "      return {\n"
+ "        get: k => (o[k] === undefined ? def[k] : o[k]),\n"
+ "        set: (k, v) => { o[k] = v; try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(o)); } catch(e) {} },\n"
+ "        all: () => Object.assign({}, def, o),\n"
+ "        reload: () => { try { o = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || '{}') || {}; } catch(e) { o = {}; } }\n"
+ "      };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    const Priv = {\n"
+ "      flag(k) {\n"
+ "        try { if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.getPrivacyFlag === 'function') return !!window.AndroidInterface.getPrivacyFlag(k); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        return localStorage.getItem('aether_' + k) === '1';\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      set(k, v) {\n"
+ "        try { if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.setPrivacyFlag === 'function') window.AndroidInterface.setPrivacyFlag(k, !!v); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        try { localStorage.setItem('aether_' + k, v ? '1' : '0'); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      noHistory() { return Priv.flag('priv_incognito') || Priv.flag('priv_nohist'); }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    window.__graceUntil = 0;\n"
+ "    const Perm = {\n"
+ "      cbs: {},\n"
+ "      ask(perm) {\n"
+ "        return new Promise(res => {\n"
+ "          if(!(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.requestPermission === 'function')) { res(true); return; }\n"
+ "          try { if(window.AndroidInterface.hasPermission(perm)) { res(true); return; } } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "          const id = 'p' + Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);\n"
+ "          Perm.cbs[id] = res;\n"
+ "          window.__graceUntil = Date.now() + 45000;\n"
+ "          window.AndroidInterface.requestPermission(perm, id);\n"
+ "          setTimeout(() => { if(Perm.cbs[id]) { delete Perm.cbs[id]; res(false); } }, 40000);\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "    window.onNativePerm = function(id, ok) {\n"
+ "      const cb = Perm.cbs[id];\n"
+ "      window.__graceUntil = Date.now() + 1500;\n"
+ "      if(cb) { delete Perm.cbs[id]; cb(!!ok); }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    const Pages = (function() {\n"
+ "      const stack = [];\n"
+ "      const hooks = {};\n"
+ "      const el = id => document.getElementById(id);\n"
+ "      function open(id) {\n"
+ "        const e = el(id); if(!e) return;\n"
+ "        e.classList.add('open');\n"
+ "        const i = stack.indexOf(id); if(i >= 0) stack.splice(i, 1);\n"
+ "        stack.push(id);\n"
+ "        if(hooks[id] && hooks[id].open) { try { hooks[id].open(); } catch(x) {} }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function close(id) {\n"
+ "        const e = el(id); if(e) e.classList.remove('open');\n"
+ "        const i = stack.indexOf(id); if(i >= 0) stack.splice(i, 1);\n"
+ "        if(hooks[id] && hooks[id].close) { try { hooks[id].close(); } catch(x) {} }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function top() {\n"
+ "        while(stack.length) {\n"
+ "          const id = stack[stack.length - 1]; const e = el(id);\n"
+ "          if(e && e.classList.contains('open')) return id;\n"
+ "          stack.pop();\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        return null;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function back() {\n"
+ "        const t = top(); if(!t) return false;\n"
+ "        if(hooks[t] && hooks[t].back && hooks[t].back()) return true;\n"
+ "        close(t); return true;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      return { open: open, close: close, top: top, back: back, hooks: hooks };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    const Ask = {\n"
+ "      cb: null,\n"
+ "      open(title, ph, val, cb) {\n"

+ "        setTimeout(() => { try { i.focus(); } catch(e) {} }, 380);\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      ok() {\n"
+ "        const v = (document.getElementById('askInp').value || '').trim();\n"
+ "        const cb = Ask.cb; Ask.cb = null; Pages.close('askMod');\n"
+ "        if(cb) cb(v);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "    };\n"

+ "      rows(n) { let h = ''; for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) h += '<div class=\"sk-row\"><div class=\"sk-img\"></div><div class=\"sk-col\"><div class=\"sk-line\"></div><div class=\"sk-line s\"></div></div></div>'; return h; },\n"
+ "      cards(n) { let h = ''; for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) h += '<div class=\"sk-card\"><div class=\"sk sk-sq\"></div><div class=\"sk-line\"></div><div class=\"sk-line s\"></div></div>'; return h; },\n"
+ "      lines(n) { let h = ''; for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) h += '<div class=\"sk-line\" style=\"height:22px;margin:16px 0;width:' + (60 + (i * 17) % 38) + '%\"></div>'; return h; }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    const DD = (function() {\n"
+ "      const reg = {};\n"
+ "      const el = id => document.getElementById(id);\n"
+ "      function render(id) {\n"
+ "        const r = reg[id], e = el(id); if(!r || !e) return;\n"
+ "        const cur = r.getVal();\n"
+ "        const curOpt = r.opts.find(o => o[0] === cur) || r.opts[0];\n"
+ "        e.innerHTML = '<div class=\"dd-head\" onclick=\"DD.toggle(\\'' + id + '\\')\"><span>' + curOpt[1] + '</span><i class=\"fa-solid fa-chevron-down\"></i></div><div class=\"dd-list\">' +\n"
+ "          r.opts.map((o, i) => '<div class=\"dd-opt' + (o[0] === cur ? ' active' : '') + '\" onclick=\"DD.pick(\\'' + id + '\\',' + i + ')\">' + o[1] + '</div>').join('') + '</div>';\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function make(id, opts, getVal, onPick) { reg[id] = { opts: opts, getVal: getVal, onPick: onPick }; render(id); }\n"
+ "      function toggle(id) {\n"
+ "        const e = el(id); if(!e) return;\n"
+ "        const willOpen = !e.classList.contains('open');\n"
+ "        e.classList.toggle('open', willOpen);\n"
+ "        if(willOpen) { const a = e.closest('.acc'); if(a) { const b = a.querySelector('.acc-body'); if(b && b.style.height !== 'auto') b.style.height = 'auto'; } }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function pick(id, i) {\n"
+ "        const r = reg[id]; if(!r) return;\n"
+ "        r.onPick(r.opts[i][0]);\n"
+ "        render(id);\n"
+ "        const e = el(id); if(e) e.classList.remove('open');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      return { make: make, render: render, toggle: toggle, pick: pick };\n"
+ "    })();\n"

+ "      toggle(id) {\n"

+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // MESIN LIRIK GLOBAL (LRCLIB) + TERJEMAHAN OTOMATIS + LIRIK PENUH ALA SPOTIFY\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const LY = (function() {\n"
+ "      const S = { key: '', lines: [], plain: '', trans: [], transPlain: '', idx: -1, status: 'idle', tlang: '' };\n"
+ "      const subs = [];\n"
+ "      const cache = {};\n"
+ "      const httpCbs = {};\n"
+ "      window.onNativeHttpResult = function(reqId, ok, body) {\n"
+ "        const cb = httpCbs[reqId];\n"
+ "        if(cb) { delete httpCbs[reqId]; cb(ok ? body : null); }\n"
+ "      };\n"
+ "      function nativeHttp(url) {\n"
+ "        return new Promise(res => {\n"
+ "          if(!(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.nativeHttpGet === 'function')) { res(null); return; }\n"
+ "          const id = 'h' + Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);\n"
+ "          httpCbs[id] = txt => { try { res(txt ? JSON.parse(txt) : null); } catch(e) { res(null); } };\n"
+ "          window.AndroidInterface.nativeHttpGet(url, id);\n"
+ "          setTimeout(() => { if(httpCbs[id]) { delete httpCbs[id]; res(null); } }, 9000);\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function httpJson(url) {\n"
+ "        try { const r = await fetch(url); if(r.ok) return await r.json(); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        return await nativeHttp(url);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function parseLRC(txt) {\n"
+ "        const out = [];\n"
+ "        String(txt || '').split('\\n').forEach(line => {\n"
+ "          const m = line.match(/^\\s*((?:\\[\\d+:\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?\\]\\s*)+)(.*)$/);\n"
+ "          if(!m) return;\n"
+ "          const s = m[2].trim(); if(!s) return;\n"
+ "          const re = /\\[(\\d+):(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)\\]/g; let mm;\n"
+ "          while((mm = re.exec(m[1])) !== null) out.push({ t: parseInt(mm[1], 10) * 60 + parseFloat(mm[2]), s: s });\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        out.sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);\n"
+ "        return out;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function cleanTitle(t) { return String(t || '').replace(/\\(.*?\\)|\\[.*?\\]/g, '').replace(/\\s+-\\s+.*$/, '').replace(/\\s{2,}/g, ' ').trim(); }\n"
+ "      function keyOf(tr) { return String(tr.t || '') + '|' + String(tr.a || ''); }\n"
+ "      function emit(type, a, b) { subs.forEach(s => { try { if(s[type]) s[type](a, b); } catch(e) {} }); }\n"
+ "      function subscribe(s) { subs.push(s); }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function fetchLyrics(tr) {\n"
+ "        const title = cleanTitle(tr.t), artist = String(tr.a || '').replace(/ - Topic/i, '').trim();\n"
+ "        let data = await httpJson('https://lrclib.net/api/get?artist_name=' + encodeURIComponent(artist) + '&track_name=' + encodeURIComponent(title));\n"
+ "        if(!data || (!data.syncedLyrics && !data.plainLyrics)) {\n"
+ "          const arr = await httpJson('https://lrclib.net/api/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(artist + ' ' + title));\n"
+ "          if(Array.isArray(arr)) data = arr.find(x => x && x.syncedLyrics) || arr.find(x => x && x.plainLyrics) || null;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        const lines = data && data.syncedLyrics ? parseLRC(data.syncedLyrics) : [];\n"
+ "        const plain = (!lines.length && data && data.plainLyrics) ? String(data.plainLyrics) : '';\n"
+ "        return { lines: lines, plain: plain, trans: [], transPlain: '', tlang: '' };\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function gtx(texts, lang) {\n"
+ "        const out = []; let srcLang = '';\n"
+ "        let chunk = [], len = 0;\n"
+ "        const flush = async () => {\n"
+ "          if(!chunk.length) return;\n"
+ "          const data = await httpJson('https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single?client=gtx&sl=auto&tl=' + encodeURIComponent(lang) + '&dt=t&q=' + encodeURIComponent(chunk.join('\\n')));\n"
+ "          let arr = null;\n"
+ "          if(data && Array.isArray(data[0])) {\n"
+ "            arr = data[0].map(x => (x && x[0]) || '').join('').split('\\n');\n"
+ "            if(data[2]) srcLang = String(data[2]);\n"
+ "          }\n"
+ "          if(!arr) arr = await mymemory(chunk, lang);\n"
+ "          for(let i = 0; i < chunk.length; i++) out.push((arr && arr[i]) || '');\n"
+ "          chunk = []; len = 0;\n"
+ "        };\n"
+ "        for(const t of texts) {\n"
+ "          if(len + t.length > 1500) await flush();\n"
+ "          chunk.push(t.replace(/\\n/g, ' ')); len += t.length + 1;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        await flush();\n"
+ "        return { out: out, src: srcLang };\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function mymemory(chunk, lang) {\n"
+ "        const res = [];\n"
+ "        for(const t of chunk) {\n"
+ "          const d = await httpJson('https://api.mymemory.translated.net/get?q=' + encodeURIComponent(t) + '&langpair=Autodetect|' + encodeURIComponent(lang));\n"
+ "          res.push(d && d.responseData ? String(d.responseData.translatedText || '') : '');\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        return res;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function maybeTranslate() {\n"
+ "        const k = S.key, lang = Cfg.get('transLang');\n"
+ "        if(!Cfg.get('trans') || S.status !== 'ok') {\n"
+ "          if(S.trans.length || S.transPlain) { S.trans = []; S.transPlain = ''; S.tlang = ''; emit('render'); }\n"
+ "          return;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        const c = cache[k];\n"
+ "        if(c && c.tlang === lang && (c.trans.length || c.transPlain)) { S.trans = c.trans; S.transPlain = c.transPlain; S.tlang = lang; emit('render'); return; }\n"
+ "        let trans = [], plain = '';\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          if(S.lines.length) {\n"
+ "            const r = await gtx(S.lines.map(l => l.s), lang);\n"
+ "            if(S.key !== k) return;\n"
+ "            if(r.src && r.src.split('-')[0] === lang.split('-')[0]) trans = [];\n"
+ "            else trans = r.out;\n"
+ "          } else if(S.plain) {\n"
+ "            const rows = S.plain.split('\\n');\n"
+ "            const r = await gtx(rows.map(x => x || ' '), lang);\n"
+ "            if(S.key !== k) return;\n"
+ "            plain = (r.src && r.src.split('-')[0] === lang.split('-')[0]) ? '' : r.out.join('\\n');\n"
+ "          }\n"
+ "        } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        if(S.key !== k) return;\n"
+ "        if(c) { c.trans = trans; c.transPlain = plain; c.tlang = lang; }\n"
+ "        S.trans = trans; S.transPlain = plain; S.tlang = lang;\n"
+ "        emit('render');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function ensure(tr) {\n"
+ "        if(!tr) return;\n"
+ "        const k = keyOf(tr);\n"
+ "        if(S.key === k) return;\n"
+ "        S.key = k; S.lines = []; S.plain = ''; S.trans = []; S.transPlain = ''; S.idx = -1; S.status = 'loading'; S.tlang = '';\n"
+ "        emit('render');\n"
+ "        let d = cache[k];\n"
+ "        if(!d) {\n"
+ "          d = await fetchLyrics(tr);\n"
+ "          if(S.key !== k) return;\n"
+ "          cache[k] = d;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        S.lines = d.lines; S.plain = d.plain; S.status = (d.lines.length || d.plain) ? 'ok' : 'none';\n"
+ "        emit('render');\n"
+ "        maybeTranslate();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      function tick() {\n"
+ "        if(S.status !== 'ok' || !S.lines.length) return;\n"
+ "        const t = getCurPos() + 0.12;\n"
+ "        let lo = 0, hi = S.lines.length - 1, idx = -1;\n"
+ "        while(lo <= hi) { const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1; if(S.lines[mid].t <= t) { idx = mid; lo = mid + 1; } else hi = mid - 1; }\n"
+ "        if(idx !== S.idx) { S.idx = idx; emit('line', idx); }\n"
+ "        let p = 0;\n"
+ "        if(idx >= 0) {\n"
+ "          const cur = S.lines[idx], nx = S.lines[idx + 1];\n"
+ "          const span = nx ? Math.min(nx.t - cur.t, 8) : 4;\n"
+ "          p = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (t - cur.t) / Math.max(0.5, span)));\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        emit('progress', idx, p);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      setInterval(tick, 120);\n"
+ "      setInterval(() => { if(typeof trk !== 'undefined' && trk) ensure({ t: trk.t, a: trk.a }); }, 700);\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      return { S: S, subscribe: subscribe, ensure: ensure, refresh: () => { emit('render'); maybeTranslate(); }, retranslate: maybeTranslate, fetchFor: tr => { const k = keyOf(tr); return cache[k] ? Promise.resolve(cache[k]) : fetchLyrics(tr).then(d => (cache[k] = d)); } };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // kartu lirik pemutar penuh: lirik berjalan tanpa menghapus elemen lama (placeholder asli tetap ada)\n"
+ "    (function() {\n"
+ "      const lc = document.querySelector('#fullPlayer .lyric-text');\n"
+ "      if(!lc) return;\n"
+ "      const live = document.createElement('div');\n"
+ "      live.id = 'fpLyrLive'; live.style.display = 'none';\n"
+ "      live.innerHTML = '<div class=\"fpl-cur\" style=\"font-size:21px;font-weight:900;color:#fff;line-height:1.3;\"></div><div class=\"fpl-tr\" style=\"font-size:13px;font-style:italic;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.75);margin-top:4px;\"></div><div class=\"fpl-next\" style=\"font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.45);margin-top:12px;\"></div>';\n"
+ "      lc.parentNode.insertBefore(live, lc);\n"
+ "      const btn = document.querySelector('#fullPlayer .lyric-box button');\n"

+ "        if(ok) line(S.idx);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function line(i) {\n"
+ "        const S = LY.S; if(!S.lines.length) return;\n"

+ "      }\n"
+ "      LY.subscribe({ render: show, line: line });\n"
+ "    })();\n"

+ "      line: i => { try { if(window.AndroidInterface && window.AndroidInterface.islandLyric) window.AndroidInterface.islandLyric(i >= 0 ? LY.S.lines[i].s : ''); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "    });\n"

+ "    const LyF = (function() {\n"
+ "      const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);\n"
+ "      function hueOf(s) { let h = 0; for(let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) % 360; return h; }\n"
+ "      function applySize() { const m = { s: '22px', m: '27px', l: '33px' }; $('lyricsFull').style.setProperty('--lf-fs', m[Cfg.get('lySize')] || '27px'); }\n"
+ "      function render() {\n"
+ "        const S = LY.S, box = $('lfScroll'); if(!box) return;\n"
+ "        $('lyricsFull').style.setProperty('--lf-h', hueOf(S.key || 'aether'));\n"

+ "        box.innerHTML = '';\n"
+ "        if(S.status === 'loading') { box.innerHTML = Skel.lines(8); return; }\n"
+ "        if(S.status === 'none' || S.status === 'idle') { box.innerHTML = '<div class=\"lf-empty\">Lirik belum tersedia untuk lagu ini</div>'; return; }\n"
+ "        if(S.lines.length) {\n"
+ "          S.lines.forEach((l, i) => {\n"

+ "          });\n"

+ "        } else {\n"
+ "          const p = document.createElement('div'); p.className = 'lf-plain'; p.textContent = S.plain; box.appendChild(p);\n"
+ "          if(S.transPlain) { const t = document.createElement('div'); t.className = 'lf-plain'; t.style.cssText = 'font-size:15px;font-style:italic;opacity:.75;margin-top:18px;'; t.textContent = S.transPlain; box.appendChild(t); }\n";
String aetherHtmlPart8 = "        }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function seekTo(t) { if(typeof Room !== 'undefined' && Room.active()) Room.seekSec(t); else seekLocal(t); }\n"
+ "      function highlight(idx, instant) {\n"
+ "        const S = LY.S, box = $('lfScroll'); if(!S.lines.length || !box) return;\n"
+ "        for(let i = 0; i < S.lines.length; i++) {\n"
+ "          const e = $('lfL' + i); if(!e) break;\n"
+ "          e.className = 'lf-line' + (i < idx ? ' past' : (i === idx ? ' active' : ''));\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        const a = $('lfL' + idx);\n"
+ "        if(a && $('lyricsFull').classList.contains('open')) {\n"
+ "          const top = a.offsetTop - box.clientHeight / 2 + a.offsetHeight / 2;\n"
+ "          box.style.scrollBehavior = instant ? 'auto' : 'smooth';\n"
+ "          box.scrollTop = top;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function progress(idx, p) {\n"
+ "        if(idx < 0) return;\n"
+ "        const e = $('lfL' + idx); if(!e) return;\n"
+ "        const f = e.querySelector('.lf-fill'); if(f) f.style.setProperty('--p', (Cfg.get('sweep') ? (p * 100).toFixed(1) : 100) + '%');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      LY.subscribe({ render: render, line: i => highlight(i, false), progress: progress });\n"
+ "      Pages.hooks.lyricsFull = { open: () => { applySize(); render(); setTimeout(() => highlight(LY.S.idx, true), 60); } };\n"
+ "      setInterval(() => {\n"
+ "        if(!$('lyricsFull').classList.contains('open')) return;\n"
+ "        const d = getCurDur(), p = getCurPos();\n"
+ "        $('lfCur').textContent = fmtTime(p); $('lfDur').textContent = fmtTime(d);\n"
+ "        if(d > 0) $('lfSeek').value = (p / d) * 100;\n"
+ "        $('lfPlay').innerHTML = isP ? '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-pause\"></i>' : '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-play\"></i>';\n"
+ "      }, 400);\n"
+ "      return {\n"
+ "        seek(v) { const d = getCurDur(); if(d > 0) seekTo((v / 100) * d); },\n"
+ "        toggleTrans() { Cfg.set('trans', !Cfg.get('trans')); LY.retranslate(); if(typeof CfgUi !== 'undefined') CfgUi.sync(); toast(Cfg.get('trans') ? 'Terjemahan lirik aktif' : 'Terjemahan lirik mati'); },\n"
+ "        applySize: applySize\n"
+ "      };\n"

+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // DYNAMIC ISLAND (kontrol di Profil + data real-time ke overlay native)\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    function getCurPos() {\n"
+ "      try {\n"
+ "        if(useN) return nAud.currentTime || 0;\n"
+ "        if(ytReady && ytP && typeof ytP.getCurrentTime === 'function') return ytP.getCurrentTime() || 0;\n"
+ "      } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      return 0;\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "    function getCurDur() {\n"
+ "      try {\n"
+ "        if(useN) { let d = nAud.duration; return (isFinite(d) && d > 0) ? d : 0; }\n"
+ "        if(ytReady && ytP && typeof ytP.getDuration === 'function') return ytP.getDuration() || 0;\n"
+ "      } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      return 0;\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "    function seekLocal(t) {\n"
+ "      try {\n"
+ "        if(useN) nAud.currentTime = t;\n"
+ "        else if(ytReady && ytP && typeof ytP.seekTo === 'function') ytP.seekTo(t, true);\n"
+ "        if(ytVidMute && typeof ytVidMute.seekTo === 'function') ytVidMute.seekTo(t, true);\n"
+ "      } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function refreshIslandToggle() {\n"
+ "      let st = 'off';\n"
+ "      try {\n"
+ "        if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.getIslandState === 'function') st = window.AndroidInterface.getIslandState();\n"
+ "      } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      let t = document.getElementById('islandToggle');\n"
+ "      if(t) t.classList.toggle('on', st === 'on');\n"
+ "      return st;\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "    window.refreshIslandToggle = refreshIslandToggle;\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    function toggleIsland() {\n"
+ "      if(!(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.setIslandEnabled === 'function')) {\n"
+ "        toast('Dynamic Island hanya tersedia di aplikasi Android'); return;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      let st = refreshIslandToggle();\n"
+ "      let res = window.AndroidInterface.setIslandEnabled(st !== 'on');\n"
+ "      refreshIslandToggle();\n"
+ "      if(res === 'UNSUPPORTED') toast('Dynamic Island tidak tersedia di perangkat TV');\n"
+ "      else if(st === 'on') toast('Dynamic Island dimatikan');\n"

+ "    }\n"

+ "\n"
+ "    const Room = (function() {\n"
+ "      const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);\n"
+ "      const S = {\n"
+ "        ready: false, db: null, uid: null, offset: 0, id: null, meta: null, state: null,\n"
+ "        queue: [], members: {}, listeners: [], tab: 'queue', lastApplied: '', justLoaded: false,\n"
+ "        unread: 0, geo: null, open: false, chatMsgs: [], lastChat: 0, lastReact: 0, joinedAt: 0,\n"
+ "        searchRes: [], seekDrag: false, stepLock: 0, mode: 'all', visible: false, lyricBackup: null,\n"
+ "        searchTimer: null, drift: 0, tickTimer: null\n"
+ "      };\n"
+ "      let blocked = [];\n"
+ "      try { blocked = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('aether_rm_blocked') || '[]'); } catch(e) { blocked = []; }\n"
+ "      const EMOJIS = ['\\u2764\\uFE0F', '\\uD83D\\uDD25', '\\uD83D\\uDC4F', '\\uD83D\\uDE02', '\\uD83C\\uDF89', '\\uD83C\\uDFB5'];\n"
+ "      const B32 = '0123456789bcdefghjkmnpqrstuvwxyz';\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      // ---------------------------------------------------------------- util\n"
+ "      function esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/\"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }\n"
+ "      function now() { return Date.now() + S.offset; }\n"
+ "      function TS() { return firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP; }\n"
+ "      function validId(id) { return typeof id === 'string' && /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{4,24}$/.test(id); }\n"
+ "      function hhmm(ts) { let d = new Date(ts || now()); return String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'); }\n"
+ "      function localRid() {\n"
+ "        let r = localStorage.getItem('aether_rid');\n"
+ "        if(!r) { r = 'u' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 12) + Date.now().toString(36).slice(-4); localStorage.setItem('aether_rid', r); }\n"
+ "        return r;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function getName() {\n"
+ "        if(Priv.flag('priv_anon_name')) return ('Anonim-' + String(localRid()).slice(-4)).slice(0, 24);\n"
+ "        let n = (localStorage.getItem('aether_room_name') || '').trim();\n"
+ "        return (n || ('Listener-' + String(userId || 'AETH').slice(-4))).slice(0, 24);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function cleanTrack(t) {\n"
+ "        if(!t || typeof t.id !== 'string' || !/^[\\w-]{11}$/.test(t.id)) return null;\n"
+ "        return { id: t.id, t: String(t.t || '').slice(0, 80), a: String(t.a || '').slice(0, 60), l: 'https://i.ytimg.com/vi/' + t.id + '/hqdefault.jpg' };\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function normQueue(v) {\n"
+ "        if(!v) return [];\n"
+ "        let arr = Array.isArray(v) ? v.slice() : Object.keys(v).sort((a, b) => a - b).map(k => v[k]);\n"
+ "        return arr.filter(x => x && typeof x.id === 'string');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function genCode() {\n"
+ "        const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';\n"
+ "        let out = '', arr = new Uint32Array(6);\n"
+ "        try { crypto.getRandomValues(arr); } catch(e) { for(let i = 0; i < 6; i++) arr[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4294967295); }\n"
+ "        for(let i = 0; i < 6; i++) out += chars[arr[i] % chars.length];\n"
+ "        return out;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Firebase\n"
+ "      function ensureFirebase() {\n"
+ "        if(S.ready) return Promise.resolve(true);\n"
+ "        if(typeof firebase === 'undefined' || !firebase.initializeApp || !firebase.database) {\n"
+ "          toast('Firebase belum termuat, periksa koneksi internet'); return Promise.resolve(false);\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          if(!firebase.apps.length) firebase.initializeApp(FIREBASE_CONFIG);\n"
+ "          S.db = firebase.database();\n"
+ "          S.db.ref('.info/serverTimeOffset').on('value', s => { S.offset = s.val() || 0; });\n"
+ "        } catch(e) { toast('Gagal memulai Firebase'); return Promise.resolve(false); }\n"
+ "        return new Promise(res => {\n"
+ "          let fin = false;\n"
+ "          const done = () => { if(!fin) { fin = true; res(); } };\n"
+ "          try {\n"
+ "            if(firebase.auth) {\n"
+ "              const au = firebase.auth();\n"
+ "              if(au.currentUser) { S.uid = au.currentUser.uid; done(); }\n"
+ "              else au.signInAnonymously().then(c => { S.uid = c.user.uid; done(); }).catch(() => done());\n"
+ "              setTimeout(done, 6000);\n"
+ "            } else done();\n"
+ "          } catch(e) { done(); }\n"
+ "        }).then(() => {\n"
+ "          if(!S.uid) S.uid = localRid();\n"
+ "          S.ready = true;\n"
+ "          return true;\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      // ---------------------------------------------------------------- lokasi & geohash\n"
+ "      function ghEncode(lat, lng, prec) {\n"
+ "        let idx = 0, bit = 0, even = true, hash = '';\n"
+ "        const latR = [-90, 90], lngR = [-180, 180];\n"
+ "        while(hash.length < prec) {\n"
+ "          const r = even ? lngR : latR;\n"
+ "          const v = even ? lng : lat;\n"
+ "          const mid = (r[0] + r[1]) / 2;\n"
+ "          if(v > mid) { idx = idx * 2 + 1; r[0] = mid; } else { idx = idx * 2; r[1] = mid; }\n"
+ "          even = !even;\n"
+ "          if(++bit === 5) { hash += B32[idx]; bit = 0; idx = 0; }\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        return hash;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function ghAround(lat, lng, prec) {\n"
+ "        const bits = prec * 5, lngBits = Math.ceil(bits / 2), latBits = Math.floor(bits / 2);\n"
+ "        const dLat = 180 / Math.pow(2, latBits), dLng = 360 / Math.pow(2, lngBits);\n"
+ "        const cLat = Math.floor((lat + 90) / dLat) * dLat - 90 + dLat / 2;\n"
+ "        const cLng = Math.floor((lng + 180) / dLng) * dLng - 180 + dLng / 2;\n"
+ "        const cells = [];\n"
+ "        for(let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {\n"
+ "          for(let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {\n"
+ "            let la = cLat + dy * dLat, ln = cLng + dx * dLng;\n"
+ "            if(la > 89.9 || la < -89.9) continue;\n"
+ "            if(ln > 180) ln -= 360;\n"
+ "            if(ln < -180) ln += 360;\n"
+ "            const g = ghEncode(la, ln, prec);\n"
+ "            if(cells.indexOf(g) < 0) cells.push(g);\n"
+ "          }\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        return { own: ghEncode(lat, lng, prec), cells: cells };\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      const locCbs = {};\n"
+ "      window.onNativeLocationResult = function(reqId, status, lat, lng) {\n"
+ "        const cb = locCbs[reqId];\n"
+ "        if(cb) { delete locCbs[reqId]; cb(status === 'OK' ? { lat: lat, lng: lng } : null); }\n"
+ "      };\n"
+ "      function nativeLoc() {\n"
+ "        return new Promise(res => {\n"
+ "          if(!(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.getCoarseLocation === 'function')) {\n"
+ "            if(navigator.geolocation) navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(p => res({ lat: p.coords.latitude, lng: p.coords.longitude }), () => res(null), { timeout: 8000, maximumAge: 600000 });\n"
+ "            else res(null);\n"
+ "            return;\n"
+ "          }\n"
+ "          const id = 'l' + Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);\n"
+ "          locCbs[id] = res;\n"
+ "          window.AndroidInterface.getCoarseLocation(id);\n"
+ "          setTimeout(() => { if(locCbs[id]) { delete locCbs[id]; res(null); } }, 40000);\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function resolveLocation() {\n"
+ "        let loc = await nativeLoc();\n"
+ "        if(loc && typeof loc.lat === 'number') return { lat: loc.lat, lng: loc.lng, src: 'gps' };\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          const r = await fetch('https://ipwho.is/');\n"
+ "          const j = await r.json();\n"
+ "          if(j && j.success !== false && typeof j.latitude === 'number') return { lat: j.latitude, lng: j.longitude, src: 'ip' };\n"
+ "        } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        return null;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function nearStatus(t) { const el = $('rmNearStatus'); if(el) el.textContent = t || ''; }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function findNearby(autoJoin) {\n"
+ "        if(Priv.flag('priv_no_loc')) { nearStatus('Lokasi dimatikan di Pengaturan > Privasi. Gabung dengan kode ruangan.'); return; }\n"
+ "        if(!(await ensureFirebase())) return;\n"
+ "        nearStatus('Mencari lokasi kasar...');\n"
+ "        const loc = await resolveLocation();\n"
+ "        if(!loc) { nearStatus('Lokasi tidak tersedia. Aktifkan lokasi/izin, atau gabung dengan kode ruangan.'); return; }\n"
+ "        const g = ghAround(loc.lat, loc.lng, 5);\n"
+ "        S.geo = { own: g.own, cells: g.cells, src: loc.src };\n"
+ "        const autoId = 'NB5-' + g.own;\n"
+ "        try { await ensureAutoRoom(autoId, g.own); } catch(e) { nearStatus('Gagal membuat ruang terdekat. Periksa aturan Firebase.'); return; }\n"
+ "        nearStatus(loc.src === 'ip' ? 'Lokasi perkiraan (kota). Izinkan lokasi untuk hasil lebih akurat.' : 'Area terdeteksi.');\n"
+ "        await listNearby();\n"
+ "        if(autoJoin) join(autoId);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function ensureAutoRoom(id, cell) {\n"
+ "        const meta = {\n"
+ "          name: 'Ruang Terdekat', hostId: S.uid, hostName: getName(), type: 'nearby',\n"
+ "          controlMode: 'all', geo: cell, createdAt: TS()\n"
+ "        };\n"
+ "        await S.db.ref('rooms/' + id + '/meta').transaction(cur => cur ? undefined : meta);\n"
+ "        await S.db.ref('geo/' + cell + '/' + id).update({ name: 'Ruang Terdekat', type: 'nearby', ts: TS() });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function listNearby() {\n"
+ "        if(!S.geo || !S.db) return;\n"
+ "        const box = $('rmNearList');\n"
+ "        const found = [];\n"
+ "        await Promise.all(S.geo.cells.map(cell =>\n"
+ "          S.db.ref('geo/' + cell).once('value').then(snap => {\n"
+ "            snap.forEach(ch => { const v = ch.val() || {}; if(validId(ch.key)) found.push({ id: ch.key, name: v.name || 'Ruangan', type: v.type || 'public', host: v.hostName || '', cell: cell }); });\n"
+ "          }).catch(() => {})\n"
+ "        ));\n"
+ "        const uniq = {}; found.forEach(f => { uniq[f.id] = f; });\n"
+ "        const rooms = Object.values(uniq).slice(0, 24);\n"
+ "        const own = 'NB5-' + S.geo.own;\n"
+ "        await Promise.all(rooms.map(r => S.db.ref('rooms/' + r.id + '/members').once('value').then(s => { r.count = s.numChildren(); }).catch(() => { r.count = 0; })));\n"
+ "        const shown = rooms.filter(r => r.id === own || r.count > 0).sort((a, b) => (b.id === own) - (a.id === own) || b.count - a.count);\n"
+ "        if(!shown.length) { box.innerHTML = '<div class=\"rm-status\">Belum ada ruangan aktif di sekitarmu.</div>'; return; }\n"
+ "        box.innerHTML = shown.map(r =>\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"rm-row\" onclick=\"Room.join(\\'' + r.id + '\\')\"><div class=\"rm-row-ic\"><i class=\"fa-solid ' + (r.type === 'nearby' ? 'fa-location-dot' : 'fa-headphones') + '\"></i></div>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"rm-row-info\"><div class=\"rm-row-title\">' + esc(r.name) + (r.id === own ? ' (area kamu)' : '') + '</div>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"rm-row-sub\">' + r.count + ' pendengar &bull; kode ' + esc(r.id) + '</div></div><i class=\"fa-solid fa-chevron-right rm-row-act\"></i></div>'\n"
+ "        ).join('');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      // ---------------------------------------------------------------- buat / gabung / keluar\n"
+ "      function setMode(m) {\n"
+ "        S.mode = m === 'host' ? 'host' : 'all';\n"
+ "        $('rmModeAll').classList.toggle('active', S.mode === 'all');\n"
+ "        $('rmModeHost').classList.toggle('active', S.mode === 'host');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function setVisible() {\n"
+ "        S.visible = !S.visible;\n"
+ "        $('rmVisToggle').classList.toggle('on', S.visible);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function saveName() {\n"
+ "        const v = ($('rmNameInp').value || '').trim().slice(0, 24);\n"
+ "        try { localStorage.setItem('aether_room_name', v); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function create() {\n"
+ "        if(!(await ensureFirebase())) return;\n"
+ "        saveName();\n"
+ "        let cell = null;\n"
+ "        if(S.visible && Priv.flag('priv_no_loc')) { toast('Lokasi dimatikan di Pengaturan > Privasi'); return; }\n"
+ "        if(S.visible) {\n"
+ "          if(!S.geo) {\n"
+ "            nearStatus('Mencari lokasi kasar...');\n"
+ "            const loc = await resolveLocation();\n"
+ "            if(!loc) { toast('Lokasi tidak tersedia. Matikan \"Tampilkan di Ruang Terdekat\" atau izinkan lokasi'); return; }\n"
+ "            const g = ghAround(loc.lat, loc.lng, 5);\n"
+ "            S.geo = { own: g.own, cells: g.cells, src: loc.src };\n"
+ "          }\n"
+ "          cell = S.geo.own;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        let code = '', ok = false;\n"
+ "        const nm = (($('rmCreateName').value || '').trim() || (getName() + \"'s Room\")).slice(0, 30);\n"
+ "        for(let i = 0; i < 5 && !ok; i++) {\n"
+ "          code = genCode();\n"
+ "          const meta = { name: nm, hostId: S.uid, hostName: getName(), type: S.visible ? 'public' : 'private', controlMode: S.mode, createdAt: TS() };\n"
+ "          if(cell) meta.geo = cell;\n"
+ "          try {\n"
+ "            const r = await S.db.ref('rooms/' + code + '/meta').transaction(cur => cur ? undefined : meta);\n"
+ "            ok = !!(r && r.committed);\n"
+ "          } catch(e) { toast('Gagal membuat ruangan. Periksa aturan Firebase'); return; }\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        if(!ok) { toast('Gagal membuat kode ruangan, coba lagi'); return; }\n"
+ "        if(cell) { try { await S.db.ref('geo/' + cell + '/' + code).set({ name: nm, type: 'public', hostName: getName(), ts: TS() }); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "        join(code);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      function joinCode() {\n"
+ "        let c = ($('rmJoinCode').value || '').trim();\n"
+ "        if(/^nb5-/i.test(c)) c = 'NB5-' + c.slice(4).toLowerCase(); else c = c.toUpperCase();\n"
+ "        join(c);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function join(id, quiet) {\n"
+ "        if(!validId(id)) { toast('Kode ruangan tidak valid'); return; }\n"
+ "        if(!(await ensureFirebase())) return;\n"
+ "        if(S.id && S.id !== id) await leave(true);\n"
+ "        if(S.id === id) { open(); return; }\n"
+ "        saveName();\n"
+ "        let snap;\n"
+ "        try { snap = await S.db.ref('rooms/' + id + '/meta').once('value'); } catch(e) { toast('Akses ditolak. Periksa aturan Firebase'); return; }\n"
+ "        if(!snap.exists()) { toast('Ruangan tidak ditemukan'); return; }\n"
+ "        S.id = id; S.meta = snap.val() || {}; S.joinedAt = now(); S.queue = []; S.state = null;\n"
+ "        S.members = {}; S.lastApplied = ''; S.chatMsgs = []; S.unread = 0; S.drift = 0;\n"
+ "        $('rmChatList').innerHTML = '';\n"
+ "        $('rmSearchInp').value = ''; $('rmSearchWrap').style.display = 'none';\n"
+ "        const base = S.db.ref('rooms/' + id);\n"
+ "\n"
+ "        listen(S.db.ref('.info/connected'), 'value', s => {\n"
+ "          if(s.val() === true && S.id === id) {\n"
+ "            const me = base.child('members/' + S.uid);\n"
+ "            me.onDisconnect().remove();\n"
+ "            me.set({ name: getName(), joinedAt: TS() });\n"
+ "          }\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('meta'), 'value', s => { if(S.id === id && s.exists()) { S.meta = s.val(); renderHeader(); } });\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('state'), 'value', s => { S.state = s.val(); applyState(); renderNP(); renderQueue(); });\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('queue'), 'value', s => { S.queue = normQueue(s.val()); renderQueue(); });\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('members'), 'child_added', s => {\n"
+ "          const v = s.val() || {}; S.members[s.key] = v; renderMembers();\n"
+ "          if(s.key !== S.uid && (v.joinedAt || 0) >= S.joinedAt - 500) sysMsg(String(v.name || 'Seseorang').slice(0, 24) + ' bergabung');\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('members'), 'child_changed', s => { S.members[s.key] = s.val() || {}; renderMembers(); });\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('members'), 'child_removed', s => {\n"
+ "          const v = S.members[s.key]; delete S.members[s.key]; renderMembers();\n"
+ "          if(v && s.key !== S.uid) sysMsg(String(v.name || 'Seseorang').slice(0, 24) + ' keluar');\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('chat').limitToLast(80), 'child_added', s => addChat(s.key, s.val()));\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('reactions').limitToLast(5), 'child_added', s => {\n"
+ "          const v = s.val() || {};\n"
+ "          if((v.ts || 0) >= S.joinedAt && typeof v.e === 'string' && EMOJIS.indexOf(v.e) >= 0) floatEmoji(v.e);\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "\n"
+ "        listen(base.child('karaoke'), 'value', s => {\n"
+ "          const v = s.val() || {}; const m = {};\n"
+ "          Object.keys(v).forEach(k => { const x = v[k]; if(x && typeof x === 'object') m[k] = { name: String(x.name || '').slice(0, 24), level: +x.level || 0, score: +x.score || 0 }; });\n"
+ "          K.singers = m; kSingers();\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        try { localStorage.setItem('aether_room_last', id); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        setMode(S.meta.controlMode === 'host' ? 'host' : 'all');\n"
+ "        S.tab = 'queue'; setTab('queue');\n"
+ "        renderHeader(); renderNP(); renderQueue(); renderMembers();\n"
+ "        if(S.tickTimer) clearInterval(S.tickTimer);\n"
+ "        S.tickTimer = setInterval(forceSync, 1000);\n"
+ "        if(!quiet) { open(); toast('Masuk ke ' + String(S.meta.name || 'ruangan')); } else updChip();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      function listen(ref, evt, cb) {\n"
+ "        ref.on(evt, cb, err => { if(S.id) { toast('Akses ruangan ditolak. Periksa aturan Firebase'); leave(true); } });\n"
+ "        S.listeners.push([ref, evt, cb]);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function detachAll() {\n"
+ "        S.listeners.forEach(l => { try { l[0].off(l[1], l[2]); } catch(e) {} });\n"
+ "        S.listeners = [];\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      async function leave(silent) {\n"
+ "        if(!S.id) return;\n"
+ "        const id = S.id;\n"
+ "        detachAll();\n"
+ "        if(S.tickTimer) { clearInterval(S.tickTimer); S.tickTimer = null; }\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          const me = S.db.ref('rooms/' + id + '/members/' + S.uid);\n"
+ "          me.onDisconnect().cancel();\n"
+ "          await me.remove();\n"
+ "        } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        S.id = null; S.meta = null; S.state = null; S.queue = []; S.members = {}; S.lastApplied = '';\n"
+ "        karaokeStop(true);\n"
+ "        K.singers = {};\n"
+ "        try { localStorage.removeItem('aether_room_last'); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        updChip();\n"
+ "        if(!silent) { toast('Keluar dari ruangan'); showLobby(); }\n"
+ "        else if(S.open) showLobby();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      // ---------------------------------------------------------------- tampil / sembunyi\n"

+ "      function canControl() {\n"
+ "        const m = S.meta; if(!S.id || !m) return false;\n"

+ "      function expectedPos(st) { return st.playing ? (st.pos || 0) + (now() - (st.ts || now())) / 1000 : (st.pos || 0); }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      function applyState() {\n"
+ "        const st = S.state;\n"
+ "        if(!S.id || !st || !st.track) return;\n"
+ "        const tr = cleanTrack(st.track); if(!tr) return;\n"
+ "        const key = tr.id + ':' + (st.sid || '0');\n"
+ "        if(S.lastApplied !== key) {\n"
+ "          S.lastApplied = key; S.justLoaded = true;\n"
+ "          const qa = S.queue.map(cleanTrack).filter(Boolean);\n"
+ "          let i = qa.findIndex(x => x.id === tr.id);\n"
+ "          if(i < 0) { qa.unshift(tr); i = 0; }\n"
+ "          q = qa;\n"
+ "          try { RAW.play(i); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "          LY.ensure({ t: tr.t, a: tr.a });\n"
+ "          setTimeout(forceSync, 500); setTimeout(forceSync, 1500); setTimeout(forceSync, 3000);\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        forceSync();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      function forceSync() {\n"
+ "        const st = S.state;\n"
+ "        if(!S.id || !st || !st.track || !trk) return;\n"
+ "        const tr = cleanTrack(st.track); if(!tr) return;\n"
+ "        if(S.lastApplied !== tr.id + ':' + (st.sid || '0')) return;\n"
+ "        const exp = expectedPos(st);\n"
+ "        const dur = getCurDur();\n"
+ "        if(dur > 0) {\n"
+ "          const cp = getCurPos();\n"
+ "          S.drift = cp - exp;\n"
+ "          const tol = S.justLoaded ? 1.0 : 2.2;\n"
+ "          if(Math.abs(S.drift) > tol && exp >= 0 && exp < dur) { seekLocal(Math.max(0, Math.min(dur - 0.3, exp))); }\n"
+ "          S.justLoaded = false;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        if(st.playing && !isP) RAW.tog();\n"
+ "        else if(!st.playing && isP) RAW.tog();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      function setTrackState(tr) {\n"
+ "        return S.db.ref('rooms/' + S.id + '/state').set({\n"
+ "          track: { id: tr.id, t: tr.t, a: tr.a }, sid: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5),\n"

+ "        });\n"

+ "        return S.db.ref('rooms/' + S.id + '/queue').transaction(cur => {\n"

+ "          if(arr.some(x => x.id === tr.id)) return arr;\n"

+ "        if(!ensureCtl()) return;\n"
+ "        const st = S.state;\n"
+ "        if(!st || !st.track) { toast('Belum ada lagu. Cari & tambahkan lagu dulu'); setTab('queue'); open(); return; }\n"
+ "        const dur = getCurDur();\n"
+ "        const pos = (S.lastApplied && dur > 0) ? getCurPos() : expectedPos(st);\n"
+ "        S.db.ref('rooms/' + S.id + '/state').update({ playing: !st.playing, pos: pos, ts: TS(), by: S.uid });\n"
+ "      }\n"

+ "      function step(dir) {\n"
+ "        if(!S.id) return;\n"
+ "        let auto = false;\n"
+ "        const d = getCurDur();\n"
+ "        if(d > 0 && getCurPos() >= d - 2.5) auto = true;\n"
+ "        if(!canControl()) { if(!auto) ensureCtl(); return; }\n"
+ "        const t = Date.now(); if(t - S.stepLock < 700) return; S.stepLock = t;\n"

+ "        if(!qa.length) { if(!auto) toast('Antrean kosong'); return; }\n"
+ "        const cur = S.state && S.state.track;\n"
+ "        let i = cur ? qa.findIndex(x => x.id === cur.id) : -1;\n"
+ "        i = i < 0 ? 0 : (i + dir + qa.length) % qa.length;\n"
+ "        const nt = cleanTrack(qa[i]); if(!nt) return;\n"
+ "        const mySid = S.state ? S.state.sid : null;\n"
+ "        S.db.ref('rooms/' + S.id + '/state').transaction(c => {\n"
+ "          if(c && mySid && c.sid !== mySid) return;\n"

+ "            track: { id: nt.id, t: nt.t, a: nt.a }, sid: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5),\n"

+ "          };\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "      }\n"

+ "      function kLine(idx) {\n"

+ "        const L = LY.S;\n"
+ "        box.innerHTML = '';\n"
+ "        if(!L.lines.length) { box.textContent = L.status === 'loading' ? 'Memuat lirik...' : 'Lirik berjalan belum tersedia untuk lagu ini'; $('kNext').textContent = ''; $('kTr').textContent = ''; return; }\n"
+ "        if(idx < 0) { box.textContent = '\\u266A  \\u266A  \\u266A'; $('kNext').textContent = L.lines[0].s; $('kTr').textContent = ''; return; }\n"
+ "        const f = document.createElement('span'); f.className = 'lf-fill'; f.textContent = L.lines[idx].s; box.appendChild(f);\n"
+ "        $('kNext').textContent = idx + 1 < L.lines.length ? L.lines[idx + 1].s : '';\n"
+ "        $('kTr').textContent = L.trans[idx] || '';\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function kProgress(idx, p) {\n"
+ "        if(idx < 0) return;\n"
+ "        const f = $('kLine') && $('kLine').querySelector('.lf-fill');\n"
+ "        if(f) f.style.setProperty('--p', (Cfg.get('sweep') ? (p * 100).toFixed(1) : 100) + '%');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      LY.subscribe({ render: roomRenderLyrics, line: roomOnLine, progress: kProgress });\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      function kBtn() {\n"
+ "        const b = $('kMicBtn'); if(!b) return;\n"
+ "        b.innerHTML = K.on ? '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-microphone-slash\"></i> Berhenti Menyanyi' : '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-microphone\"></i> Mulai Menyanyi';\n"
+ "        b.className = 'm-btn-full ' + (K.on ? 'b-red' : 'b-green');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function kSingers() {\n"
+ "        const box = $('kSingers'); if(!box) return;\n"
+ "        const arr = Object.keys(K.singers).map(k => Object.assign({ uid: k }, K.singers[k])).sort((a, b) => (b.score || 0) - (a.score || 0));\n"
+ "        if(!arr.length) { box.innerHTML = '<div class=\"rm-status\">Belum ada yang menyanyi. Tekan Mulai Menyanyi untuk jadi yang pertama!</div>'; return; }\n"
+ "        box.innerHTML = arr.map((s, i) => {\n"
+ "          const nm = String(s.name || 'Listener').slice(0, 24);\n"
+ "          const lv = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, +s.level || 0));\n"
+ "          return '<div class=\"k-sing\"><div class=\"k-av\" style=\"background:' + avColor(nm) + '\">' + esc(initial(nm)) + '</div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div style=\"flex:1;min-width:0;\"><div class=\"rm-row-title\">' + (i === 0 && s.score > 0 ? '\\uD83D\\uDC51 ' : '') + esc(nm) + (s.uid === S.uid ? ' (kamu)' : '') + '</div><div class=\"k-bar\"><i style=\"width:' + Math.round(lv * 100) + '%\"></i></div></div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"k-score\">' + Math.max(0, Math.round(+s.score || 0)) + '</div></div>';\n"
+ "        }).join('');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function karaokeToggle() {\n"
+ "        if(K.on) { karaokeStop(false); return; }\n"
+ "        if(!S.id) return;\n"
+ "        const ok = await Perm.ask('android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO');\n"
+ "        if(!ok) { toast('Izin mikrofon diperlukan untuk karaoke'); return; }\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          K.stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: { echoCancellation: true, noiseSuppression: true } });\n"
+ "          const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;\n"
+ "          K.ctx = new AC();\n"
+ "          if(K.ctx.state === 'suspended') { try { await K.ctx.resume(); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "          const src = K.ctx.createMediaStreamSource(K.stream);\n"
+ "          K.an = K.ctx.createAnalyser(); K.an.fftSize = 1024; src.connect(K.an);\n"
+ "          K.data = new Uint8Array(K.an.fftSize);\n"
+ "          K.on = true; K.score = 0; K.level = 0;\n"
+ "          K.ref = S.db.ref('rooms/' + S.id + '/karaoke/' + S.uid);\n"
+ "          try { K.ref.onDisconnect().remove(); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "          K.timer = setInterval(kSample, 100);\n"
+ "          kBtn(); toast('Mikrofon aktif. Nyanyi bareng!');\n"
+ "        } catch(e) { toast('Mikrofon tidak bisa diakses'); karaokeStop(true); }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function kSample() {\n"
+ "        if(!K.an || !K.on) return;\n"
+ "        K.an.getByteTimeDomainData(K.data);\n"
+ "        let sum = 0;\n"
+ "        for(let i = 0; i < K.data.length; i++) { const v = (K.data[i] - 128) / 128; sum += v * v; }\n"
+ "        const lv = Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(sum / K.data.length) * 5);\n"
+ "        K.level = K.level * 0.5 + lv * 0.5;\n"
+ "        if(LY.S.idx >= 0 && isP && K.level > 0.12) K.score += K.level * 0.8;\n"
+ "        K.singers[S.uid] = { name: getName(), level: K.level, score: K.score };\n"
+ "        if(Date.now() - K.lastSend > 250 && K.ref) {\n"
+ "          K.lastSend = Date.now();\n"
+ "          K.ref.set({ name: getName(), level: +K.level.toFixed(2), score: Math.round(K.score), ts: TS() }).catch(() => {});\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        kSingers();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function karaokeStop(silent) {\n"
+ "        if(K.timer) { clearInterval(K.timer); K.timer = null; }\n"
+ "        try { if(K.stream) K.stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        try { if(K.ctx) K.ctx.close(); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        try { if(K.ref) { K.ref.remove(); K.ref.onDisconnect().cancel(); } } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        K.stream = null; K.ctx = null; K.an = null; K.ref = null; K.on = false; K.level = 0;\n"
+ "        kBtn();\n"
+ "        if(!silent) toast('Mikrofon dimatikan');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function seekSec(sec) {\n"
+ "        if(!S.id || !S.state || !S.state.track) return;\n"
+ "        if(!ensureCtl()) return;\n"
+ "        S.db.ref('rooms/' + S.id + '/state').update({ pos: Math.max(0, sec), ts: TS(), by: S.uid });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function renderAvatars() {\n"
+ "        const box = $('rmAvs'); if(!box) return;\n"
+ "        const names = Object.keys(S.members).map(k => S.members[k].name).slice(0, 7);\n"
+ "        box.innerHTML = names.map(n => '<div class=\"rm-av\" style=\"background:' + avColor(n) + '\">' + esc(initial(n)) + '</div>').join('');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      // slider pemutar penuh ikut menyiarkan seek saat di dalam ruangan\n"
+ "      document.getElementById('seek').addEventListener('change', e => { if(S.id) seekFromFullPlayer(e.target.value); });\n"
+ "\n"
+ "      return {\n"
+ "        active: () => !!S.id, screenOpen: () => S.open, open: open, close: close, openFromCreate: openFromCreate,\n"
+ "        findNearby: findNearby, join: join, joinCode: joinCode, create: create, leave: leave, setTab: setTab,\n"
+ "        sendChat: sendChat, react: react, toggle: toggle, step: step, onLocalPlay: onLocalPlay,\n"

+ "        seekSec: seekSec, karaoke: karaokeToggle, rejoin: function() { let id = null; try { id = localStorage.getItem('aether_room_last'); } catch(e) {} if(id && !S.id) join(id, true); }\n"
+ "      };\n"
+ "    })();\n"

+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // v4.1.1 - FITUR: sesi, statistik, perbanyak, top 5, home otomatis, playlist\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    function escHtml(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/\"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }\n"
+ "    function fmtBytes(b) {\n"
+ "      b = +b || 0;\n"
+ "      if(b >= 1073741824) return (b / 1073741824).toFixed(2) + ' GB';\n"
+ "      if(b >= 1048576) return (b / 1048576).toFixed(b >= 104857600 ? 0 : 1) + ' MB';\n"
+ "      if(b >= 1024) return Math.round(b / 1024) + ' KB';\n"
+ "      return b + ' B';\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "    function countUp(el, to) {\n"
+ "      if(!el) return;\n"
+ "      const t0 = performance.now(), dur = 900;\n"
+ "      function f(t) { const p = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / dur); el.textContent = Math.round(to * (1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3))); if(p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f); }\n"
+ "      requestAnimationFrame(f);\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "    const $$ = id => document.getElementById(id);\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- penjaga riwayat (Mode Penyamaran) + sesi\n"
+ "    const Sess = {\n"
+ "      pending: null,\n"
+ "      save() {\n"
+ "        if(typeof trk === 'undefined' || !trk) return;\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          localStorage.setItem('aether_session', JSON.stringify({\n"
+ "            q: (q || []).slice(0, 40).map(x => ({ id: x.id, t: x.t, a: x.a, l: x.l })),\n"
+ "            idx: idx, trk: { id: trk.id, t: trk.t, a: trk.a, l: trk.l },\n"
+ "            pos: Sess.pending ? Sess.pending.pos : getCurPos(), tab: activeTab, ts: Date.now()\n"
+ "          }));\n"
+ "        } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      restore() {\n"
+ "        if(!Cfg.get('resume')) return;\n"
+ "        let s = null;\n"
+ "        try { s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('aether_session') || 'null'); } catch(e) { s = null; }\n"
+ "        if(!s || !s.trk || !s.q || !s.q.length) return;\n"
+ "        if(typeof trk !== 'undefined' && trk) return;\n"
+ "        q = s.q; idx = Math.min(Math.max(0, s.idx || 0), q.length - 1); trk = q[idx] || s.trk;\n"
+ "        const t = trk;\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          $$('pArt').src = t.l; $$('pTit').innerText = t.t; $$('pArtis').innerText = t.a;\n"
+ "          $$('mImg').src = t.l; $$('mTit').innerText = t.t; $$('mArtis').innerText = t.a;\n"
+ "          $$('dynamicBg').style.backgroundImage = \"url('\" + t.l + \"')\";\n"
+ "          $$('lyrTitle').innerText = t.t; $$('artBioImg').src = t.l; $$('artBioName').innerText = t.a;\n"
+ "          $$('mini').classList.remove('hidden');\n"
+ "          $$('cTime').innerText = fmtTime(s.pos || 0);\n"
+ "        } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        isP = false; Sess.pending = { idx: idx, pos: s.pos || 0 };\n"
+ "        try { updUI(); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        if(typeof s.tab === 'number' && s.tab !== activeTab && s.tab !== 4) swTab(s.tab);\n"
+ "        setTimeout(() => { if(Cfg.get('resume')) Room.rejoin(); }, 2500);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "    setInterval(() => Sess.save(), 3000);\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    (function() {\n"
+ "      const _rp = RAW.play, _rt = RAW.tog;\n"
+ "      RAW.play = function(i) {\n"
+ "        if(q[i] && !q[i].id) { Resolver.resolveThenPlay(i); return; }\n"
+ "        Sess.pending = null;\n"
+ "        const keep = Priv.noHistory();\n"
+ "        let sr, sp, lr, lp;\n"
+ "        if(keep) { sr = recents.slice(); sp = JSON.stringify(playCounts); lr = localStorage.getItem('aether_rec'); lp = localStorage.getItem('aether_play_counts'); }\n"
+ "        const r = _rp(i);\n"
+ "        if(keep) {\n"
+ "          recents = sr; playCounts = JSON.parse(sp);\n"
+ "          if(lr === null) localStorage.removeItem('aether_rec'); else localStorage.setItem('aether_rec', lr);\n"
+ "          if(lp === null) localStorage.removeItem('aether_play_counts'); else localStorage.setItem('aether_play_counts', lp);\n"
+ "          try { renderRecentCarousel(recents.length ? recents : homeTracks.slice(0, 6)); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        return r;\n"
+ "      };\n"
+ "      RAW.tog = function() {\n"
+ "        if(Sess.pending) {\n"
+ "          const p = Sess.pending; Sess.pending = null;\n"
+ "          RAW.play(p.idx);\n"
+ "          const t0 = Date.now();\n"
+ "          const iv = setInterval(() => {\n"
+ "            if(getCurDur() > 0) { if(p.pos > 1) seekLocal(p.pos); clearInterval(iv); }\n"
+ "            else if(Date.now() - t0 > 12000) clearInterval(iv);\n"
+ "          }, 400);\n"
+ "          return;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        return _rt();\n"
+ "      };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- statistik bulan ini\n"
+ "    const Stats = (function() {\n"
+ "      const KEY = 'aether_stats_v1';\n"
+ "      let d = null, lastKey = '';\n"
+ "      const monthKey = () => { const n = new Date(); return n.getFullYear() + '-' + String(n.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'); };\n"
+ "      const blank = () => ({ m: monthKey(), sec: 0, plays: 0, tracks: {}, artists: {}, days: {}, hours: {} });\n"
+ "      function load() { try { d = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || 'null'); } catch(e) { d = null; } if(!d || d.m !== monthKey()) d = blank(); }\n"
+ "      function save() { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(d)); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "      load();\n"
+ "      function rec(id) { return d.tracks[id] || (d.tracks[id] = { t: trk.t, a: trk.a, l: trk.l, c: 0, s: 0 }); }\n"
+ "      function tick() {\n"
+ "        if(Priv.noHistory()) { lastKey = ''; return; }\n"
+ "        if(typeof trk === 'undefined' || !trk) return;\n"
+ "        if(d.m !== monthKey()) d = blank();\n"
+ "        const k = trk.t + '|' + trk.a;\n"
+ "        if(k !== lastKey) {\n"
+ "          lastKey = k; d.plays++;\n"
+ "          rec(trk.id).c++;\n"
+ "          const ar = d.artists[trk.a] || (d.artists[trk.a] = { c: 0, s: 0 }); ar.c++;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        if(isP) {\n"
+ "          const n = new Date();\n"
+ "          rec(trk.id).s += 5;\n"
+ "          (d.artists[trk.a] || (d.artists[trk.a] = { c: 0, s: 0 })).s += 5;\n"
+ "          d.sec += 5; d.days[n.getDate()] = (d.days[n.getDate()] || 0) + 5; d.hours[n.getHours()] = (d.hours[n.getHours()] || 0) + 5;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        save();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      setInterval(tick, 5000);\n"
+ "      return { get() { if(d.m !== monthKey()) d = blank(); return d; }, clear() { d = blank(); save(); }, reload() { load(); } };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    const StatsPg = {\n"
+ "      render() {\n"
+ "        const d = Stats.get(), n = new Date();\n"
+ "        const MN = ['Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni', 'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'];\n"
+ "        countUp($$('stHeroMin'), Math.round(d.sec / 60));\n"
+ "        $$('stHeroSub').textContent = 'Waktu mendengarkan \\u2022 ' + MN[n.getMonth()] + ' ' + n.getFullYear();\n"
+ "        countUp($$('stPlays'), d.plays); countUp($$('stArtists'), Object.keys(d.artists).length); countUp($$('stDays'), Object.keys(d.days).length);\n"
+ "        const dim = new Date(n.getFullYear(), n.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate();\n"
+ "        let max = 1; for(let i = 1; i <= dim; i++) max = Math.max(max, d.days[i] || 0);\n"
+ "        let bars = '';\n"
+ "        for(let i = 1; i <= dim; i++) bars += '<div class=\"stat-bar' + ((d.days[i] || 0) ? '' : ' zero') + '\" data-h=\"' + Math.max(4, Math.round(((d.days[i] || 0) / max) * 100)) + '\" title=\"' + i + ': ' + Math.round((d.days[i] || 0) / 60) + ' mnt\"></div>';\n"
+ "        $$('stBars').innerHTML = bars;\n"
+ "        $$('stAxis').innerHTML = '<span>1</span><span>' + Math.round(dim / 4) + '</span><span>' + Math.round(dim / 2) + '</span><span>' + Math.round(dim * 3 / 4) + '</span><span>' + dim + '</span>';\n"
+ "        setTimeout(() => { document.querySelectorAll('#stBars .stat-bar').forEach(b => { b.style.height = b.getAttribute('data-h') + '%'; }); }, 80);\n"
+ "        const ars = Object.keys(d.artists).map(k => ({ n: k, v: d.artists[k] })).sort((a, b) => (b.v.s + b.v.c * 10) - (a.v.s + a.v.c * 10)).slice(0, 5);\n"
+ "        const amax = ars.length ? (ars[0].v.s + ars[0].v.c * 10) || 1 : 1;\n"
+ "        $$('stTopArtists').innerHTML = ars.length ? ars.map((a, i) =>\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"st-row\"><div class=\"rank-badge\">' + (i + 1) + '</div><div class=\"st-meta\"><div class=\"st-t\">' + escHtml(a.n) + '</div><div class=\"st-s\">' + a.v.c + 'x diputar \\u2022 ' + Math.round(a.v.s / 60) + ' menit</div><div class=\"stat-hbar\"><i data-w=\"' + Math.round(((a.v.s + a.v.c * 10) / amax) * 100) + '\"></i></div></div></div>').join('') : '<div class=\"sp-p\">Belum ada data bulan ini. Putar lagu untuk mulai.</div>';\n"
+ "        const trs = Object.keys(d.tracks).map(k => d.tracks[k]).sort((a, b) => (b.c * 60 + b.s) - (a.c * 60 + a.s)).slice(0, 5);\n"
+ "        $$('stTopTracks').innerHTML = trs.length ? trs.map((t, i) =>\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"st-row\"><div class=\"rank-badge\">' + (i + 1) + '</div><img src=\"' + escHtml(t.l) + '\"/><div class=\"st-meta\"><div class=\"st-t\">' + escHtml(t.t) + '</div><div class=\"st-s\">' + escHtml(t.a) + ' \\u2022 ' + t.c + 'x \\u2022 ' + Math.round(t.s / 60) + ' mnt</div></div></div>').join('') : '<div class=\"sp-p\">Belum ada lagu.</div>';\n"
+ "        setTimeout(() => { document.querySelectorAll('#stTopArtists .stat-hbar i').forEach(b => { b.style.width = b.getAttribute('data-w') + '%'; }); }, 100);\n"
+ "        let bh = -1, bv = 0; Object.keys(d.hours).forEach(h => { if(d.hours[h] > bv) { bv = d.hours[h]; bh = +h; } });\n"
+ "        $$('stHour').textContent = bh >= 0 ? 'Kamu paling sering mendengarkan sekitar pukul ' + String(bh).padStart(2, '0') + ':00 - ' + String((bh + 1) % 24).padStart(2, '0') + ':00 (' + Math.round(bv / 60) + ' menit).' : 'Belum cukup data untuk menentukan jam favorit.';\n"
+ "        if(Priv.noHistory()) $$('stHour').textContent += ' (Penyamaran/riwayat dimatikan: data baru tidak dicatat)';\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      clear() { Stats.clear(); StatsPg.render(); }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "    Pages.hooks.pgStats = { open: StatsPg.render };\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- PERBANYAK: tampil 5, klik muncul 5 lagi\n"
+ "    const Pager = (function() {\n"
+ "      const st = {};\n"
+ "      function apply(id) {\n"
+ "        const s = st[id], el = $$(id);\n"
+ "        if(!el || !s || s.busy) return;\n"
+ "        s.busy = true;\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          const its = Array.from(el.children).filter(c => !c.classList.contains('pg-more') && !c.classList.contains('pg-more-card'));\n"
+ "          const sig = its.length + '|' + (its[0] ? (its[0].textContent || '').slice(0, 40) : '');\n"
+ "          if(sig !== s.sig) { s.sig = sig; s.shown = 5; }\n"
+ "          its.forEach((c, i) => c.classList.toggle('pg-hide', i >= s.shown));\n"
+ "          let more = el.querySelector('.pg-more, .pg-more-card');\n"
+ "          const rest = its.length - s.shown;\n"
+ "          if(rest > 0) {\n"
+ "            if(!more) {\n"
+ "              more = document.createElement('div');\n"
+ "              more.className = s.h ? 'pg-more-card' : 'pg-more';\n"
+ "              more.setAttribute('onclick', \"Pager.more('\" + id + \"')\");\n"
+ "              el.appendChild(more);\n"
+ "            }\n"
+ "            more.innerHTML = s.h ? '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-circle-plus\"></i><span>Perbanyak</span>' : '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-angles-down\"></i> Perbanyak (+' + Math.min(5, rest) + ' lagi)';\n"
+ "          } else if(more) more.remove();\n"
+ "        } finally { if(s.obs) s.obs.takeRecords(); s.busy = false; }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function watch(id, h) {\n"
+ "        const el = $$(id); if(!el) return;\n"

+ "        DD.make('ddLockMethod', [['off', 'Tidak ada'], ['pin', 'PIN'], ['bio', 'Biometrik (sidik jari / wajah) + PIN cadangan'], ['both', 'PIN + Biometrik']], () => Lock.method(), v => Lock.setMethod(v));\n"

+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // KUSTOM DYNAMIC ISLAND (ukuran, posisi, template + preview)\n"

+ "    const IslandCfg = (function() {\n"

+ "      let cfg = { cw: 0, ch: 36, ew: 0, eh: 160, x: 0, y: 0, tpl: 0, swdp: 360, canDraw: false, tv: false };\n"
+ "      let saved = null, dirty = false, testTimer = null;\n"
+ "      function read() {\n"
+ "        try { if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.getIslandConfig === 'function') return JSON.parse(window.AndroidInterface.getIslandConfig()); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('aether_island_cfg') || 'null') || {}; } catch(e) { return {}; }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function native(save) {\n"

+ "      }\n"
+ "      function labels() {\n"

+ "        const sw = Math.max(280, cfg.swdp || 360), sc = st.clientWidth / sw;\n"

+ "        const ew = cfg.ew > 0 ? cfg.ew : Math.min(sw - 16, 400);\n"

+ "        x.style.width = (ew * sc) + 'px'; x.style.height = (cfg.eh * sc) + 'px'; x.style.borderRadius = Math.min(cfg.eh * sc / 2, 40 * sc) + 'px';\n"

+ "      }\n"
+ "      function tplGrid() {\n"

+ "      }\n"
+ "      function load() {\n"

+ "        cfg.tpl = Math.max(0, Math.min(4, +cfg.tpl || 0));\n"

+ "      }\n"
+ "      function input() {\n"

+ "      }\n"

+ "      }\n"
+ "      function reset() { cfg.cw = 0; cfg.ch = 36; cfg.ew = 0; cfg.eh = 160; cfg.x = 0; cfg.y = 0; cfg.tpl = 0; dirty = true; sliders(); labels(); tplGrid(); mock(); native(false); toast('Dikembalikan ke bawaan. Tekan Simpan untuk menyimpan'); }\n"
+ "      function stopTest() {\n"

+ "      }\n"
+ "      function test(expanded) {\n"

+ "        Object.assign(cfg, read(), { cw: cfg.cw, ch: cfg.ch, ew: cfg.ew, eh: cfg.eh, x: cfg.x, y: cfg.y, tpl: cfg.tpl });\n"
+ "        try { cfg.canDraw = JSON.parse(window.AndroidInterface.getIslandConfig()).canDraw; } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        perm();\n"
+ "        if(!cfg.canDraw) { toast('Beri izin \"Tampil di atas aplikasi lain\" dulu'); return; }\n"
+ "        native(false);\n"
+ "        window.AndroidInterface.islandPreview(true, !!expanded);\n"
+ "        toast(expanded ? 'Preview expanded tampil di atas layar' : 'Preview compact tampil di atas layar');\n"
+ "        if(testTimer) clearTimeout(testTimer);\n"
+ "        testTimer = setTimeout(stopTest, 9000);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function askPerm() { window.__graceUntil = Date.now() + 60000; try { window.AndroidInterface.openOverlaySettings(); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "      function quickTpl(i) {\n"
+ "        const c = Object.assign({ cw: 0, ch: 36, ew: 0, eh: 160, x: 0, y: 0 }, read());\n"
+ "        cfg = Object.assign(cfg, c, { tpl: i });\n"
+ "        Cfg.set('islandTpl', i); native(true);\n"

+ "        DD.render('ddIslandTpl'); updSub();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function updSub() { const e = $$('accIslandSub'); if(e) e.textContent = 'Template ' + TPLS[Math.max(0, Math.min(4, +Cfg.get('islandTpl') || 0))]; }\n"
+ "      Pages.hooks.pgIsland = {\n"
+ "        open: load,\n"
+ "        close: () => {\n"

+ "          if(dirty && saved) { Object.assign(cfg, saved); native(false); toast('Perubahan belum disimpan dibatalkan'); dirty = false; }\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "      };\n"

+ "      read() { try { if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.getStorageInfo === 'function') StoragePg.info = JSON.parse(window.AndroidInterface.getStorageInfo()); } catch(e) { StoragePg.info = null; } },\n"
+ "      render() {\n"
+ "        StoragePg.read();\n"
+ "        const i = StoragePg.info || { used: 0, count: 0, limitMb: 1024, free: 0, total: 0, files: [], publicCopy: true };\n"

+ "        $$('stUsed').textContent = fmtBytes(i.used); $$('stCount').textContent = i.count;\n"

+ "        const avg = i.count ? i.used / i.count : 4.5 * 1048576;\n"
+ "        const room = i.limitMb > 0 ? Math.max(0, i.limitMb * 1048576 - i.used) : (i.free || 0);\n"
+ "        $$('stEst').textContent = 'Perkiraan muat sekitar ' + Math.floor(room / avg) + ' lagu lagi' + (i.limitMb > 0 && room <= 0 ? '. Penyimpanan penuh, naikkan batas untuk mengunduh lagi.' : '.');\n"
+ "        $$('tgStPublic').classList.toggle('on', !!i.publicCopy);\n"
+ "        DD.make('ddStLimit', [[512, '512 MB (\\u00B1120 lagu)'], [1024, '1 GB (\\u00B1240 lagu)'], [2048, '2 GB (\\u00B1480 lagu)'], [5120, '5 GB (\\u00B11.200 lagu)'], [10240, '10 GB'], [20480, '20 GB'], [0, 'Tanpa batas (sampai HP penuh)']], () => (StoragePg.info ? StoragePg.info.limitMb : 1024), v => StoragePg.setLimit(v));\n"
+ "        const meta = {}; offs.forEach(o => { meta[o.id] = o; });\n"
+ "        const files = (i.files || []).slice().sort((a, b) => b.mod - a.mod);\n"
+ "        $$('stList').innerHTML = files.length ? files.map(f => {\n"
+ "          const m = meta[f.id];\n"
+ "          return '<div class=\"st-row\">' + (m ? '<img src=\"' + escHtml(m.l) + '\"/>' : '<div class=\"ic\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-music\"></i></div>') +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"st-meta\"><div class=\"st-t\">' + escHtml(m ? m.t : f.id) + '</div><div class=\"st-s\">' + escHtml(m ? m.a : 'Lagu offline') + ' \\u2022 ' + fmtBytes(f.size) + '</div></div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"st-del\" onclick=\"StoragePg.del(\\'' + escHtml(f.id) + '\\')\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-trash-can\"></i></div></div>';\n"
+ "        }).join('') : '<div class=\"sp-p\">Belum ada lagu yang diunduh.</div>';\n"
+ "        const n = $$('navStorageSub'); if(n) n.textContent = 'Terpakai ' + fmtBytes(i.used) + ' \\u2022 ' + i.count + ' lagu offline';\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      setLimit(mb) {\n"
+ "        try { window.AndroidInterface.setStorageLimit(mb); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        if(StoragePg.info) StoragePg.info.limitMb = mb;\n"
+ "        toast(mb > 0 ? 'Batas unduhan diperluas' : 'Batas unduhan dimatikan');\n"
+ "        StoragePg.render();\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      togglePublic() {\n"
+ "        const on = !(StoragePg.info && StoragePg.info.publicCopy);\n"
+ "        try { window.AndroidInterface.setPublicCopy(on); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        if(StoragePg.info) StoragePg.info.publicCopy = on;\n"
+ "        $$('tgStPublic').classList.toggle('on', on);\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      forget(id) {\n"
+ "        offs = offs.filter(o => o.id !== id);\n"
+ "        try { localStorage.setItem('aether_off_meta', JSON.stringify(offs)); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        if(db) { try { db.transaction('trks', 'readwrite').objectStore('trks').delete(id); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      del(id) {\n"
+ "        let ok = false;\n"
+ "        try { ok = window.AndroidInterface.deleteOfflineFile(id); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        StoragePg.forget(id); updLibCounters(); StoragePg.render();\n"

+ "      },\n"
+ "      delAll() {\n"
+ "        Ask.open('Ketik HAPUS untuk menghapus semua unduhan', 'HAPUS', '', v => {\n"
+ "          if(v.toUpperCase() !== 'HAPUS') { toast('Dibatalkan'); return; }\n"
+ "          let n = 0; try { n = window.AndroidInterface.deleteAllOffline(); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "          offs = []; try { localStorage.setItem('aether_off_meta', '[]'); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "          if(db) { try { db.transaction('trks', 'readwrite').objectStore('trks').clear(); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "          updLibCounters(); StoragePg.render(); toast(n + ' unduhan dihapus');\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "      }\n"

'Pratinjau aplikasi terbaru disembunyikan' : 'Pratinjau aplikasi terbaru tampil', priv_notif_private: v ? 'Notifikasi privat di layar kunci' : 'Notifikasi tampil penuh', priv_nohist: v ? 'Riwayat & statistik tidak disimpan' : 'Riwayat & statistik disimpan', priv_anon_name: v ? 'Nama anonim di ruangan' : 'Nama asli di ruangan', priv_no_loc: v ?
