+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const Voice = (function() {\n"
+ "      let id = null, active = false;\n"
+ "      const ERR = { permission: 'Izin mikrofon ditolak', unavailable: 'Layanan suara tidak tersedia di HP ini', exception: 'Terjadi kesalahan mikrofon', '1': 'Koneksi internet lambat', '2': 'Tidak ada koneksi internet', '3': 'Masalah audio', '4': 'Server suara bermasalah', '5': 'Gagal memulai', '6': 'Tidak ada suara terdengar', '7': 'Suara tidak dikenali, coba lagi', '8': 'Perekam sibuk, coba lagi', '9': 'Izin mikrofon kurang' };\n"
+ "      function orb(on) { $$('vcOrb').classList.toggle('off', !on); }\n"
+ "      function start() {\n"
+ "        if(!(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.startVoiceSearch === 'function')) { toast('Pencarian suara hanya tersedia di aplikasi Android'); return; }\n"
+ "        id = 'v' + Date.now(); active = true; window.__graceUntil = Date.now() + 45000;\n"
+ "        $$('vcText').textContent = 'Sebutkan judul lagu atau nama artis'; $$('vcStatus').textContent = 'Menyiapkan mikrofon...'; orb(false);\n"
+ "        $$('vcCore').style.setProperty('--lv', 1);\n"
+ "        Pages.open('voiceMod');\n"
+ "        try { window.AndroidInterface.startVoiceSearch(id, Cfg.get('voiceLang') || ''); } catch(e) { fail('exception'); }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function fail(code) { active = false; orb(false); $$('vcStatus').textContent = ERR[code] || 'Gagal mendengar'; }\n"
+ "      function evt(rid, type, text) {\n"
+ "        if(rid !== id) return;\n"
+ "        if(type === 'ready') { $$('vcStatus').textContent = 'Mendengarkan...'; orb(true); }\n"
+ "        else if(type === 'begin') { $$('vcStatus').textContent = 'Suara terdeteksi'; }\n"
+ "        else if(type === 'level') { const r = Math.max(0, parseFloat(text) || 0); $$('vcCore').style.setProperty('--lv', (1 + Math.min(0.4, r / 25)).toFixed(2)); }\n"
+ "        else if(type === 'partial') { $$('vcText').textContent = text; }\n"
+ "        else if(type === 'end') { $$('vcStatus').textContent = 'Memproses...'; orb(false); }\n"
+ "        else if(type === 'final') { active = false; window.__graceUntil = Date.now() + 1500; Pages.close('voiceMod'); if(text) { swTab(1); if(LyricSearch.on) LyricSearch.run(text); else quickSearch(text); toast('Mencari: ' + text); } else toast('Suara tidak dikenali'); }\n"
+ "        else if(type === 'error') { fail(text); }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function stop() { Pages.close('voiceMod'); }\n"
+ "      Pages.hooks.voiceMod = { close: () => { if(active) { try { window.AndroidInterface.stopVoiceSearch(); } catch(e) {} } active = false; id = null; } };\n"
+ "      window.onNativeVoice = evt;\n"
+ "      return { start: start, stop: stop };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // KUNCI APLIKASI (PIN + biometrik)\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const Lock = (function() {\n"
+ "      let cfg = { method: 'off', delay: 30, salt: '', hash: '', alg: 'sha', len: 4 };\n"
+ "      let st = { mode: 'unlock', buf: '', first: '', fails: 0, until: 0, done: null };\n"
+ "      let leftAt = 0, bioBusy = false, bioCb = {};\n"
+ "      const el = id => document.getElementById(id);\n"
+ "      function load() {\n"
+ "        let s = '';\n"

+ "        const s = p.tracks.reduce((a, t) => a + (+t.d || 0), 0), h = Math.floor(s / 3600), m = Math.round((s % 3600) / 60);\n"
+ "        return h ? h + ' j ' + m + ' mnt' : m + ' mnt';\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      render(p) {\n"
+ "        const head = $$('plSpHead'), list = $$('plSpList');\n"
+ "        $$('plBtnsDef').style.display = 'none'; $$('plTracks').style.display = 'none';\n"
+ "        head.style.setProperty('--spx-h', SpxView.hue(p.name));\n"
+ "        head.innerHTML = '<img class=\"spx-cover\" src=\"' + escHtml(p.cover || (p.tracks[0] && p.tracks[0].l) || '') + '\"/>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"spx-name\">' + escHtml(p.name) + '</div>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"spx-owner\"><span class=\"spx-av\"><i class=\"fa-brands fa-spotify\"></i></span>' + escHtml(p.owner || 'Spotify') + '</div>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"spx-meta\">' + (p.type === 'album' ? 'Album' : 'Playlist') + ' \\u2022 ' + p.tracks.length + ' lagu \\u2022 ' + SpxView.total(p) + '</div>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"spx-actions\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-rotate spx-ic\" title=\"Perbarui dari Spotify\" onclick=\"SpxView.reimport()\"></i>' +\n"
+ "          '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-trash-can spx-ic\" onclick=\"PL.del()\"></i><div class=\"spx-grow\"></div>' +\n"
+ "          '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-shuffle spx-ic' + (SpxView.shuffle ? ' on' : '') + '\" id=\"spxShuf\" onclick=\"SpxView.toggleShuffle()\"></i>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"spx-play\" onclick=\"SpxView.playAll()\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-play\"></i></div></div>';\n"
+ "        list.style.display = 'block';\n"
+ "        list.innerHTML = p.tracks.map((t, i) =>\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"spx-row\" style=\"animation-delay:' + Math.min(i, 14) * 0.03 + 's\" onclick=\"SpxView.play(' + i + ')\"><div class=\"spx-n\">' + (i + 1) + '</div>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"spx-info\"><div class=\"spx-t\">' + escHtml(t.t) + '</div><div class=\"spx-a\">' + (t.id ? '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-circle-check\"></i>' : '') + escHtml(t.a) + '</div></div>' +\n"
+ "          '<div class=\"spx-d\">' + SpxView.dur(t.d) + '</div></div>').join('');\n"
+ "        SpxView.mark();\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      hide() {\n"
+ "        const head = $$('plSpHead'); if(head) head.innerHTML = '';\n"
+ "        const list = $$('plSpList'); if(list) { list.innerHTML = ''; list.style.display = 'none'; }\n"
+ "        const d = $$('plBtnsDef'); if(d) d.style.display = '';\n"
+ "        const t = $$('plTracks'); if(t) t.style.display = '';\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      cur() { try { return PL.find(PL.cur()); } catch(e) { return null; } },\n"
+ "      play(i) { const p = SpxView.cur(); if(!p) return; q = p.tracks.slice(); play(i); },\n"
+ "      playAll() { PL.playAll(SpxView.shuffle); },\n"
+ "      toggleShuffle() { SpxView.shuffle = !SpxView.shuffle; const e = $$('spxShuf'); if(e) e.classList.toggle('on', SpxView.shuffle); toast(SpxView.shuffle ? 'Acak aktif' : 'Acak mati'); },\n"
+ "      reimport() { const p = SpxView.cur(); if(p && p.src) { SpImport.open(); $$('spiInp').value = p.src; SpImport.go(); } },\n"
+ "      mark() {\n"
+ "        const p = SpxView.cur(); if(!p) return;\n"
+ "        document.querySelectorAll('#plSpList .spx-row').forEach((r, i) => { const t = p.tracks[i]; r.classList.toggle('playing', !!(t && t.id && typeof trk !== 'undefined' && trk && trk.id === t.id)); });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "    setInterval(() => { if(Pages.top() === 'pgPlaylists') SpxView.mark(); }, 1200);\n"
+ "    (function() {\n"
+ "      const _po = PL.open;\n"
+ "      PL.open = function(id) { _po(id); const p = PL.find(id); if(p && p.spotify) SpxView.render(p); else SpxView.hide(); };\n"
+ "      const _ho = Pages.hooks.pgPlaylists.open;\n"
+ "      Pages.hooks.pgPlaylists.open = function() { _ho(); SpxView.hide(); };\n"

+ "    // LINK MASUK (klik link musik / bagikan dari Spotify -> buka AETHER)\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const Links = {\n"
+ "      pending: '',\n"
+ "      queue(l) { Links.pending = l; Links.tryRun(); },\n"
+ "      tryRun() {\n"
+ "        if(!Links.pending) return;\n"
+ "        const sp = $$('splash');\n"
+ "        if(Lock.visible() || (sp && !sp.classList.contains('hidden'))) { setTimeout(Links.tryRun, 900); return; }\n"
+ "        const l = Links.pending; Links.pending = '';\n"
+ "        Links.open(l);\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      ytId(s) {\n"
+ "        const pats = [/[?&]id=([\\w-]{11})/, /youtu\\.be\\/([\\w-]{11})/, /[?&]v=([\\w-]{11})/, /shorts\\/([\\w-]{11})/, /aether:\\/\\/play\\/?\\??.*id=([\\w-]{11})/];\n"
+ "        for(const p of pats) { const m = s.match(p); if(m) return m[1]; }\n"
+ "        return null;\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      async open(raw) {\n"
+ "        raw = String(raw || '').trim();\n"
+ "        if(SpImport.isSpotify(raw)) {\n"
+ "          const m = raw.match(/https?:\\/\\/[^\\s]+/); const url = m ? m[0] : raw;\n"
+ "          Pages.open('pgPlaylists'); SpImport.open(); $$('spiInp').value = url; SpImport.go();\n"
+ "          return;\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        const id = Links.ytId(raw);\n"
+ "        if(!id) { toast('Link tidak dikenali'); return; }\n"
+ "        toast('Membuka lagu dari link...');\n"
+ "        let tr = { id: id, t: 'Lagu dari link', a: 'AETHER', l: 'https://i.ytimg.com/vi/' + id + '/hqdefault.jpg' };\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          const u = 'https://www.youtube.com/oembed?format=json&url=' + encodeURIComponent('https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=' + id);\n"
+ "          let d = null;\n"
+ "          try { const r = await fetch(u); if(r.ok) d = await r.json(); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "          if(d && d.title) {\n"
+ "            tr.t = String(d.title).replace(/\\(Official.*?\\)|\\[Official.*?\\]|Official Music Video|Official Video|Official Audio|Lyric Video|Lyrics/gi, '').trim().slice(0, 60) || tr.t;\n"
+ "            tr.a = String(d.author_name || 'AETHER').replace(/ - Topic|VEVO/gi, '').trim().slice(0, 40);\n"
+ "            if(tr.t.indexOf(' - ') > 0) { const p = tr.t.split(' - '); tr.a = p[0].trim(); tr.t = p.slice(1).join(' - ').trim(); }\n"
+ "          }\n"
+ "        } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        swTab(0);\n"
+ "        q = [tr]; play(0);\n"
+ "        setTimeout(() => { try { openFullPlayer(); } catch(e) {} }, 500);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "    window.checkIncomingLink = function() {\n"

+ "    };\n"

+ "    // CARI LAGU DARI LIRIK\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const LyricSearch = (function() {\n"
+ "      let on = false, timer = null, tok = 0;\n"
+ "      const norm = s => String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\\u00c0-\\uffff\\s]/g, ' ').replace(/\\s+/g, ' ').trim();\n"
+ "      function score(frag, lines) {\n"
+ "        const f = norm(frag), ft = f.split(' ').filter(w => w.length > 1);\n"
+ "        let best = { s: 0, line: '' };\n"
+ "        if(!ft.length) return best;\n"
+ "        const L = lines.map(norm);\n"
+ "        for(let i = 0; i < L.length; i++) {\n"
+ "          for(let w = 1; w <= 2; w++) {\n"
+ "            const seg = w === 1 ? L[i] : (L[i] + ' ' + (L[i + 1] || ''));\n"
+ "            if(!seg.trim()) continue;\n"
+ "            let s;\n"
+ "            if(seg.indexOf(f) >= 0) s = 1;\n"
+ "            else { const set = new Set(seg.split(' ')); let hit = 0; ft.forEach(t => { if(set.has(t)) hit++; }); s = (hit / ft.length) * 0.92; }\n"
+ "            if(s > best.s) best = { s: s, line: lines[i] };\n"
+ "          }\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        return best;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function run(text) {\n"
+ "        text = String(text || '').trim(); if(!text) return;\n"
+ "        const my = ++tok;\n"
+ "        $$('searchBrowseWrap').style.display = 'none'; $$('searchResultsWrap').style.display = 'block'; $$('clearSearch').style.display = 'block';\n"
+ "        $$('listSearch').innerHTML = Skel.rows(5);\n"
+ "        const cands = (await search(text + ' lyrics', false, 15) || []).slice(0, 8);\n"
+ "        if(my !== tok) return;\n"
+ "        const out = await Promise.all(cands.map(async c => {\n"
+ "          let sc = { s: 0, line: '' };\n"
+ "          try {\n"
+ "            const d = await LY.fetchFor({ t: c.t, a: c.a });\n"
+ "            const lines = d.lines.length ? d.lines.map(x => x.s) : (d.plain ? d.plain.split('\\n').filter(x => x.trim()) : []);\n"
+ "            if(lines.length) sc = score(text, lines);\n"
+ "          } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "          return Object.assign({}, c, { score: sc.s, line: sc.line });\n"
+ "        }));\n"
+ "        if(my !== tok) return;\n"
+ "        out.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);\n"
+ "        const box = $$('listSearch'); box.innerHTML = '';\n"
+ "        if(!out.length) { box.innerHTML = '<div style=\"text-align:center; padding:20px; color:var(--text-sub);\">Tidak ada lagu yang cocok dengan lirik itu</div>'; return; }\n"
+ "        window._lastList = out;\n"
+ "        out.forEach((t, i) => {\n"
+ "          const pct = Math.round(t.score * 100);\n"
+ "          const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'track liquid-glass';\n"
+ "          d.innerHTML = '<img src=\"' + escHtml(t.l) + '\"><div class=\"track-info\"><div class=\"track-title ellipsis\">' + escHtml(t.t) + '</div><div class=\"track-artist ellipsis\">' + escHtml(t.a) + '</div>' +\n"
+ "            (t.line ? '<div class=\"ly-match\">\\u201C' + escHtml(String(t.line).slice(0, 70)) + '\\u201D</div>' : '') + '</div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"ly-badge' + (pct < 45 ? ' low' : '') + '\">' + (pct >= 45 ? 'Cocok ' + pct + '%' : 'Mungkin') + '</div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"track-more\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); openTrackMod(' + i + ')\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-ellipsis-vertical\"></i></div>';\n"
+ "          d.onclick = () => { q = out; play(i); };\n"
+ "          box.appendChild(d);\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function setMode(b) {\n"
+ "        on = !!b;\n"
+ "        $$('smTitle').classList.toggle('active', !on); $$('smLyric').classList.toggle('active', on);\n"
+ "        $$('sInp').placeholder = on ? 'Tulis potongan lirik yang kamu ingat...' : 'Apa yang ingin kamu dengarkan?';\n"
+ "        const h = document.querySelector('#searchResultsWrap .sec-title'); if(h) h.textContent = on ? 'Lagu yang cocok dengan lirik' : 'Hasil Pencarian (< 10 Menit)';\n"
+ "        if($$('sInp').value.trim()) { if(on) run($$('sInp').value); else quickSearch($$('sInp').value.trim()); }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      document.addEventListener('input', e => {\n"
+ "        if(!on || !e.target || e.target.id !== 'sInp') return;\n"
+ "        e.stopImmediatePropagation();\n"
+ "        const v = e.target.value.trim(); clearTimeout(timer);\n"
+ "        if(!v) { resetSearch(); return; }\n"
+ "        $$('clearSearch').style.display = 'block'; $$('searchBrowseWrap').style.display = 'none'; $$('searchResultsWrap').style.display = 'block';\n"
+ "        $$('listSearch').innerHTML = Skel.rows(5);\n"
+ "        timer = setTimeout(() => run(v), 1000);\n"
+ "      }, true);\n"
+ "      document.addEventListener('keypress', e => {\n"
+ "        if(!on || !e.target || e.target.id !== 'sInp' || e.key !== 'Enter') return;\n"
+ "        e.stopImmediatePropagation(); clearTimeout(timer);\n"
+ "        const v = e.target.value.trim(); if(v) run(v);\n"
+ "      }, true);\n"
+ "      return { run: run, setMode: setMode, get on() { return on; } };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"

+ "      save() { try { localStorage.setItem('aether_artist_plays', JSON.stringify(ArtistStore.data)); } catch(e) {} },\n"

+ "        const x = o.tr[t.id] || (o.tr[t.id] = { id: t.id, t: t.t, a: t.a, l: t.l, c: 0 });\n"

+ "    // HALLOWEEN: jaring laba-laba, kelelawar, hantu, laba-laba bergelantungan, labu\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    function webSvg() {\n"
+ "      const R = [34, 68, 102, 136, 170], A = [0, 18, 36, 54, 72, 90];\n"
+ "      let d = '';\n"
+ "      A.forEach(a => { const r = a * Math.PI / 180; d += 'M0 0 L' + (180 * Math.cos(r)).toFixed(1) + ' ' + (180 * Math.sin(r)).toFixed(1) + ' '; });\n"
+ "      R.forEach(rad => {\n"
+ "        for(let i = 0; i < A.length - 1; i++) {\n"
+ "          const a1 = A[i] * Math.PI / 180, a2 = A[i + 1] * Math.PI / 180, am = (a1 + a2) / 2;\n"
+ "          d += 'M' + (rad * Math.cos(a1)).toFixed(1) + ' ' + (rad * Math.sin(a1)).toFixed(1) + ' Q' + (rad * 0.86 * Math.cos(am)).toFixed(1) + ' ' + (rad * 0.86 * Math.sin(am)).toFixed(1) + ' ' + (rad * Math.cos(a2)).toFixed(1) + ' ' + (rad * Math.sin(a2)).toFixed(1) + ' ';\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "      });\n"
+ "      return '<svg viewBox=\"0 0 185 185\" xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\"><path d=\"' + d + '\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"rgba(255,255,255,0.75)\" stroke-width=\"1.1\" stroke-linecap=\"round\"/><circle cx=\"2\" cy=\"2\" r=\"3\" fill=\"rgba(255,255,255,0.8)\"/></svg>';\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "    function fxHalloween(on) {\n"
+ "      let top = document.getElementById('hwTop');\n"
+ "      if(!on) { if(top) top.remove(); return; }\n"
+ "      if(!top) { top = document.createElement('div'); top.id = 'hwTop'; document.body.appendChild(top); }\n"
+ "      let h = '<div class=\"hw-web l\">' + webSvg() + '</div><div class=\"hw-web r\">' + webSvg() + '</div>';\n"
+ "      for(let i = 0; i < 4; i++) h += '<div class=\"hw-bat\" style=\"top:' + (6 + i * 9) + '%;animation-duration:' + (11 + i * 4) + 's;animation-delay:-' + (i * 4) + 's\">\\uD83E\\uDD87</div>';\n"
+ "      for(let i = 0; i < 3; i++) h += '<div class=\"hw-ghost\" style=\"left:' + (12 + i * 34) + '%;top:' + (30 + i * 14) + '%;animation-duration:' + (5 + i * 2) + 's;animation-delay:-' + i + 's\">\\uD83D\\uDC7B</div>';\n"
+ "      [[22, 3.4], [58, 4.6], [84, 3.9]].forEach(s => { h += '<div class=\"hw-spider\" style=\"left:' + s[0] + '%;animation-duration:' + s[1] + 's\"><span>\\uD83D\\uDD77\\uFE0F</span></div>'; });\n"
+ "      [[5, 0], [46, 1], [90, 2]].forEach(s => { h += '<div class=\"hw-pump\" style=\"left:' + s[0] + '%;animation-delay:-' + s[1] + 's\">\\uD83C\\uDF83</div>'; });\n"
+ "      top.innerHTML = h;\n"
+ "    }\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // GESTUR TUTUP PLAYER (geser ke bawah)\n"

+ "    (function() {\n"

+ "    const SYNC_KEYS = ['aether_f', 'aether_rec', 'aether_play_counts', 'aether_playlists', 'aether_artist_plays', 'aether_stats_v1', 'aether_streak', 'aether_cfg'];\n"
+ "    const uniqBy = (arr, k) => { const s = {}; return arr.filter(x => x && x[k] && !s[x[k]] && (s[x[k]] = 1)); };\n"
+ "    const CloudMerge = {\n"
+ "      aether_f: (l, r) => uniqBy((l || []).concat(r || []), 'id'),\n"
+ "      aether_rec: (l, r) => uniqBy((l || []).concat(r || []), 'id').slice(0, 12),\n"
+ "      aether_play_counts: (l, r) => { const o = Object.assign({}, r || {}); Object.keys(l || {}).forEach(k => { if(!o[k] || (l[k].count || 0) > (o[k].count || 0)) o[k] = l[k]; }); return o; },\n"
+ "      aether_playlists: (l, r) => {\n"
+ "        const m = {}; (r || []).forEach(p => { m[p.id] = p; });\n"
+ "        (l || []).forEach(p => {\n"
+ "          const o = m[p.id];\n"
+ "          if(!o) { m[p.id] = p; return; }\n"
+ "          const keyOf = t => t.id || ((t.a || '') + '|' + (t.t || ''));\n"
+ "          const seen = {}; const tr = [];\n"
+ "          (p.tracks || []).concat(o.tracks || []).forEach(t => { const k = keyOf(t); if(!seen[k]) { seen[k] = 1; tr.push(t); } });\n"
+ "          m[p.id] = Object.assign({}, o, p, { tracks: tr });\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        return Object.keys(m).map(k => m[k]);\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      aether_artist_plays: (l, r) => {\n"
+ "        const o = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(r || {}));\n"
+ "        Object.keys(l || {}).forEach(a => {\n"
+ "          const x = l[a], y = o[a];\n"
+ "          if(!y) { o[a] = x; return; }\n"
+ "          y.c = Math.max(y.c || 0, x.c || 0); y.l = x.l || y.l;\n"
+ "          Object.keys(x.tr || {}).forEach(id => { const t = x.tr[id], u = (y.tr = y.tr || {})[id]; if(!u || (t.c || 0) > (u.c || 0)) y.tr[id] = t; });\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        return o;\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      aether_stats_v1: (l, r) => !l ? r : (!r ? l : (l.m !== r.m ? (l.m > r.m ? l : r) : ((l.sec || 0) >= (r.sec || 0) ? l : r))),\n"
+ "      aether_streak: (l, r) => {\n"
+ "        const o = { days: Object.assign({}, (r || {}).days || {}), best: Math.max((l || {}).best || 0, (r || {}).best || 0), celebrated: (l || {}).celebrated || '' };\n"
+ "        Object.keys((l || {}).days || {}).forEach(k => { o.days[k] = Math.max(o.days[k] || 0, l.days[k]); });\n"
+ "        return o;\n"
+ "      },\n"
+ "      aether_cfg: (l, r) => Object.assign({}, l || {}, r || {})\n"
+ "    };\n"
+ "    const Cloud = (function() {\n"
+ "      let uid = null, timer = null, lastHash = '', busy = false, name = '', email = '';\n"
+ "      const ls = k => { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch(e) { return null; } };\n"
+ "      const parse = s => { try { return s ? JSON.parse(s) : null; } catch(e) { return null; } };\n"
+ "      function snap() { const o = {}; SYNC_KEYS.forEach(k => { const v = ls(k); if(v) o[k] = v; }); return o; }\n"
+ "      function hashOf(o) { const s = JSON.stringify(o); let h = 7; for(let i = 0; i < s.length; i += 7) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) | 0; return s.length + ':' + h; }\n"

+ "      }\n"
+ "      function merge(remote) {\n"
+ "        SYNC_KEYS.forEach(k => {\n"
+ "          if(!remote[k]) return;\n"
+ "          const l = parse(ls(k)), r = parse(remote[k]);\n"
+ "          const m = CloudMerge[k] ? CloudMerge[k](l, r) : r;\n"
+ "          try { localStorage.setItem(k, JSON.stringify(m)); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        applyLive();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function push(force) {\n"
+ "        if(!uid || busy) return;\n"
+ "        const s = snap(), h = hashOf(s);\n"
+ "        if(!force && h === lastHash) return;\n"
+ "        busy = true;\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          let blob = JSON.stringify(s);\n"
+ "          if(blob.length > 900000) { const st = parse(s.aether_stats_v1); if(st) { st.tracks = {}; s.aether_stats_v1 = JSON.stringify(st); blob = JSON.stringify(s); } }\n"
+ "          await ref().set({ name: name, email: email, updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), blob: blob }, { merge: true });\n"
+ "          lastHash = h;\n"
+ "          const n = new Date(); setSync('Tersinkron ' + String(n.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(n.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'));\n"
+ "        } catch(e) { setSync('Belum tersinkron (' + (e.code || 'offline') + ')'); }\n"
+ "        busy = false;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function start(u) {\n"
+ "        uid = u.uid; name = u.displayName || ''; email = u.email || '';\n"
+ "        setSync('Menyinkronkan...');\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          const d = await ref().get();\n"
+ "          if(d.exists && d.data().blob) { const remote = parse(d.data().blob); if(remote) merge(remote); }\n"
+ "        } catch(e) { setSync('Gagal membaca cloud (' + (e.code || 'offline') + '). Cek aturan Firestore.'); }\n"
+ "        await push(true);\n"
+ "        if(timer) clearInterval(timer);\n"
+ "        timer = setInterval(() => push(false), 8000);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function stop() { uid = null; if(timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; } lastHash = ''; }\n"
+ "      return { start: start, stop: stop, push: push, syncNow(show) { if(!uid) { toast('Masuk dulu untuk sinkronisasi'); return; } lastHash = ''; push(true).then(() => { if(show) toast('Sinkronisasi selesai'); }); }, active: () => !!uid };\n"
+ "    })();\n"

+ "      function save() { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(d)); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "      const goalSec = () => (Cfg.get('streakGoal') || 5) * 60;\n"
+ "      function calc() {\n"
+ "        const g = goalSec(), t = key(new Date()), met = (d.days[t] || 0) >= g;\n"
+ "        const dt = new Date(); if(!met) dt.setDate(dt.getDate() - 1);\n"
+ "        let s = 0; while((d.days[key(dt)] || 0) >= g) { s++; dt.setDate(dt.getDate() - 1); }\n"
+ "        return { streak: s, met: met, sec: d.days[t] || 0, goal: g };\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function level(n) { let l = LV[0]; LV.forEach(x => { if(n >= x[0]) l = x; }); return l; }\n"
+ "      function weekDays() {\n"
+ "        const out = [], g = goalSec(); const dt = new Date(); dt.setDate(dt.getDate() - 6);\n"
+ "        for(let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { out.push({ on: (d.days[key(dt)] || 0) >= g, ch: ['M', 'S', 'S', 'R', 'K', 'J', 'S'][dt.getDay()] }); dt.setDate(dt.getDate() + 1); }\n"
+ "        return out;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function render() {\n"
+ "        const c = calc(), pct = Math.min(100, Math.round(c.sec / c.goal * 100));\n"
+ "        const set = (id, v) => { const e = $$(id); if(e) e.textContent = v; };\n"

+ "        $$('skGoalBar').style.width = Math.min(100, Math.round(c.sec / c.goal * 100)) + '%';\n"
+ "        const n = new Date(), first = new Date(n.getFullYear(), n.getMonth(), 1), dim = new Date(n.getFullYear(), n.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate();\n"
+ "        const MN = ['Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni', 'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'];\n"
+ "        $$('skMonth').textContent = MN[n.getMonth()] + ' ' + n.getFullYear();\n"
+ "        let h = ['M', 'S', 'S', 'R', 'K', 'J', 'S'].map(x => '<div class=\"hd\">' + x + '</div>').join('');\n"
+ "        for(let i = 0; i < first.getDay(); i++) h += '<div style=\"background:none\"></div>';\n"
+ "        for(let i = 1; i <= dim; i++) { const k = n.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(n.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(i); h += '<div class=\"' + ((d.days[k] || 0) >= c.goal ? 'on' : '') + (i === n.getDate() ? ' today' : '') + '\">' + ((d.days[k] || 0) >= c.goal ? '\\uD83D\\uDD25' : i) + '</div>'; }\n"
+ "        $$('skCal').innerHTML = h;\n"
+ "        const days = Object.keys(d.days).filter(k => d.days[k] >= c.goal).length;\n"
+ "        $$('skBest').textContent = Math.max(d.best || 0, c.streak); $$('skTotal').textContent = days; $$('skMin').textContent = Math.floor(c.sec / 60);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function pop(n) {\n"
+ "        $$('spNum').textContent = Math.max(1, n - 1); $$('streakPop').classList.add('show');\n"
+ "        const lv = level(n);\n"
+ "        $$('spTx').textContent = lv[1] + '! ' + n + ' hari berturut-turut mendengarkan musik';\n"
+ "        setTimeout(() => { countUp($$('spNum'), n); confetti(110); }, 600);\n"
+ "        try { if(navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([60, 40, 90]); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function tick() {\n"
+ "        if(typeof trk === 'undefined' || !trk || !isP || Priv.noHistory()) return;\n"
+ "        const t = key(new Date()); d.days[t] = (d.days[t] || 0) + 5;\n"
+ "        const c = calc();\n"
+ "        if(c.streak > (d.best || 0)) d.best = c.streak;\n"
+ "        if(c.met && d.celebrated !== t) { d.celebrated = t; save(); render(); pop(c.streak); return; }\n"
+ "        save(); render();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      setInterval(tick, 5000);\n"
+ "      Pages.hooks.pgStreak = { open: () => page() };\n"
+ "      load();\n"
+ "      return { render: render, reload: () => { load(); render(); }, calc: calc, level: level, weekDays: weekDays, closePop: () => $$('streakPop').classList.remove('show'), data: () => d, goalSec: goalSec };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // KARTU JPG: streak & statistik (unduh / bagikan)\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const Share = (function() {\n"
+ "      function flame(ctx, cx, cy, s) {\n"
+ "        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - s);\n"
+ "        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx + s * 0.35, cy - s * 0.55, cx + s * 0.78, cy - s * 0.1, cx + s * 0.55, cy + s * 0.45);\n"
+ "        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx + s * 0.45, cy + s * 0.85, cx + s * 0.15, cy + s, cx, cy + s);\n"
+ "        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx - s * 0.15, cy + s, cx - s * 0.5, cy + s * 0.85, cx - s * 0.55, cy + s * 0.45);\n"
+ "        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx - s * 0.72, cy + s * 0.05, cx - s * 0.2, cy - s * 0.3, cx, cy - s); ctx.closePath();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function bigFlame(ctx, cx, cy, s, col) {\n"
+ "        ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = 90;\n"
+ "        let g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, cy - s, 0, cy + s); g.addColorStop(0, '#ffd23f'); g.addColorStop(0.5, col); g.addColorStop(1, '#ff2d55');\n"
+ "        ctx.fillStyle = g; flame(ctx, cx, cy, s); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n"
+ "        g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, cy, 0, cy + s); g.addColorStop(0, '#fff3b0'); g.addColorStop(1, '#ffb347');\n"
+ "        ctx.fillStyle = g; flame(ctx, cx, cy + s * 0.28, s * 0.58); ctx.fill();\n"
+ "        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)'; flame(ctx, cx, cy + s * 0.5, s * 0.28); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function bg(ctx, w, h, c1, c2) {\n"
+ "        const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h); g.addColorStop(0, c1); g.addColorStop(1, c2); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);\n"
+ "        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.07)';\n"
+ "        for(let y = 20; y < h; y += 34) for(let x = (y / 34 % 2) * 17; x < w; x += 34) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 2.2, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); }\n"
+ "        const r = ctx.createRadialGradient(w / 2, h * 0.3, 40, w / 2, h * 0.3, w * 0.8); r.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)'); r.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); ctx.fillStyle = r; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function rr(ctx, x, y, w, h, r) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + r, y); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r); ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); ctx.closePath(); }\n"
+ "      function who() { try { const u = firebase.auth().currentUser; if(u && !u.isAnonymous && u.displayName) return u.displayName; } catch(e) {} return 'AETHER Listener'; }\n"
+ "      function out(cv, name, share) {\n"
+ "        const url = cv.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.92);\n"
+ "        if(window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.saveImage === 'function') {\n"
+ "          const r = window.AndroidInterface.saveImage(url.split(',')[1], name, !!share);\n"
+ "          toast(r === 'OK' ? (share ? 'Membuka menu bagikan...' : 'Tersimpan di Galeri (Pictures/AETHER)') : 'Gagal menyimpan gambar. Izinkan penyimpanan');\n"
+ "        } else { const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = name; a.click(); }\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function streak(share) {\n"
+ "        try { await document.fonts.ready; } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        const c = Streak.calc(), lv = Streak.level(c.streak), W = 1080, H = 1350;\n"
+ "        const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H; const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');\n"
+ "        bg(ctx, W, H, '#1a0716', lv[2] === '#00e5ff' ? '#06384a' : '#5a1400');\n"
+ "        ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)'; ctx.font = '800 40px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('A E T H E R   M U S I C', W / 2, 110);\n"
+ "        bigFlame(ctx, W / 2, 470, 250, lv[2]);\n"
+ "        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.font = '900 300px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30; ctx.fillText(String(c.streak), W / 2, 960); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;\n"
+ "        ctx.font = '800 54px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)'; ctx.fillText('HARI BERTURUT-TURUT', W / 2, 1040);\n"
+ "        ctx.font = '900 40px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; const tw = ctx.measureText(lv[1].toUpperCase()).width + 80; ctx.fillStyle = lv[2]; rr(ctx, W / 2 - tw / 2, 1075, tw, 70, 35); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#000'; ctx.fillText(lv[1].toUpperCase(), W / 2, 1124);\n"
+ "        const wk = Streak.weekDays(); wk.forEach((x, i) => {\n"
+ "          const cx = W / 2 - 3 * 120 + i * 120, cy = 1230;\n"
+ "          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, 36, 0, 7); ctx.fillStyle = x.on ? lv[2] : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.14)'; ctx.fill();\n"
+ "          if(x.on) { ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; flame(ctx, cx, cy, 20); ctx.fill(); } else { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)'; ctx.font = '800 28px sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(x.ch, cx, cy + 10); }\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)'; ctx.font = '700 34px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(who() + '  \\u2022  rajin dengerin lagu \\uD83C\\uDFA7', W / 2, 1310);\n"
+ "        out(cv, 'AETHER-streak-' + c.streak + 'hari.jpg', share);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      async function stats(share) {\n"
+ "        try { await document.fonts.ready; } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        const d = Stats.get(), W = 1080, H = 1350, MN = ['Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni', 'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'];\n"
+ "        const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H; const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');\n"
+ "        bg(ctx, W, H, '#0b3d2a', '#2b1066');\n"
+ "        ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)'; ctx.font = '800 38px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('REKAP MUSIK  \\u2022  ' + MN[new Date().getMonth()].toUpperCase() + ' ' + new Date().getFullYear(), W / 2, 110);\n"
+ "        ctx.fillStyle = '#1ed760'; ctx.font = '900 320px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(String(Math.round(d.sec / 60)), W / 2, 420);\n"
+ "        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.font = '800 56px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('MENIT MENDENGARKAN', W / 2, 500);\n"
+ "        const cards = [[d.plays, 'Lagu'], [Object.keys(d.artists).length, 'Artis'], [Object.keys(d.days).length, 'Hari aktif']];\n"
+ "        cards.forEach((x, i) => { const cx = 190 + i * 350; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)'; rr(ctx, cx - 150, 560, 300, 160, 28); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#1ed760'; ctx.font = '900 70px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(String(x[0]), cx, 650); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)'; ctx.font = '700 30px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(x[1], cx, 695); });\n"
+ "        ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.font = '900 44px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('Artis teratas', 90, 810);\n"
+ "        const ars = Object.keys(d.artists).map(k => ({ n: k, v: d.artists[k] })).sort((a, b) => (b.v.s + b.v.c * 10) - (a.v.s + a.v.c * 10)).slice(0, 5);\n"
+ "        const mx = ars.length ? (ars[0].v.s + ars[0].v.c * 10) || 1 : 1;\n"
+ "        ars.forEach((a, i) => {\n"
+ "          const y = 860 + i * 78;\n"
+ "          ctx.fillStyle = '#1ed760'; ctx.font = '900 40px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(String(i + 1), 90, y + 34);\n"
+ "          ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.font = '800 36px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; let nm = a.n; while(ctx.measureText(nm).width > 520 && nm.length > 3) nm = nm.slice(0, -2); ctx.fillText(nm === a.n ? nm : nm + '...', 150, y + 30);\n"
+ "          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.14)'; rr(ctx, 700, y + 8, 290, 22, 11); ctx.fill();\n"
+ "          ctx.fillStyle = '#1ed760'; rr(ctx, 700, y + 8, Math.max(24, 290 * ((a.v.s + a.v.c * 10) / mx)), 22, 11); ctx.fill();\n"
+ "        });\n"
+ "        ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)'; ctx.font = '700 34px \"Plus Jakarta Sans\",sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(who() + '  \\u2022  AETHER Music v4.3', W / 2, 1300);\n"
+ "        out(cv, 'AETHER-statistik.jpg', share);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      return { streak: streak, stats: stats };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // MUSIC SHORTS ala TikTok (klip rekomendasi, tap = dengar full)\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    const Shorts = (function() {\n"
+ "      let items = [], cur = -1, yt = null, ytOk = false, startSec = 0, full = false, obs = null, loading = false, rendered = 0, lastTap = 0, tapT = null, seenIds = {};\n"
+ "      const feed = () => $$('shFeed');\n"
+ "      const hash = s => { let h = 0; for(let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0; return h; };\n"
+ "      const color = s => 'hsl(' + (hash(s) % 360) + ',75%,62%)';\n"
+ "      function saveCache() { try { localStorage.setItem('aether_shorts_cache', JSON.stringify(items.slice(0, 24))); } catch(e) {} }\n"
+ "      async function fetchMore() {\n"
+ "        if(loading) return; loading = true;\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          const arts = Object.keys(ArtistStore.data).sort((a, b) => ArtistStore.data[b].c - ArtistStore.data[a].c).slice(0, 3);\n"
+ "          const pool = HOME_POOL.map(x => x.replace('Official Audio', 'Official Music Video'));\n"
+ "          const qs = [];\n"
+ "          if(arts.length) qs.push(arts[Math.floor(Math.random() * arts.length)] + ' Official Music Video');\n"
+ "          qs.push(pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)]); qs.push(pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)]);\n"
+ "          const res = await Promise.all(qs.map(q2 => search(q2, true, 12)));\n"
+ "          let fresh = []; res.forEach(r => (r || []).forEach(t => { if(t && t.id && !seenIds[t.id]) { seenIds[t.id] = 1; fresh.push(t); } }));\n"
+ "          for(let i = fresh.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1)); const t = fresh[i]; fresh[i] = fresh[j]; fresh[j] = t; }\n"
+ "          items = items.concat(fresh); saveCache(); render();\n"
+ "        } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "        loading = false;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function render() {\n"
+ "        const f = feed(); const ld = f.querySelector('.sh-load'); if(ld && items.length) ld.remove();\n"
+ "        for(let i = rendered; i < items.length; i++) {\n"
+ "          const t = items[i], d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'sh-item'; d.setAttribute('data-i', i);\n"
+ "          const th = 'https://i.ytimg.com/vi/' + t.id + '/hqdefault.jpg';\n"
+ "          d.innerHTML = '<div class=\"sh-bg\" style=\"background-image:url(' + th + ')\"></div><img class=\"sh-thumb\" src=\"' + th + '\"/><div class=\"sh-shade\"></div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"sh-ui\" onclick=\"Shorts.tap(' + i + ',event)\"><div class=\"sh-info\"><div class=\"sh-art\"><div class=\"sh-av\" style=\"background:' + color(t.a) + '\">' + escHtml((t.a || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()) + '</div>' + escHtml(t.a) + '</div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"sh-tt\">' + escHtml(t.t) + '</div><div class=\"sh-hint\" id=\"shHint' + i + '\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-play\"></i> Ketuk untuk dengar full</div></div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"sh-rail\"><div class=\"sh-btn\" id=\"shLike' + i + '\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); Shorts.like(' + i + ')\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-heart\"></i>Suka</div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"sh-btn\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); Shorts.save(' + i + ')\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-bookmark\"></i>Simpan</div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"sh-btn\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); Shorts.share(' + i + ')\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-share\"></i>Bagikan</div>' +\n"
+ "            '<div class=\"sh-btn\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); Shorts.full(' + i + ')\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-headphones\"></i>Full</div></div></div>';\n"
+ "          f.appendChild(d); if(obs) obs.observe(d);\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        rendered = items.length;\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function ensurePlayer() {\n"
+ "        if(yt || !(window.YT && YT.Player)) return;\n"
+ "        yt = new YT.Player('shYt', { width: '100%', height: '100%', playerVars: { autoplay: 1, controls: 0, playsinline: 1, mute: 1, disablekb: 1, fs: 0, rel: 0, modestbranding: 1, iv_load_policy: 3, origin: 'https://music.aether.app' },\n"
+ "          events: { onReady: () => { ytOk = true; if(cur >= 0) activate(cur); }, onStateChange: e => { if(e.data === 1 && cur >= 0) $$('shLive').classList.add('on'); }, onError: () => { $$('shLive').classList.remove('on'); } } });\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function activate(i) {\n"
+ "        cur = i; full = false; const t = items[i]; if(!t) return;\n"
+ "        $$('shLive').classList.remove('on');\n"
+ "        refreshBtns(); const h = $$('shHint' + i); if(h) { h.className = 'sh-hint'; h.innerHTML = '<i class=\"fa-solid fa-play\"></i> Ketuk untuk dengar full'; }\n"
+ "        if(i >= items.length - 3) fetchMore();\n"
+ "        if(!yt) { ensurePlayer(); return; }\n"
+ "        if(!ytOk) return;\n"
+ "        startSec = 20 + (hash(t.id) % 45);\n"
+ "        try { yt.mute(); yt.loadVideoById({ videoId: t.id, startSeconds: startSec }); } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function refreshBtns() { items.forEach((t, i) => { const b = $$('shLike' + i); if(b) b.classList.toggle('on', favs.some(f => f.id === t.id)); }); }\n"
+ "      function heartAt(i, ev) {\n"
+ "        const h = document.createElement('div'); h.className = 'sh-heart'; h.textContent = '\\u2764\\uFE0F';\n"
+ "        h.style.left = (ev && ev.clientX ? ev.clientX : window.innerWidth / 2) + 'px'; h.style.top = (ev && ev.clientY ? ev.clientY : window.innerHeight / 2) + 'px';\n"
+ "        $$('pgShorts').appendChild(h); setTimeout(() => h.remove(), 950);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function like(i, ev) {\n"
+ "        const t = items[i]; if(!t) return;\n"
+ "        if(!favs.some(f => f.id === t.id)) { modTrk = { id: t.id, t: t.t, a: t.a, l: t.l }; like_(); heartAt(i, ev); }\n"
+ "        else if(!ev) { modTrk = { id: t.id, t: t.t, a: t.a, l: t.l }; like_(); }\n"
+ "        else heartAt(i, ev);\n"
+ "        refreshBtns();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function like_() { window.like(); }\n"
+ "      function playFull(i) {\n"
+ "        const t = items[i]; if(!t) return;\n"
+ "        q = items.map(x => ({ id: x.id, t: x.t, a: x.a, l: x.l })); play(i); full = true;\n"
+ "        const h = $$('shHint' + i); if(h) { h.className = 'sh-hint full'; h.innerHTML = '<span class=\"sh-eq\"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span> Memutar penuh'; }\n"
+ "        toast('Memutar lagu penuh');\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      function tap(i, ev) {\n"
+ "        const now = Date.now();\n"
+ "        if(now - lastTap < 300) { clearTimeout(tapT); lastTap = 0; like(i, ev); return; }\n"
+ "        lastTap = now; const cx = ev; tapT = setTimeout(() => { if(full && cur === i) { try { tog(); } catch(e) {} } else playFull(i); }, 300);\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      setInterval(() => {\n"
+ "        if(!yt || !ytOk || cur < 0 || !$$('pgShorts').classList.contains('open')) return;\n"
+ "        try {\n"
+ "          const t = yt.getCurrentTime();\n"
+ "          if(full) { const m = getCurPos(); if(isP && Math.abs(t - m) > 2.5) yt.seekTo(m, true); $$('shProg').style.width = (getCurDur() > 0 ? m / getCurDur() * 100 : 0) + '%'; }\n"
+ "          else { if(t > startSec + 20) yt.seekTo(startSec, true); $$('shProg').style.width = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (t - startSec) / 20 * 100)) + '%'; }\n"
+ "        } catch(e) {}\n"
+ "      }, 500);\n"
+ "      function open() {\n"
+ "        Pages.open('pgShorts');\n"
+ "        const f = feed();\n"
+ "        if(!obs) { obs = new IntersectionObserver(es => { es.forEach(e => { if(e.isIntersecting && e.intersectionRatio > 0.65) activate(+e.target.getAttribute('data-i')); }); }, { root: f, threshold: [0.66] }); }\n"
+ "        if(!items.length) {\n"
+ "          try { items = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('aether_shorts_cache') || '[]') || []; } catch(e) { items = []; }\n"
+ "          items.forEach(t => { seenIds[t.id] = 1; });\n"
+ "          f.innerHTML = '<div class=\"sh-load\"><div><i class=\"fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin\"></i>&nbsp; Menyiapkan klip...</div></div>';\n"
+ "          rendered = 0; render();\n"
+ "          fetchMore();\n"
+ "        }\n"
+ "        ensurePlayer();\n"
+ "      }\n"
+ "      Pages.hooks.pgShorts = { close: () => { try { if(yt) yt.pauseVideo(); } catch(e) {} $$('shLive').classList.remove('on'); } };\n"
+ "      return {\n"
+ "        open: open, close: () => Pages.close('pgShorts'), tap: tap, like: i => like(i), full: playFull,\n"
+ "        save: i => { const t = items[i]; if(t) { modTrk = { id: t.id, t: t.t, a: t.a, l: t.l }; PL.pick(); } },\n"
+ "        share: i => { const t = items[i]; if(!t) return; const l = 'https://youtu.be/' + t.id + '?aether=1'; if(window.AndroidInterface && AndroidInterface.shareTrack) AndroidInterface.shareTrack(l, t.t + ' oleh ' + t.a); else { try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(l); toast('Link disalin'); } catch(e) {} } },\n"
+ "        reload: () => { items = []; rendered = 0; seenIds = {}; try { localStorage.removeItem('aether_shorts_cache'); } catch(e) {} const f = feed(); f.innerHTML = ''; open(); }\n"
+ "      };\n"
+ "    })();\n"
+ "\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    // BLUETOOTH, UNDUHAN, LINK, NAVIGASI\n"
+ "    // =====================================================================\n"
+ "    window.onBtEvent = function(name, conn, shown) {\n"
+ "      if(shown) return;\n"
